### [1] [Hoeffding-Style Concentration Bounds for Exchangeable Random Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10190)
*Nina Maria Gottschling,Michele Caprio*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文建立了可交换随机变量和的高尾和低尾的Hoeffding型集中不等式，揭示了由于可交换性假设导致的尾界反对称性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献对可交换随机变量和的尾界估计通常基于总体均值，但本文旨在建立基于de Finetti混合测度支撑中分布最大/最小均值的尾界，以弥合有限样本均值、总体均值和分布均值之间的差距

Method: 建立可交换随机变量和的Hoeffding型集中不等式，特别关注尾界的反对称性，提出基于de Finetti混合测度支撑中分布最大均值（上尾）和最小均值（下尾）的尾界估计方法

Result: 获得了可交换随机变量和的上尾界相对于de Finetti混合测度支撑中分布的最大均值，下尾界相对于最小均值的集中不等式，揭示了尾界的反对称特性

Conclusion: 本文建立了可交换随机变量和的新型尾界估计，基于de Finetti混合测度支撑中的极值均值而非总体均值，这为理解可交换随机变量的有限样本行为与分布特性之间的关系提供了新的理论工具

Abstract: We establish Hoeffding-type concentration inequalities for the low and high tail bounds of sums of exchangeable random variables. Our results exhibit an anti-symmetry in such tail bounds due to the assumption of exchangeability, a generalization of the i.i.d. setting. In contrast to the existing literature on this problem, our result provides an upper tail bound with respect to the largest mean of a distribution in the support of the de Finetti mixing measure, and not the population mean. Equivalently, we establish a lower tail bound with respect to the smallest mean of a distribution in the support of the de Finetti mixing measure. This bridges the gap between finite sample and population means of exchangeable random variables, and distributional means.

</details>


### [2] [A Trust-Region Interior-Point Stochastic Sequential Quadratic Programming Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10230)
*Yuchen Fang,Jihun Kim,Sen Na,James Demmel,Javad Lavaei*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出TR-IP-SSQP方法，用于求解具有随机目标函数和确定性非线性等式/不等式约束的优化问题，使用随机估计替代精确梯度，结合内点法处理不等式约束，证明全局几乎必然收敛到一阶驻点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有随机目标函数和确定性约束的优化问题，其中目标函数及其梯度无法精确计算，只能获得随机估计。需要开发能够处理这种混合随机-确定性设置且保证收敛性的算法。

Method: 提出信任域内点随机序列二次规划方法（TR-IP-SSQP）。在每个迭代中构建满足自适应精度条件的随机估计器（以固定概率）。采用内点法处理不等式约束，屏障参数遵循预定衰减序列。结合信任域框架确保全局收敛性。

Result: 在标准假设下，证明了该方法全局几乎必然收敛到一阶驻点。在CUTEst测试集子集和逻辑回归问题上进行了实现，展示了实际性能。

Conclusion: TR-IP-SSQP方法能够有效处理随机目标函数与确定性约束的混合优化问题，在理论上保证收敛性，并在实际测试中表现出良好性能，为这类问题提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a trust-region interior-point stochastic sequential quadratic programming (TR-IP-SSQP) method for solving optimization problems with a stochastic objective and deterministic nonlinear equality and inequality constraints. In this setting, exact evaluations of the objective function and its gradient are unavailable, but their stochastic estimates can be constructed. In particular, at each iteration our method builds stochastic oracles, which estimate the objective value and gradient to satisfy proper adaptive accuracy conditions with a fixed probability. To handle inequality constraints, we adopt an interior-point method (IPM), in which the barrier parameter follows a prescribed decaying sequence. Under standard assumptions, we establish global almost-sure convergence of the proposed method to first-order stationary points. We implement the method on a subset of problems from the CUTEst test set, as well as on logistic regression problems, to demonstrate its practical performance.

</details>


### [3] [Avoiding Semi-Infinite Programming in Distributionally Robust Control Based on Mean-Variance Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10280)
*Yuma Shida,Yuji Ito*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种新的分布鲁棒控制方法，通过引入基于特定分布距离的惩罚项，将分布鲁棒优化问题转化为均值-方差最小化问题，避免了传统方法中的半无限规划问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机控制方法需要明确指定系统随机行为的概率分布，而基于Wasserstein距离的分布鲁棒控制方法通常需要处理半无限规划问题，计算复杂。本文旨在开发一种无需半无限规划即可解决离散时间、折扣分布鲁棒最优控制问题的方法。

Method: 通过引入基于特定分布距离的惩罚项，建立上界，并在适当条件下证明分布鲁棒优化问题与均值-方差最小化问题的等价性。将原始分布鲁棒控制问题转化为折扣均值-方差成本优化问题。在线性二次调节器设置中，通过求解Riccati方程获得相应的控制律。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提方法的折扣累积成本理论最大值低于传统方法。在线性二次调节器场景中，控制律可通过求解Riccati方程获得，计算效率更高。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无需半无限规划的分布鲁棒控制新方法，通过将问题转化为均值-方差最小化问题，简化了计算复杂度，并在理论上和数值实验中证明了其优于传统方法的性能。

Abstract: Conventional stochastic control methods have several limitations. They focus on optimizing the average performance and, in some cases, performance variability; however, their problem settings still require an explicit specification of the probability distributions that determine the system's stochastic behavior. Distributionally robust control (DRC) methods have recently been developed to address these challenges. However, many DRC approaches involve handling infinitely many inequalities. For instance, DRC problems based on the Wasserstein distance are commonly obtained by solving semi-infinite programming (SIP) problems. Our proposed method eliminates the need for SIP when solving discrete-time, discounted, distributionally robust optimal control problems. By introducing a penalty term based on a specific distributional distance, we establish upper bounds, and under appropriate conditions, demonstrate the equivalence between distributionally robust optimization problems and mean-variance minimization problems. This reformulation reduces the original DRC problem to a discounted mean-variance cost optimization problem. In linear-quadratic regulator settings, the corresponding control laws are obtained by solving the Riccati equation. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the theoretical maximum value of the discounted cumulative cost for the proposed method is lower than that for the conventional method.

</details>


### [4] [Equilibrium under Time-Inconsistency: A New Existence Theory by Vanishing Entropy Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10321)
*Zhenhua Wang,Xiang Yu,Jingjie Zhang,Zhou Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文通过熵正则化方法解决了时间不一致随机控制问题中均衡HJB方程解的存在性问题，建立了正则化均衡的存在性及其收敛性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间不一致随机控制问题中，均衡HJB方程（EHJB）的经典解存在性在一般模型假设下仍是一个开放问题，这阻碍了均衡策略的严格理论分析。

Method: 采用熵正则化松弛策略，通过不动点论证建立探索性均衡HJB方程（EEHJB）的经典解存在性，获得正则化均衡的吉布斯形式表征，并发展精细的PDE估计进行收敛性分析。

Result: 证明了EEHJB解收敛到广义EHJB的弱解，正则化均衡收敛到原始问题的均衡策略，为时间不一致扩散模型中的均衡存在性提供了新的充分条件。

Conclusion: 熵正则化方法成功解决了时间不一致随机控制中均衡HJB方程解的存在性问题，避免了强正则性假设，为这类问题提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: This paper studies time-inconsistent stochastic control problems in a continuous-time setting. The time-inconsistency arises from the initial-time dependence such as the non-exponential discounting. The classical approach typically relates the existence of equilibrium to the classical solution of the equilibrium HJB equation (EHJB), whose existence is still an open problem under general model assumptions. We resolve this challenge by invoking the entropy regularization on relaxed policies. First, by employing some fixed point arguments, we establish the existence of classical solution of the exploratory equilibrium HJB equation (EEHJB) and obtain the Gibbs form characterization of the regularized equilibrium. Next, we develop some delicate PDE estimates for the solution of the EEHJB and its derivatives, facilitating the novel convergence analysis under suitable norms as the entropy regularization vanishes. It is shown that the solutions of EEHJB converges to a weak solution of a generalized EHJB, which is associated with the limit of the regularized equilibrium. This core convergence result allows us to conduct the verification to conclude that the limiting regularized equilibrium constitutes the equilibrium in the original problem. We thus contribute a new sufficient condition to the literature for the equilibrium in diffusion models under time-inconsistency without resorting to strong regularity assumptions of the EHJB.

</details>


### [5] [Linear complementarity properties of some classes of banded matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10322)
*Samapti Pratihar,M. Seetharama Gowda,K. C. Sivakumar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了带状矩阵及其变体的线性互补性质，特别关注三角矩阵和双对角西南矩阵，描述了Q性质的符号模式和行列式特征，并将结果扩展到欧几里得约当代数中的矩阵线性变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带状矩阵及其变体的线性互补性质，旨在建立这类特殊结构矩阵的Q性质特征，为线性互补问题的理论分析提供新的工具和结果。

Method: 通过分析三角矩阵和新定义的双对角西南矩阵，研究其符号模式和行列式特征与Q性质的关系；将结果扩展到欧几里得约当代数中的矩阵线性变换，特别研究秩一线性变换a⊗b的Q性质。

Result: 描述了三角矩阵和双对角西南矩阵Q性质的符号模式和行列式特征；给出了所有2×2 Q矩阵的完整描述；证明了秩一线性变换a⊗b具有Q性质当且仅当a>0,b>0或a<0,b<0。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了带状矩阵及其变体的线性互补性质特征，为特殊结构矩阵的Q性质分析提供了系统框架，并将结果成功扩展到欧几里得约当代数中的矩阵线性变换。

Abstract: A banded matrix is a real square matrix where nonzero entries appear around the main diagonal. In this article, we consider linear complementarity properties of (variants) of banded matrices. Focusing on triangular matrices and the newly defined bidiagonal southwest matrices, we describe several results characterizing the Q-property in terms of the sign patterns and determinant of the given matrix. As a byproduct, we describe all Q-matrices of size 2 by 2. Extending these results to Euclidean Jordan algebras, we consider matrix-based linear transformations and study the Q-property. In particular, we show that a rank-one linear transformation of the form a\otimes b has the Q-property if and only if either a>0,b>0, or a<0, b<0.

</details>


### [6] [A Globally Convergent Flow for Time-Dependent Mean Field Games and a Solver-Agnostic Framework for Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10336)
*Hanwei Yan,Xianjin Yang,Jingguo Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了解决平均场博弈正问题和反问题的统一框架：正问题采用单调Hessian-Riemannian流保证全局收敛；反问题采用伴随方法和高斯-牛顿加速实现求解器无关的参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均场博弈的正问题和反问题都存在挑战。正问题需要具有全局收敛保证且不依赖初始化的数值方法；反问题需要将参数优化与正向求解器解耦，使参数更新不依赖实现细节，且反演方法在更换正向求解器时无需重新设计。

Method: 1. 正问题：提出密度可行流形上的单调Hessian-Riemannian流，保持密度正性并证明全局收敛性。2. 反问题：将参数估计建模为外层优化问题，内层求解MFG系统；采用伴随方法梯度下降和高斯-牛顿加速，通过隐式微分收敛MFG解而非特定求解器。

Result: 在多个稳态和时变MFG示例中验证了方法有效性，高斯-牛顿方法相比梯度下降始终需要更少的外层迭代次数，证明了框架的实用性和效率。

Conclusion: 该研究为平均场博弈的正问题和反问题提供了统一的数值框架：正问题通过单调Hessian-Riemannian流获得全局收敛保证；反问题通过求解器无关的伴随方法实现参数估计，为MFG的实际应用提供了可靠的计算工具。

Abstract: Mean field games (MFGs) model the limit of large populations of strategically interacting agents, yet both forward and inverse problems remain challenging. For the forward problem, a difficulty is to design numerical methods with global convergence guarantees whose convergence does not depend on careful initialization. For the inverse problem, a difficulty is to decouple parameter optimization from the forward solver, so that parameter updates do not depend on implementation details, and the inverse method does not need to be reformulated when the forward solver is changed. We address both issues as follows. For the forward problem, we propose a monotone Hessian-Riemannian flow for time-dependent MFGs on the feasible manifold of densities. The flow preserves the positivity of densities and is proved to be globally convergent. For the inverse problem, we cast parameter estimation as an outer optimization problem over the unknown coefficients, with the MFG system solved in an inner step for each parameter value. For solving this problem, we consider an adjoint-based gradient method together with a Gauss-Newton acceleration. This leads to a solver-agnostic framework, in which parameter updates are computed by implicitly differentiating the discrete MFG equations satisfied by the converged MFG solution, rather than by differentiating through a particular forward solver. We demonstrate the approach on several stationary and time-dependent MFG examples, where the Gauss-Newton method consistently requires fewer outer iterations than gradient descent.

</details>


### [7] [Minimal polynomials, scaled Jordan frames, and Schur-type majorization in hyperbolic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10522)
*M. Seetharama Gowda,Juyoung Jeong,Sudheer Shukla*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了具有缩放Jordan框架的双曲系统，证明了当系统存在缩放Jordan框架且n≥2时，双曲多项式p及其导数多项式p'是最小多项式，并建立了Jordan框架与欧几里得Jordan代数之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双曲系统(V,p,e)中缩放Jordan框架的性质，扩展Ito和Lourenço在秩一生成双曲锥上的结果，探索Jordan框架与欧几里得Jordan代数结构之间的关系。

Method: 使用双曲多项式理论，定义缩放Jordan框架和Jordan框架，分析特征值映射λ和双曲锥Λ_+的性质，通过代数方法证明多项式的最小性，并建立框架的正交性和Schur型控制结果。

Result: 1) 当系统存在缩放Jordan框架且n≥2时，p和p'是最小多项式；2) Jordan框架在λ诱导的半内积下是正交的，恰好有n个元素；3) V包含R^n的副本（作为欧几里得Jordan代数）；4) 建立了关于Jordan框架和e-双随机n元组的Schur型控制结果。

Conclusion: 该研究成功扩展了秩一生成双曲锥上的结果到更一般的双曲系统，建立了缩放Jordan框架与多项式最小性之间的联系，揭示了Jordan框架的代数结构特性，为双曲多项式理论提供了新的见解。

Abstract: Corresponding to a hyperbolic system $(V, p, e)$, where $V$ is a real finite-dimensional vector space and $p$ is a hyperbolic polynomial of degree $n$ in the direction $e$, we consider the eigenvalue map $λ: V \to R^n$ and the hyperbolicity cone $Λ_+$. In such a system, a scaled Jordan frame is defined as a finite set of rank-one elements whose sum lies in the interior of $Λ_+$. We show that when the system has a scaled Jordan frame and $n \geq 2$, $p$ and its derivative polynomial $p^\prime$ are minimal polynomials (generating their respective hyperbolicity cones), thereby extending a result of Ito and Louren{\c c}o proved in the setting of a rank-one generated (proper) hyperbolicity cone. When each element of a scaled Jordan frame has trace one and the total sum is $e$ (such a set is called a Jordan frame), we show that the frame is orthonormal relative to the semi-inner product induced by $λ$ with exactly $n$ elements, and $V$ contains a copy of $R^n$ (as a Euclidean Jordan algebra). We also present a Schur-type majorization result corresponding to a Jordan frame and an $e$-doubly stochastic $n$-tuple.

</details>


### [8] [Bridging local and semilocal stability: A topological approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10542)
*J. Camacho*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文建立了在一般拓扑条件下，集值映射的半局部稳定性可由其局部稳定性精确确定的理论框架，揭示了Lipschitz上半连续模与局部镇定模之间的等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究集值映射半局部稳定性与局部稳定性之间的关系。对于具有凸图的映射，Lipschitz上半连续模与局部镇定模一致，但在非凸情况下这种关系通常失效，导致半局部模计算极其困难。需要建立一般拓扑条件来确保这两种模的等价性。

Method: 采用拓扑分析方法，证明如果映射在Painlevé-Kuratowski意义下是外半连续的，并且在标称参数附近局部紧致，则Lipschitz上半连续模恰好等于标称集上局部镇定模的上确界。通过点基公式实现半局部误差界的精确计算。

Result: 建立了在Painlevé-Kuratowski外半连续性和局部紧致性条件下，Lipschitz上半连续模等于局部镇定模上确界的理论结果。这一等式使得能够通过点基公式精确计算半局部误差界。

Conclusion: 该定理为多个非凸框架中的参数优化问题提供了广泛适用性，包括分段凸和半代数映射、完全数据扰动下的可行集和最优集映射、广义方程和线性互补问题、半无限不等式系统以及参数化子水平集等。

Abstract: This paper establishes a general topological condition under which the semilocal stability of a set-valued mapping can be exactly determined by its local stability properties. Specifically, we investigate the relationship between the Lipschitz upper semicontinuity modulus -- a semilocal measure of variation for the image set -- and the local calmness moduli. While these two quantities coincide for mappings with convex graphs, the relationship generally breaks down in the absence of convexity, making the semilocal modulus exceptionally difficult to compute. We prove that if a mapping is outer semicontinuous in the Painlevé-Kuratowski sense and locally compact around the nominal parameter, the Lipschitz upper semicontinuity modulus is exactly the supremum of the local calmness moduli over the nominal set. In addition to the theoretical advance, this equality enables the precise calculation of semilocal error bounds via point-based formulae. We illustrate the broad applicability of this theorem by setting it up in several non-convex frameworks in parametric optimization, including piecewise convex and semi-algebraic mappings, feasible and optimal set mappings under full data perturbations, generalized equations and linear complementarity problems, semi-infinite inequality systems, and parameterized sub-level sets.

</details>


### [9] [Practical Regularized Quasi-Newton Methods with Inexact Function Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10642)
*Hiroki Hamaguchi,Naoki Marumo,Akiko Takeda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种噪声容忍的正则化拟牛顿方法，结合松弛Armijo线搜索，用于处理目标函数值存在数值误差的平滑非凸优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际优化问题中目标函数值常受数值误差影响。传统拟牛顿方法假设精确函数评估，在噪声环境下可能失效，需要开发快速且鲁棒的拟牛顿方法。

Method: 提出噪声容忍正则化拟牛顿方法，结合松弛Armijo型线搜索。采用受Objective-Function-Free Optimization和AdaGrad-Norm启发的正则化参数更新规则，确保在函数评估不准确时保持稳定。

Result: 在假设误差模型下，证明达到一阶稳定点的全局收敛率为$\mathcal{O}(1/\varepsilon^2)$。在CUTEst基准测试中，使用人工噪声和低精度浮点运算（64、32、16位）验证了方法的鲁棒性和收敛速度。

Conclusion: 该方法比现有方法显著更鲁棒，同时保持竞争力的实际收敛速度和计算成本，适用于存在数值误差的平滑非凸优化问题。

Abstract: Many practical optimization problems involve objective function values that are corrupted by unavoidable numerical errors. In smooth nonconvex optimization, quasi-Newton methods combined with line search are widely used due to their efficiency and scalability. These methods implicitly assume accurate function evaluations and thus may fail to converge in noisy settings. Developing fast and robust quasi-Newton methods for such scenarios is therefore crucial.
  To address this issue, we propose a noise-tolerant regularized quasi-Newton method equipped with a relaxed Armijo-type line search, designed to remain stable under inaccurate function evaluations. By combining a regularization parameter update rule inspired by Objective-Function-Free Optimization and the AdaGrad-Norm method, we establish a global convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(1/\varepsilon^2)$ for reaching a first-order stationary point under the assumed error model. We performed extensive experiments on the CUTEst benchmark collection with artificially noisy objective function evaluations, as well as with low-precision floating-point arithmetic (64-, 32-, and 16-bit). The results demonstrate that the proposed method is substantially more robust than several existing methods, while maintaining competitive practical convergence speed and computational cost.

</details>


### [10] [Distributed Stability Certification and Control from Local Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10812)
*Surya Malladi,Nima Monshizadeh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 分布式数据驱动系统分析与控制：在测量数据分散于多个智能体且不共享原始数据的情况下，开发分布式算法计算全局系统证书（Lyapunov证书和代数Riccati方程稳定解）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据驱动分析和控制方法依赖集中式访问系统测量数据，但在实际分布式系统中，测量数据分散于多个智能体且原始数据不共享，每个智能体只能访问本地样本（可能仅单个测量），智能体间仅交换本地计算信号，导致没有单个智能体具备足够信息独立识别整个系统或合成控制器。

Method: 开发分布式动态算法：1) 针对稳定LTI系统，通过完全分布式方式求解Lyapunov方程计算Lyapunov证书；2) 针对一般LTI系统，计算代数Riccati方程的稳定解从而获得最优LQR控制器。提出两种方案：初始方案保证实际收敛，后续增强的PI型算法实现精确收敛到期望解。

Result: 证明了所得LQR控制器对不确定性和测量噪声的鲁棒性。通过四水箱过程的分布式Lyapunov认证和直升机动力学的分布式LQR设计进行了方法验证。

Conclusion: 提出了一种在测量数据分布式持有且不共享原始数据场景下的分布式系统分析与控制框架，成功实现了从局部数据集体计算全局系统证书，为解决分布式数据驱动控制问题提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Most data-driven analysis and control methods rely on centralized access to system measurements. In contrast, we consider a setting in which the measurements are distributed across multiple agents and raw data are not shared. Each agent has access only to locally held samples, possibly as little as a single measurement, and agents exchange only locally computed signals. Consequently, no individual agent possesses sufficient information to identify the entire system or synthesize a controller independently. To address this limitation, we develop distributed dynamical algorithms that enable the agents to collectively compute global system certificates from local data. Two problems are addressed. First, for stable linear time-invariant (LTI) systems, the agents compute a Lyapunov certificate by solving the Lyapunov equation in a fully distributed manner. Second, for general LTI systems, they compute the stabilizing solution of the algebraic Riccati equation and hence the optimal linear-quadratic regulator (LQR). An initially proposed scheme guarantees practical convergence, while a subsequent augmented PI-type algorithm achieves exact convergence to the desired solution. We further establish robustness of the resulting LQR controller to uncertainty and measurement noise. The approach is illustrated through distributed Lyapunov certification of a quadruple-tank process and distributed LQR design for helicopter dynamics.

</details>


### [11] [On Utility Maximization under Multivariate Fake Stationary Affine Volterra Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11046)
*Emmanuel Gnabeyeu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 在Volterra随机环境下解决Merton投资组合优化问题，使用BSDE方法处理非马尔可夫和非半鞅过程，得到半封闭形式的最优策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多元伪平稳Volterra-Heston模型的Volterra随机环境中，由于基础过程的非马尔可夫性和非半鞅性，经典随机控制方法无法直接应用，需要开发新的数学工具

Method: 使用Riccati向后随机微分方程(BSDE)的随机因子解，结合鞅最优性原理和适当的验证论证来处理非马尔可夫和非半鞅过程

Result: 得到了Merton问题的最优策略的半封闭形式解，这些解依赖于时变多元Riccati-Volterra方程的解，并通过二维伪平稳粗糙Heston模型的数值结果展示了平稳粗糙波动率对最优策略的影响

Conclusion: 成功解决了Volterra随机环境下的Merton投资组合优化问题，为处理非马尔可夫和非半鞅过程提供了有效的BSDE方法，并揭示了粗糙波动率对最优投资策略的重要影响

Abstract: This paper is concerned with Merton's portfolio optimization problem in a Volterra stochastic environment described by a multivariate fake stationary Volterra--Heston model. Due to the non-Markovianity and non-semimartingality of the underlying processes, the classical stochastic control approach cannot be directly applied in this setting. Instead, the problem is tackled using a stochastic factor solution to a Riccati backward stochastic differential equation (BSDE). Our approach is inspired by the martingale optimality principle combined with a suitable verification argument. The resulting optimal strategies for Merton's problems are derived in semi-closed form depending on the solutions to time-dependent multivariate Riccati-Volterra equations. Numerical results on a two dimensional fake stationary rough Heston model illustrate the impact of stationary rough volatilities on the optimal Merton strategies.

</details>

### [12] [OmniGuide: Universal Guidance Fields for Enhancing Generalist Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10052)
*Yunzhou Song,Long Le,Yong-Hyun Park,Jie Wang,Junyao Shi,Lingjie Liu,Jiatao Gu,Eric Eaton,Dinesh Jayaraman,Kostas Daniilidis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OMNIGUIDE是一个提升视觉-语言-动作模型在复杂任务上性能的框架，通过将多种引导源（如3D基础模型、语义推理VLM、人体姿态模型）表达为可微能量函数来指导VLA动作采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型在简单任务上表现良好，但在需要复杂空间/语义理解、杂乱环境操作或精确操控的复杂任务上性能有限。需要一种灵活框架来整合多种互补的引导源以提升VLA在挑战性任务上的表现。

Method: 提出OMNIGUIDE框架，将各种引导源自然表达为可微能量函数，包含任务特定的吸引子和排斥子，这些能量函数位于3D空间中并影响VLA动作的采样过程。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的大量实验表明，OMNIGUIDE显著提升了最先进通用策略（如π₀.₅、GR00T N1.6）的成功率和安全性。该统一框架匹配或超越了专门为特定引导源设计的先前方法。

Conclusion: OMNIGUIDE通过整合多种互补的引导源，有效提升了VLA模型在复杂任务上的性能，为构建更强大的通用机器人策略提供了灵活统一的框架。

Abstract: Vision-language-action(VLA) models have shown great promise as generalist policies for a large range of relatively simple tasks. However, they demonstrate limited performance on more complex tasks, such as those requiring complex spatial or semantic understanding, manipulation in clutter, or precise manipulation. We propose OMNIGUIDE, a flexible framework that improves VLA performance on such tasks by leveraging arbitrary sources of guidance, such as 3D foundation models, semantic-reasoning VLMs, and human pose models. We show how many kinds of guidance can be naturally expressed as differentiable energy functions with task-specific attractors and repellers located in 3D space, that influence the sampling of VLA actions. In this way, OMNIGUIDE enables guidance sources with complementary task-relevant strengths to improve a VLA model's performance on challenging tasks. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments, across diverse sources of guidance, demonstrate that OMNIGUIDE enhances the performance of state-of-the-art generalist policies (e.g., $π_{0.5}$, GR00T N1.6) significantly across success and safety rates. Critically, our unified framework matches or surpasses the performance of prior methods designed to incorporate specific sources of guidance into VLA policies. Project Page: $\href{https://omniguide.github.io/}{this \; url}$

</details>


### [13] [Decision-Aware Uncertainty Evaluation of Vision-Language Model-Based Early Action Anticipation for Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10061)
*Zhaoda Du,Michael Bowman,Qiaojie Zheng,Xiaoli Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首次系统评估基于视觉语言模型的短期动作识别不确定性，为置信度门控的人机交互模块提供可靠性证据


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享工作空间中的机器人需要从部分、模糊的观察中解释人类动作，早期过度自信的预测可能导致不安全或破坏性的交互。特别是在以自我为中心的视角下，视角变化和遮挡增加了感知噪声和模糊性，下游人机交互模块不仅需要动作假设，还需要在部分观察下的可信置信度估计。

Method: 提出时间前缀评估协议和校准与选择性预测指标，系统评估基于视觉语言模型的短期动作识别不确定性，并表征部分观察下的校准错误模式和失败模式。

Result: 研究提供了使用视觉语言模型预测于置信度门控人机交互模块所需的缺失可靠性证据，揭示了在部分观察下的校准模式和失败特征。

Conclusion: 这是首个针对基于视觉语言模型的短期动作识别不确定性进行系统评估的研究，为人机交互中可靠使用视觉语言模型预测提供了关键基础。

Abstract: Robots in shared workspaces must interpret human actions from partial, ambiguous observations, where overconfident early predictions can lead to unsafe or disruptive interaction. This challenge is amplified in egocentric views, where viewpoint changes and occlusions increase perceptual noise and ambiguity. As a result, downstream human-robot interaction modules require not only an action hypothesis but also a trustworthy estimate of confidence under partial observation. Recent vision-language model-based approaches have been proposed for short-term action recognition due to their open-vocabulary and context-aware reasoning, but their uncertainty reliability in the temporal-prefix regime is largely uncharacterized. We present the first systematic evaluation of uncertainty in vision-language model-based short-term action recognition for human-robot interaction. We introduce a temporal-prefix evaluation protocol and metrics for calibration and selective prediction. We also characterize miscalibration patterns and failure modes under partial observations. Our study provides the missing reliability evidence needed to use vision-language model predictions in confidence-gated human-robot interaction modules.

</details>


### [14] [AR-VLA: True Autoregressive Action Expert for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10126)
*Yutong Hu,Jan-Nico Zaech,Nikolay Nikolov,Yuanqi Yao,Sombit Dey,Giuliano Albanese,Renaud Detry,Luc Van Gool,Danda Paudel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AR-VLA：一种自回归动作专家，将动作生成为连续因果序列，通过长时记忆保持上下文感知，解决快速控制与慢速推理的频率不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型和扩散策略在每个新观测时重置时间上下文，反应式预测动作，缺乏历史感知能力。需要解决快速控制与慢速感知推理之间的频率不匹配问题，实现时空一致的动作生成。

Method: 1. 设计自回归动作专家，将动作生成为连续因果序列；2. 引入长时记忆保持历史上下文；3. 使用重新锚定机制同步异步的视觉-语言-动作模态；4. 支持运动学语法的独立预训练和与重型感知骨干的模块化集成。

Result: 在模拟和真实机器人操作任务中，AR-VLA能有效替代传统的基于块的动作头，在保持或超越最先进反应式VLA任务成功率的同时，展现出优越的历史感知能力和显著更平滑的动作轨迹。

Conclusion: 提出了一种可扩展的、上下文感知的动作生成框架，为训练有效的机器人策略提供了稳健的结构基础，解决了VLA模型中时间一致性和频率不匹配的关键问题。

Abstract: We propose a standalone autoregressive (AR) Action Expert that generates actions as a continuous causal sequence while conditioning on refreshable vision-language prefixes. In contrast to existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models and diffusion policies that reset temporal context with each new observation and predict actions reactively, our Action Expert maintains its own history through a long-lived memory and is inherently context-aware. This structure addresses the frequency mismatch between fast control and slow reasoning, enabling efficient independent pretraining of kinematic syntax and modular integration with heavy perception backbones, naturally ensuring spatio-temporally consistent action generation across frames. To synchronize these asynchronous hybrid V-L-A modalities, we utilize a re-anchoring mechanism that mathematically accounts for perception staleness during both training and inference. Experiments on simulated and real-robot manipulation tasks demonstrate that the proposed method can effectively replace traditional chunk-based action heads for both specialist and generalist policies. AR-VLA exhibits superior history awareness and substantially smoother action trajectories while maintaining or exceeding the task success rates of state-of-the-art reactive VLAs. Overall, our work introduces a scalable, context-aware action generation schema that provides a robust structural foundation for training effective robotic policies.

</details>


### [15] [Cross-Hand Latent Representation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10158)
*Guangqi Jiang,Yutong Liang,Jianglong Ye,Jia-Yang Huang,Changwei Jing,Rocky Duan,Pieter Abbeel,Xiaolong Wang,Xueyan Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: XL-VLA：一种用于灵巧操作的跨具身视觉-语言-动作框架，通过统一的潜在动作空间实现不同灵巧手之间的数据共享和高效训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作对机器人自主性至关重要，人类依赖多模态感知（视觉、声音、语言引导意图）执行灵巧动作。然而，训练可靠的视觉-语言-动作模型需要大量不同灵巧手的演示数据，而新灵巧具身的快速出现使得为每个具身收集数据成本高昂且不切实际，因此需要可扩展的跨具身学习方法。

Method: 提出XL-VLA框架，集成一个跨不同灵巧手共享的统一潜在动作空间。这个具身不变的潜在空间可直接插入标准VLA架构，实现无缝的跨具身训练，并高效复用现有和新收集的数据。

Result: 实验结果表明，XL-VLA在性能上持续优于在原始关节空间中操作的基线VLA模型，证明了其作为可扩展跨具身灵巧操作解决方案的有效性。

Conclusion: XL-VLA通过统一的潜在动作空间解决了跨具身灵巧操作的数据可扩展性问题，为不同灵巧手之间的知识迁移和数据复用提供了有效框架，推动了视觉-语言-动作模型在实际机器人应用中的发展。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation is essential for real-world robot autonomy, mirroring the central role of human hand coordination in daily activity. Humans rely on rich multimodal perception--vision, sound, and language-guided intent--to perform dexterous actions, motivating vision-based, language-conditioned manipulation systems for robots. However, training reliable vision-language-action (VLA) models for dexterous manipulation requires large-scale demonstrations across many robotic hands. In addition, as new dexterous embodiments appear rapidly, collecting data for each becomes costly and impractical, creating a need for scalable cross-embodiment learning. We introduce XL-VLA, a vision-language-action framework integrated with a unified latent action space shared across diverse dexterous hands. This embodiment-invariant latent space is directly pluggable into standard VLA architectures, enabling seamless cross-embodiment training and efficient reuse of both existing and newly collected data. Experimental results demonstrate that XL-VLA consistently outperforms baseline VLA models operating in raw joint spaces, establishing it as an effective solution for scalable cross-embodiment dexterous manipulation.

</details>


### [16] [Dance2Hesitate: A Multi-Modal Dataset of Dancer-Taught Hesitancy for Understandable Robot Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10166)
*Srikrishna Bangalore Raghu,Anna Soukhovei,Divya Sai Sindhuja Vankineni,Alexandra Bacula,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并开源了一个多模态、舞者生成的犹豫运动数据集，用于研究人机协作中机器人犹豫表达对人类协调策略的影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机协作中，机器人表达犹豫是影响人类协调策略、注意力分配和安全判断的关键因素，但设计具有泛化能力的犹豫机器人运动具有挑战性，因为观察者的推断高度依赖于具体形态和上下文

Method: 创建了一个多模态数据集，包括：(1) 在Franka Emika Panda机械臂上通过动觉示教演示从固定起始配置到目标(Jenga塔)的三种犹豫等级(轻微、显著、极端)运动；(2) 同步RGB-D运动捕捉舞者使用上肢执行相同到达行为的三种犹豫等级，以及极端犹豫下的全身序列

Result: 获得了70个独特的全身轨迹，84个跨越三种犹豫等级的上肢轨迹，以及66个跨越三种犹豫等级的动觉示教轨迹，提供了可重复基准测试的文档

Conclusion: 该数据集为研究犹豫运动在特定上下文-形态对中的表达提供了宝贵资源，有助于开发更自然、安全的人机协作系统

Abstract: In human-robot collaboration, a robot's expression of hesitancy is a critical factor that shapes human coordination strategies, attention allocation, and safety-related judgments. However, designing hesitant robot motion that generalizes is challenging because the observer's inference is highly dependent on embodiment and context. To address these challenges, we introduce and open-source a multi-modal, dancer-generated dataset of hesitant motion where we focus on specific context-embodiment pairs (i.e., manipulator/human upper-limb approaching a Jenga Tower, and anthropomorphic whole body motion in free space). The dataset includes (i) kinesthetic teaching demonstrations on a Franka Emika Panda reaching from a fixed start configuration to a fixed target (a Jenga tower) with three graded hesitancy levels (slight, significant, extreme) and (ii) synchronized RGB-D motion capture of dancers performing the same reaching behavior using their upper limb across three hesitancy levels, plus full human body sequences for extreme hesitancy. We further provide documentation to enable reproducible benchmarking across robot and human modalities. Across all dancers, we obtained 70 unique whole-body trajectories, 84 upper limb trajectories spanning over the three hesitancy levels, and 66 kinesthetic teaching trajectories spanning over the three hesitancy levels. The dataset can be accessed here: https://brsrikrishna.github.io/Dance2Hesitate/.

</details>


### [17] [Characterizing Healthy & Post-Stroke Neuromotor Behavior During 6D Upper-Limb Isometric Gaming: Implications for Design of End-Effector Rehabilitation Robot Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10173)
*Ajay Anand,Gabriel Parra,Chad A. Berghoff,Laura A. Hallock*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究利用OpenRobotRehab 1.0数据集，分析健康和中风后用户在等距轨迹跟踪任务中的行为特征，评估界面设计对行为的影响，开发基于HMM的神经运动行为分类方法，并探讨自适应康复机器人设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助康复需要设计能促进健康运动实践的游戏和机器人干预，但由于用户神经运动行为、游戏界面和物理机器人之间的复杂相互作用，使得系统设计（甚至定义"健康"或病理行为）具有挑战性。

Method: 利用OpenRobotRehab 1.0开放数据集，分析13名健康用户和2名中风后用户在末端执行器康复机器人上执行等距轨迹跟踪任务时的力输出、肌肉激活和游戏表现。开发基于表面肌电信号的隐马尔可夫模型神经运动行为分类方法，分析界面设计对行为的影响，以及病理行为在末端执行器力动力学中的表现。

Result: 任务规范（包括约束轴和用户对跟踪指令的解释）影响用户行为；病理相关特征可在等距任务执行期间的6D末端执行器力数据中检测到（健康和中风后用户在力误差和平均力产生方面存在显著差异，p=0.05）；健康神经运动策略具有异质性且难以表征；HMM模型能区分健康和中风后神经运动动力学，而基于协同作用的分解方法无法实现这种区分。

Conclusion: 研究结果为设计能够促进不同用户群体采用更健康运动策略的自适应末端执行器康复机器人提供了重要见解，强调了界面设计、病理特征检测和个性化神经运动行为分类在康复机器人开发中的重要性。

Abstract: Successful robot-mediated rehabilitation requires designing games and robot interventions that promote healthy motor practice. However, the interplay between a given user's neuromotor behavior, the gaming interface, and the physical robot makes designing system elements -- and even characterizing what behaviors are "healthy" or pathological -- challenging. We leverage our OpenRobotRehab 1.0 open access data set to assess the characteristics of 13 healthy and 2 post-stroke users' force output, muscle activations, and game performance while executing isometric trajectory tracking tasks using an end-effector rehabilitation robot. We present an assessment of how subtle aspects of interface design impact user behavior; an analysis of how pathological neuromotor behaviors are reflected in end-effector force dynamics; and a novel hidden Markov model (HMM)-based neuromotor behavior classification method based on surface electromyography (sEMG) signals during cyclic motions. We demonstrate that task specification (including which axes are constrained and how users interpret tracking instructions) shapes user behavior; that pathology-related features are detectable in 6D end-effector force data during isometric task execution (with significant differences between healthy and post-stroke profiles in force error and average force production at $p=0.05$); and that healthy neuromotor strategies are heterogeneous and inherently difficult to characterize. We also show that our HMM-based models discriminate healthy and post-stroke neuromotor dynamics where synergy-based decompositions reflect no such differentiation. Lastly, we discuss these results' implications for the design of adaptive end-effector rehabilitation robots capable of promoting healthier movement strategies across diverse user populations.

</details>


### [18] [Autonomous Search for Sparsely Distributed Visual Phenomena through Environmental Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10174)
*Eric Chen,Travis Manderson,Nare Karapetyan,Peter Edmunds,Nicholas Roy,Yogesh Girdhar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种利用视觉环境上下文指导AUV高效搜索稀疏分布珊瑚物种的方法，通过单样本检测和自适应上下文建模，在稀疏目标分布下比全覆盖搜索节省约一半时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水下航行器(AUV)在珊瑚礁调查中面临挑战：目标物种分布稀疏且电池寿命有限，无法进行全面搜索。当目标检测过于稀疏无法提供方向指导时，需要额外信号来决定下一步搜索方向。

Method: 使用视觉环境上下文（与目标物种共生的栖息地特征）作为指导信号。从单张标注图像出发，利用patch级DINOv2嵌入进行在线单样本检测，同时检测目标物种及其周围环境上下文，结合自适应上下文建模指导搜索规划。

Result: 在美属维尔京群岛圣约翰两个珊瑚礁站点的真实AUV图像数据上进行离线模拟验证。结果显示：当目标稀疏分布时，该方法能采样约75%的目标物种，所需时间约为全覆盖搜索的一半，且优于仅使用目标检测的搜索策略。

Conclusion: 单样本检测与自适应上下文建模相结合，能够实现高效的自主珊瑚礁调查，在稀疏目标分布场景下显著提升搜索效率，为AUV的有限电池寿命约束下的智能搜索提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) are increasingly used to survey coral reefs, yet efficiently locating specific coral species of interest remains difficult: target species are often sparsely distributed across the reef, and an AUV with limited battery life cannot afford to search everywhere. When detections of the target itself are too sparse to provide directional guidance, the robot benefits from an additional signal to decide where to look next. We propose using the visual environmental context -- the habitat features that tend to co-occur with a target species -- as that signal. Because context features are spatially denser and often vary more smoothly than target detections, we hypothesize that a reward function targeted at broader environmental context will enable adaptive planners to make better decisions on where to go next, even in regions where no target has yet been observed. Starting from a single labeled image, our method uses patch-level DINOv2 embeddings to perform one-shot detections of both the target species and its surrounding context online. We validate our approach using real imagery collected by an AUV at two reef sites in St. John, U.S. Virgin Islands, simulating the robot's motion offline. Our results demonstrate that one-shot detection combined with adaptive context modeling enables efficient autonomous surveying, sampling up to 75$\%$ of the target in roughly half the time required by exhaustive coverage when the target is sparsely distributed, and outperforming search strategies that only use target detections.

</details>


### [19] [Octopus-inspired Distributed Control for Soft Robotic Arms: A Graph Neural Network-Based Attention Policy with Environmental Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10198)
*Linxin Hou,Qirui Wu,Zhihang Qin,Yongxin Guo,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SoftGM：一种受章鱼启发的分布式控制架构，用于分段软体机械臂，能够在接触丰富的环境中通过在线障碍物发现学习到达目标，无需依赖全局障碍物几何信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软体机械臂控制方法通常依赖全局环境信息或预定义障碍物模型，难以在未知或动态接触丰富的环境中实现鲁棒控制。需要一种能够在线发现障碍物并协调多段臂节的分布式控制架构。

Method: 将每个臂段建模为协作智能体，将臂-环境交互表示为图。采用集中训练分散执行（CTDE）范式，使用两阶段图注意力消息传递方案，包含集中式评论家和分散式执行器。

Result: 在Cosserat-rod模拟器（PyElastica）中评估，包含三个任务：无障碍、结构化障碍、墙孔场景。相比六种MARL基线方法（IDDPG、IPPO、ISAC、MADDPG、MAPPO、MASAC），SoftGM在简单场景中匹配强CTDE方法，在墙孔任务中表现最佳。在观测噪声、单段驱动故障和瞬态干扰的鲁棒性测试中，SoftGM保持成功率同时控制努力有界。

Conclusion: SoftGM通过选择性接触相关信息路由驱动的弹性协调，实现了在接触丰富环境中的鲁棒目标到达能力。该架构为软体机器人提供了无需全局环境模型的分布式控制解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes SoftGM, an octopus-inspired distributed control architecture for segmented soft robotic arms that learn to reach targets in contact-rich environments using online obstacle discovery without relying on global obstacle geometry. SoftGM formulates each arm section as a cooperative agent and represents the arm-environment interaction as a graph. SoftGM uses a two-stage graph attention message passing scheme following a Centralised Training Decentralised Execution (CTDE) paradigm with a centralised critic and decentralised actor. We evaluate SoftGM in a Cosserat-rod simulator (PyElastica) across three tasks that increase the complexity of the environment: obstacle-free, structured obstacles, and a wall-with-hole scenario. Compared with six widely used MARL baselines (IDDPG, IPPO, ISAC, MADDPG, MAPPO, MASAC) under identical information content and training conditions, SoftGM matches strong CTDE methods in simpler settings and achieves the best performance in the wall-with-hole task. Robustness tests with observation noise, single-section actuation failure, and transient disturbances show that SoftGM preserves success while keeping control effort bounded, indicating resilient coordination driven by selective contact-relevant information routing.

</details>


### [20] [Perceptive Hierarchical-Task MPC for Sequential Mobile Manipulation in Unstructured Semi-Static Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10227)
*Xintong Du,Jingxing Qian,Siqi Zhou,Angela P. Schoellig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出感知分层任务模型预测控制框架，用于非结构化变化环境中的顺序移动操作，通过贝叶斯推理建模物体级变化并嵌入词典优化框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序移动操作面临独特挑战：机器人长时间操作时，成功完成任务不仅依赖一致的运动生成，还需要对操作环境变化的感知和适应。现有运动规划器通常假设环境静态并依赖预计算地图，这在长期操作中经常失效，因为半静态变化（如物体移除、引入或移动）很常见。

Method: 提出感知分层任务模型预测控制框架：1）利用贝叶斯推理框架显式建模物体级变化，维护时间准确的3D环境表示；2）将这种最新表示嵌入词典优化框架，实现顺序任务的高效执行。

Result: 通过仿真和真实机器人实验验证了感知HTMPC方法。与基线方法相比，该方法系统性地考虑了移动和幻影障碍物，以更高的效率和反应性成功完成顺序任务，无需依赖先验地图或外部基础设施。

Conclusion: 提出的感知HTMPC框架能够有效处理非结构化变化环境中的顺序移动操作，通过显式建模环境变化和嵌入优化框架，实现了对动态环境的适应性和任务执行效率的提升。

Abstract: As compared to typical mobile manipulation tasks, sequential mobile manipulation poses a unique challenge -- as the robot operates over extended periods, successful task completion is not solely dependent on consistent motion generation but also on the robot's awareness and adaptivity to changes in the operating environment. While existing motion planners can generate whole-body trajectories to complete sequential tasks, they typically assume that the environment remains static and rely on precomputed maps. This assumption often breaks down during long-term operations, where semi-static changes such as object removal, introduction, or shifts are common. In this work, we propose a novel perceptive hierarchical-task model predictive control (HTMPC) framework for efficient sequential mobile manipulation in unstructured, changing environments. To tackle the challenge, we leverage a Bayesian inference framework to explicitly model object-level changes and thereby maintain a temporally accurate representation of the 3D environment; this up-to-date representation is embedded in a lexicographic optimization framework to enable efficient execution of sequential tasks. We validate our perceptive HTMPC approach through both simulated and real-robot experiments. In contrast to baseline methods, our approach systematically accounts for moved and phantom obstacles, successfully completing sequential tasks with higher efficiency and reactivity, without relying on prior maps or external infrastructure.

</details>


### [21] [Hierarchical Task Model Predictive Control for Sequential Mobile Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10232)
*Xintong Du,Siqi Zhou,Angela P. Schoellig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于非线性词典优化的分层任务模型预测控制框架，用于移动机械臂的顺序任务执行，相比现有方法在轨迹跟踪性能上提升42%，执行时间快2.3倍


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的发展，任务规划器能更好地将人类语言指令转换为任务序列，但需要决策算法与高层任务规划器无缝对接以高效执行任务序列

Method: 基于非线性词典优化思想，提出分层任务模型预测控制框架，有效利用机器人冗余度，通过分层轨迹跟踪完成顺序任务

Result: 相比最先进的任务优先级逆运动学控制方法，在任务变化、机器人奇异性和参考变化情况下，分层轨迹跟踪性能平均提升42%；相比典型单任务架构，在任务空间中路径更短，执行时间快2.3倍

Conclusion: 提出的分层任务控制框架能够高效执行顺序任务，在9自由度移动机械臂上通过真实世界实验验证了其优越性能

Abstract: Mobile manipulators are envisioned to serve more complex roles in people's everyday lives. With recent breakthroughs in large language models, task planners have become better at translating human verbal instructions into a sequence of tasks. However, there is still a need for a decision-making algorithm that can seamlessly interface with the high-level task planner to carry out the sequence of tasks efficiently. In this work, building on the idea of nonlinear lexicographic optimization, we propose a novel Hierarchical-Task Model Predictive Control framework that is able to complete sequential tasks with improved performance and reactivity by effectively leveraging the robot's redundancy. Compared to the state-of-the-art task-prioritized inverse kinematic control method, our approach has improved hierarchical trajectory tracking performance by 42% on average when facing task changes, robot singularity and reference variations. Compared to a typical single-task architecture, our proposed hierarchical task control architecture enables the robot to traverse a shorter path in task space and achieves an execution time 2.3 times faster when executing a sequence of delivery tasks. We demonstrated the results with real-world experiments on a 9 degrees of freedom mobile manipulator.

</details>


### [22] [Degeneracy-Resilient Teach and Repeat for Geometrically Challenging Environments Using FMCW Lidar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10248)
*Katya M. Papais,Wenda Zhao,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于FMCW激光雷达的退化环境鲁棒性Teach and Repeat导航系统，通过多普勒速度里程计和退化感知扫描到地图定位，解决了传统ICP方法在几何退化环境中的失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Teach and Repeat导航系统依赖ICP估计，但在几何退化环境（如稀疏结构地形）中，ICP会变得病态，导致定位性能下降和导航不可靠。需要一种能在退化环境中稳定工作的导航方案。

Method: 1) 利用FMCW激光雷达提供的每点径向速度测量，开发几何无关、无对应关系的运动估计方法，包含稳定的位姿不确定性估计；2) 提出退化感知定位方法，结合每点曲率改进数据关联，统一平移和旋转尺度以实现一致的退化检测。

Result: 在三个不同结构丰富度的环境中进行闭环现场实验，证明所提系统能可靠完成自主导航，包括在平坦机场测试场等挑战性环境中，而传统ICP系统在该环境下会失败。

Conclusion: 基于FMCW激光雷达的退化弹性T&R导航系统能有效应对几何退化环境，通过多普勒速度里程计和退化感知定位，在传统ICP方法失效的场景中仍能保持可靠导航性能。

Abstract: Teach and Repeat (T&R) topometric navigation enables robots to autonomously repeat previously traversed paths without relying on GPS, making it well suited for operations in GPS-denied environments such as underground mines and lunar navigation. State-of-the-art T&R systems typically rely on iterative closest point (ICP)-based estimation; however, in geometrically degenerate environments with sparsely structured terrain, ICP often becomes ill-conditioned, resulting in degraded localization and unreliable navigation performance. To address this challenge, we present a degeneracy-resilient Frequency-Modulated Continuous-Wave (FMCW) lidar T&R navigation system consisting of Doppler velocity-based odometry and degeneracy-aware scan-to-map localization. Leveraging FMCW lidar, which provides per-point radial velocity measurements via the Doppler effect, we extend a geometry-independent, correspondence-free motion estimation to include principled pose uncertainty estimation that remains stable in degenerate environments. We further propose a degeneracy-aware localization method that incorporates per-point curvature for improved data association, and unifies translational and rotational scales to enable consistent degeneracy detection. Closed-loop field experiments across three environments with varying structural richness demonstrate that the proposed system reliably completes autonomous navigation, including in a challenging flat airport test field where a conventional ICP-based system fails.

</details>


### [23] [From Prior to Pro: Efficient Skill Mastery via Distribution Contractive RL Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10263)
*Zhanyi Sun,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DICE-RL是一个强化学习框架，通过"分布收缩"操作将预训练的生成式机器人策略精炼为高性能策略，利用在线反馈放大高成功率行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练的生成式机器人策略虽然具有广泛的行为覆盖，但性能不够优化。需要一种方法能够稳定、高效地将这些行为先验转化为高性能策略，同时处理复杂的长时程操作任务。

Method: 首先预训练基于扩散或流的策略以获得广泛行为覆盖，然后通过结合选择性行为正则化和价值引导动作选择的残差离策略强化学习框架进行微调，实现稳定且样本高效的策略优化。

Result: DICE-RL在模拟和真实机器人实验中均表现出可靠的性能提升，具有强稳定性和样本效率，能够直接从高维像素输入掌握复杂的长时程操作技能。

Conclusion: DICE-RL提供了一个有效的框架，能够将预训练的生成式行为先验转化为高性能策略，为复杂机器人技能学习提供了稳定且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce Distribution Contractive Reinforcement Learning (DICE-RL), a framework that uses reinforcement learning (RL) as a "distribution contraction" operator to refine pretrained generative robot policies. DICE-RL turns a pretrained behavior prior into a high-performing "pro" policy by amplifying high-success behaviors from online feedback. We pretrain a diffusion- or flow-based policy for broad behavioral coverage, then finetune it with a stable, sample-efficient residual off-policy RL framework that combines selective behavior regularization with value-guided action selection. Extensive experiments and analyses show that DICE-RL reliably improves performance with strong stability and sample efficiency. It enables mastery of complex long-horizon manipulation skills directly from high-dimensional pixel inputs, both in simulation and on a real robot. Project website: https://zhanyisun.github.io/dice.rl.2026/.

</details>


### [24] [Design of a Robot-Assisted Chemical Dialysis System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10264)
*Diane Jung,Caleb Escobedo,Noah Liska,Maitrey Gramopadhye,Daniel Szafir,Alessandro Roncone,Carson Bruns*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究人员开发了一种用于透析实验的机器人辅助系统，旨在减轻科学家在繁琐实验程序中的工作负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学实验中的手动操作程序繁琐且耗时，成为现代实验科学的瓶颈，特别是在材料科学和医学领域。透析作为聚合物和蛋白质合成中常用的多日纯化方法，需要自动化解决方案来减轻科学家的工作负担。

Method: 采用以用户为中心的设计方法，通过两次可用性研究收集参与者反馈，修订设计需求，最终开发出满足科学家需求的机器人辅助透析系统。

Result: 开发出最终系统，该系统满足科学家的需求，并具有应用于其他实验工作流程的潜力。系统设计经过可用性研究的验证和优化。

Conclusion: 将该系统整合到化学湿实验室的实际合成程序中，将减少科学家在长期实验过程中的工作负担，并为设计更多能够加速科学发现、解放科学家免受繁琐劳动的系统提供有效方法。

Abstract: Scientists perform diverse manual procedures that are tedious and laborious. Such procedures are considered a bottleneck for modern experimental science, as they consume time and increase burdens in fields including material science and medicine. We employ a user-centered approach to designing a robot-assisted system for dialysis, a common multi-day purification method used in polymer and protein synthesis. Through two usability studies, we obtain participant feedback and revise design requirements to develop the final system that satisfies scientists' needs and has the potential for applications in other experimental workflows. We anticipate that integration of this system into real synthesis procedures in a chemical wet lab will decrease workload on scientists during long experimental procedures and provide an effective approach to designing more systems that have the potential to accelerate scientific discovery and liberate scientists from tedious labor.

</details>


### [25] [Update-Free On-Policy Steering via Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10282)
*Maria Attarian,Ian Vyse,Claas Voelcker,Jasper Gerigk,Evgenii Opryshko,Anas Almasri,Sumeet Singh,Yilun Du,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UF-OPS是一种无需更新的在线策略引导方法，通过训练验证器函数来预测动作成功概率，引导基础策略选择更高成功率的动作，显著提升行为克隆策略的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆策略虽然广泛应用，但通常脆弱且难以处理精确操作任务。现有方法在策略执行时缺乏适应性，无法根据实时情况调整动作选择。

Method: 提出UF-OPS方法：1）在策略初始评估阶段收集策略展开数据；2）使用这些数据训练验证器函数来预测动作成功概率；3）在执行时使用验证器引导基础策略选择更高成功率的动作。该方法不改变基础策略参数，保持轻量级和灵活性。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，该方法在5个真实任务上平均提升了49%的成功率，显著改善了黑盒扩散策略的性能。

Conclusion: UF-OPS通过在线策略引导有效解决了行为克隆策略的脆弱性问题，提供了一种轻量级、无需参数更新的性能提升方法，在机器人精确操作任务中表现出显著优势。

Abstract: In recent years, Behavior Cloning (BC) has become one of the most prevalent methods for enabling robots to mimic human demonstrations. However, despite their successes, BC policies are often brittle and struggle with precise manipulation. To overcome these issues, we propose UF-OPS, an Update-Free On-Policy Steering method that enables the robot to predict the success likelihood of its actions and adapt its strategy at execution time. We accomplish this by training verifier functions using policy rollout data obtained during an initial evaluation of the policy. These verifiers are subsequently used to steer the base policy toward actions with a higher likelihood of success. Our method improves the performance of black-box diffusion policy, without changing the base parameters, making it light-weight and flexible. We present results from both simulation and real-world data and achieve an average 49% improvement in success rate over the base policy across 5 real tasks.

</details>


### [26] [SteadyTray: Learning Object Balancing Tasks in Humanoid Tray Transport via Residual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10306)
*Anlun Huang,Zhenyu Wu,Soofiyan Atar,Yuheng Zhi,Michael Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReST-RL：一种分层强化学习架构，将双足机器人的运动与负载稳定解耦，通过残差模块主动抵消步态引起的扰动，实现稳定托盘运输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决双足机器人在非结构化环境中动态运动时负载不稳定的关键工程瓶颈问题，传统端到端学习方法难以同时保证运动稳定性和负载稳定性。

Method: 提出ReST-RL分层强化学习架构：1）鲁棒的基础运动策略；2）动态残差模块专门用于主动抵消末端执行器的步态诱导扰动。这种架构分离确保托盘稳定运输而不损害双足稳定性。

Result: 在仿真中显著优于端到端基线：步态平滑度和方向精度提升，变速度跟踪成功率96.9%，外部力扰动鲁棒性74.5%。成功部署在Unitree G1人形机器人硬件上，实现零样本仿真到现实泛化。

Conclusion: ReST-RL的分层架构有效解决了双足机器人负载稳定问题，模块化设计实现了可靠的零样本仿真到现实泛化，为动态双足机器人在非结构化环境中的应用提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Stabilizing unsecured payloads against the inherent oscillations of dynamic bipedal locomotion remains a critical engineering bottleneck for humanoids in unstructured environments. To solve this, we introduce ReST-RL, a hierarchical reinforcement learning architecture that explicitly decouples locomotion from payload stabilization, evaluated via the SteadyTray benchmark. Rather than relying on monolithic end-to-end learning, our framework integrates a robust base locomotion policy with a dynamic residual module engineered to actively cancel gait-induced perturbations at the end-effector. This architectural separation ensures steady tray transport without degrading the underlying bipedal stability. In simulation, the residual design significantly outperforms end-to-end baselines in gait smoothness and orientation accuracy, achieving a 96.9% success rate in variable velocity tracking and 74.5% robustness against external force disturbances. Successfully deployed on the Unitree G1 humanoid hardware, this modular approach demonstrates highly reliable zero-shot sim-to-real generalization across various objects and external force disturbances.

</details>


### [27] [PC-Diffuser: Path-Consistent Capsule CBF Safety Filtering for Diffusion-Based Trajectory Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10330)
*Eugene Ku,Yiwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PC-Diffuser：一种将可认证的安全屏障函数结构嵌入扩散规划去噪循环的安全增强框架，通过路径一致的安全过滤实现迭代式安全保障


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂交通中的自动驾驶需要超越手工规则的数据驱动规划器，但基于扩散的轨迹规划器虽然闭环性能强，却难以认证且在罕见或分布外场景可能发生灾难性故障

Method: PC-Diffuser框架将可认证的路径一致屏障函数结构直接嵌入扩散规划的去噪循环中：(1)使用胶囊距离屏障函数评估碰撞风险，(2)将去噪航点转换为动力学自行车模型下的可行运动，(3)应用路径一致安全过滤器消除约束违规而不产生几何失真

Result: 通过在每个去噪步骤注入安全一致的修正并将精炼轨迹反馈回扩散过程，PC-Diffuser实现了迭代式、上下文感知的安全保障而非后处理修复

Conclusion: PC-Diffuser使安全成为轨迹生成的内在部分而非后处理修复，在保持扩散模型路径几何的同时确保前向不变性，提高了自动驾驶规划的安全性和可认证性

Abstract: Autonomous driving in complex traffic requires planners that generalize beyond hand-crafted rules, motivating data-driven approaches that learn behavior from expert demonstrations. Diffusion-based trajectory planners have recently shown strong closed-loop performance by iteratively denoising a full-horizon plan, but they remain difficult to certify and can fail catastrophically in rare or out-of-distribution scenarios. To address this challenge, we present PC-Diffuser, a safety augmentation framework that embeds a certifiable, path-consistent barrier-function structure directly into the denoising loop of diffusion planning. The key idea is to make safety an intrinsic part of trajectory generation rather than a post-hoc fix: we enforce forward invariance along the rollout while preserving the diffusion model's intended path geometry. Specifically, PC-Diffuser (i) evaluates collision risk using a capsule-distance barrier function that better reflects vehicle geometry and reduces unnecessary conservativeness, (ii) converts denoised waypoints into dynamically feasible motion under a kinematic bicycle model, and (iii) applies a path-consistent safety filter that eliminates residual constraint violations without geometric distortion, so the corrected plan remains close to the learned distribution. By injecting these safety-consistent corrections at every denoising step and feeding the refined trajectory back into the diffusion process, PC-Diffuser enables iterative, context-aware safeguarding instead of post-hoc repair...

</details>


### [28] [Adaptive Manipulation Potential and Haptic Estimation for Tool-Mediated Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10352)
*Lin Yang,Anirvan Dutta,Yuan Ji,Yanxin Zhou,Shilin Shan,Lv Chen,Etienne Burdet,Domenico Campolo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于参数化平衡流形的工具操作统一表示方法，结合触觉SLAM、在线规划和自适应刚度控制，实现接触丰富的工具操作任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决工具操作中视觉遮挡和触觉感知不确定性带来的挑战，实现人类水平的灵巧操作能力

Method: 引入参数化平衡流形作为统一表示，建立物理-几何对偶性，采用混合推理策略（触觉SLAM）进行物体形状分类和位姿估计，通过动态更新操作势能参数实现在线轨迹重规划和不确定性感知阻抗控制

Result: 在模拟和260多次真实世界螺丝松动实验中验证了系统有效性，展示了在标准场景下的鲁棒识别和操作成功率，同时保持精确跟踪；消融研究证实触觉SLAM和不确定性感知刚度调制优于固定阻抗基线

Conclusion: 提出的平衡流形框架为接触丰富的工具操作提供了统一的表示和控制方法，通过触觉感知和自适应控制的闭环集成，有效解决了视觉遮挡和触觉不确定性问题

Abstract: Achieving human-level dexterity in contact-rich, tool-mediated manipulation remains a significant challenge due to visual occlusion and the underdetermined nature of haptic sensing. This paper introduces a parameterized Equilibrium Manifold (EM) as a unified representation for tool-mediated interaction, and develops a closed-loop framework that integrates haptic estimation, online planning, and adaptive stiffness control. We establish a physical-geometric duality using an adaptive manipulation potential incorporating a differentiable contact model, which induces the manifold's geometric structure and ensures that complex physical interactions are encapsulated as continuous operations on the EM. Within this framework, we reformulate haptic estimation as a manifold parameter estimation problem. Specifically, a hybrid inference strategy (haptic SLAM) is employed in which discrete object shapes are classified via particle filtering, while the continuous object pose is estimated using analytical gradients for efficient optimization. By continuously updating the parameters of the manipulation potential, the framework dynamically reshapes the induced EM to guide online trajectory replanning and implement uncertainty-aware impedance control, thereby closing the perception-action loop. The system is validated through simulation and over 260 real-world screw-loosening trials. Experimental results demonstrate robust identification and manipulation success in standard scenarios while maintaining accurate tracking. Furthermore, ablation studies confirm that haptic SLAM and uncertainty-aware stiffness modulation outperform fixed impedance baselines, effectively preventing jamming during tight tolerance interactions.

</details>


### [29] [Few-Shot Adaptation to Non-Stationary Environments via Latent Trend Embedding for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10373)
*Yasuyuki Fujii,Emika Kameda,Hiroki Fukada,Yoshiki Mori,Tadashi Matsuo,Nobutaka Shimada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于潜在趋势ID的少样本适应框架，通过估计低维环境状态而非修改模型参数来应对非平稳环境中的概念漂移问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在真实环境中运行时面临概念漂移问题，传统适应方法通过更新模型参数会导致灾难性遗忘和高计算成本，需要一种更高效、可扩展的适应方案

Method: 提出潜在趋势ID框架：1）通过反向传播估计低维环境状态（趋势ID）而不修改模型参数；2）引入时间正则化防止过拟合；3）使用状态转移模型确保潜在空间平滑演化

Result: 在定量食物抓取任务上的实验表明：学习的趋势ID在潜在空间中分布在不同的区域且具有时间一致的轨迹，无需修改模型参数即可实现少样本适应到未见环境

Conclusion: 该框架为在多样化和演化环境中运行的机器人应用提供了可扩展和可解释的解决方案，通过估计环境状态而非修改模型权重来适应概念漂移

Abstract: Robotic systems operating in real-world environments often suffer from concept shift, where the input-output relationship changes due to latent environmental factors that are not directly observable. Conventional adaptation methods update model parameters, which may cause catastrophic forgetting and incur high computational cost. This paper proposes a latent Trend ID-based framework for few-shot adaptation in non-stationary environments. Instead of modifying model weights, a low-dimensional environmental state, referred to as the Trend ID, is estimated via backpropagation while the model parameters remain fixed. To prevent overfitting caused by per-sample latent variables, we introduce temporal regularization and a state transition model that enforces smooth evolution of the latent space. Experiments on a quantitative food grasping task demonstrate that the learned Trend IDs are distributed across distinct regions of the latent space with temporally consistent trajectories, and that few-shot adaptation to unseen environments is achieved without modifying model parameters. The proposed framework provides a scalable and interpretable solution for robotics applications operating across diverse and evolving environments.

</details>


### [30] [ScanDP: Generalizable 3D Scanning with Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10390)
*Itsuki Hirako,Ryo Hakoda,Yubin Liu,Matthew Hwang,Yoshihiro Sato,Takeshi Oishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据高效的3D扫描框架，使用扩散策略模仿人类扫描策略，通过占据栅格映射和混合空间表示提高鲁棒性和泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的3D扫描方法需要大量训练数据且难以泛化到未见过的物体类别，需要更高效、鲁棒且泛化能力强的扫描方法

Method: 采用扩散策略模仿人类扫描策略，使用占据栅格映射替代直接点云处理以提高噪声鲁棒性，结合球体空间表示和路径优化确保路径安全与扫描效率

Result: 在形状和尺度各异的未见物体上评估，相比基线方法获得更高的覆盖率和更短的路径，对传感器噪声保持鲁棒，在真实世界执行中验证了实用可行性

Conclusion: 提出的数据高效3D扫描框架通过模仿人类策略和鲁棒的空间表示，解决了传统方法的泛化限制，实现了高效、鲁棒的扫描性能

Abstract: Learning-based 3D Scanning plays a crucial role in enabling efficient and accurate scanning of target objects. However, recent reinforcement learning-based methods often require large-scale training data and still struggle to generalize to unseen object categories.In this work, we propose a data-efficient 3D scanning framework that uses Diffusion Policy to imitate human-like scanning strategies. To enhance robustness and generalization, we adopt the Occupancy Grid Mapping instead of direct point cloud processing, offering improved noise resilience and handling of diverse object geometries. We also introduce a hybrid approach combining a sphere-based space representation with a path optimization procedure that ensures path safety and scanning efficiency. This approach addresses limitations in conventional imitation learning, such as redundant or unpredictable behavior. We evaluate our method on diverse unseen objects in both shape and scale. Ours achieves higher coverage and shorter paths than baselines, while remaining robust to sensor noise. We further confirm practical feasibility and stable operation in real-world execution.

</details>


### [31] [Safe Probabilistic Planning for Human-Robot Interaction using Conformal Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10392)
*Jake Gonzales,Kazuki Mizuta,Karen Leung,Lillian J. Ratliff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结合控制屏障函数与保形风险控制的概率安全控制框架，为人机交互提供形式化安全保证，通过动态调整安全边界显著降低碰撞率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互中需要考虑复杂的人类行为，现有方法难以在提供形式化安全保证的同时处理预测不确定性，需要一种能量化预测误差并控制安全风险的框架

Method: 结合控制屏障函数与保形风险控制，使用保形风险控制量化CBF安全值的预测误差，建立约束满足概率的形式化保证，并引入基于当前交互上下文动态调整安全边界的算法

Result: 在人机导航场景实验中，相比基线方法显著降低了碰撞率和安全违规，同时在目标到达任务中保持高成功率和高控制效率

Conclusion: 提出的概率安全控制框架能有效处理人机交互中的不确定性，提供形式化安全保证，在保证安全的同时维持任务性能，为安全关键人机交互系统提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: In this paper, we present a novel probabilistic safe control framework for human-robot interaction that combines control barrier functions (CBFs) with conformal risk control to provide formal safety guarantees while considering complex human behavior. The approach uses conformal risk control to quantify and control the prediction errors in CBF safety values and establishes formal guarantees on the probability of constraint satisfaction during interaction. We introduce an algorithm that dynamically adjusts the safety margins produced by conformal risk control based on the current interaction context. Through experiments on human-robot navigation scenarios, we demonstrate that our approach significantly reduces collision rates and safety violations as compared to baseline methods while maintaining high success rates in goal-reaching tasks and efficient control. The code, simulations, and other supplementary material can be found on the project website: https://jakeagonzales.github.io/crc-cbf-website/.

</details>


### [32] [Rethinking Gaussian Trajectory Predictors: Calibrated Uncertainty for Safe Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10407)
*Fatemeh Cheraghi Pouria,Mahsa Golchoubian,Katherine Driggs-Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于校准轨迹预测不确定性的新损失函数，通过核密度估计匹配经验分布与卡方分布，提高高斯轨迹预测器置信水平的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯轨迹预测器主要依赖负对数似然损失，容易产生过度自信或不足自信的分布，影响下游不确定性感知规划器的安全性。预测置信水平的可靠性问题尚未得到充分解决。

Method: 提出新颖的损失函数，利用核密度估计估计置信水平的经验分布，通过显式匹配经验分布与卡方分布来强制满足高斯假设特性。同时结合均方误差项确保准确的均值预测。

Result: 在真实世界轨迹数据集上的实验表明，该方法显著提高了不同最先进高斯轨迹预测器的置信水平可靠性。与不确定性感知模型预测控制集成后，在复杂场景中实现了更好的规划性能。

Conclusion: 通过校准置信水平，为规划器提供可靠的概率洞察对于复杂场景中的无碰撞导航至关重要。所提方法有效解决了高斯轨迹预测器置信水平可靠性问题，提升了不确定性感知规划的安全性。

Abstract: Accurate trajectory prediction is critical for safe autonomous navigation in crowded environments. While many trajectory predictors output Gaussian distributions to represent the multi-modal distribution over future pedestrian positions, the reliability of their confidence levels often remains unaddressed. This limitation can lead to unsafe or overly conservative motion planning when the predictor is integrated with an uncertainty-aware planner. Existing Gaussian trajectory predictors primarily rely on the Negative Log-Likelihood loss, which is prone to predict over- or under-confident distributions, and may compromise downstream planner safety. This paper introduces a novel loss function for calibrating prediction uncertainty which leverages Kernel Density Estimation to estimate the empirical distribution of confidence levels. The proposed formulation enforces consistency with the properties of a Gaussian assumption by explicitly matching the estimated empirical distribution to the Chi-squared distribution. To ensure accurate mean prediction, a Mean Squared Error term is also incorporated in the final loss formulation. Experimental results on real-world trajectory datasets show that our method significantly improves the reliability of confidence levels predicted by different State-Of-The-Art Gaussian trajectory predictors. We also demonstrate the importance of providing planners with reliable probabilistic insights (i.e. calibrated confidence levels) for collision-free navigation in complex scenarios. For this purpose, we integrate Gaussian trajectory predictors trained with our loss function with an uncertainty-aware Model Predictive Control on scenarios extracted from real-world datasets, achieving improved planning performance through calibrated confidence levels.

</details>


### [33] [AsyncMDE: Real-Time Monocular Depth Estimation via Asynchronous Spatial Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10438)
*Lianjie Ma,Yuquan Li,Bingzheng Jiang,Ziming Zhong,Han Ding,Lijun Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AsyncMDE：一种异步深度感知系统，通过基础模型和轻量级模型的分工合作，在边缘设备上实现高效的单目深度估计，显著降低计算成本同时保持较高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于基础模型的单目深度估计计算成本高，难以在边缘平台部署。现有方法独立处理每帧，浪费了连续机器人操作中相邻视角间的计算冗余。

Method: 提出异步深度感知系统AsyncMDE，包含基础模型和轻量级模型。基础模型在后台生成高质量空间特征，轻量级模型在前台异步运行，通过互补融合将缓存记忆与当前观测结合，输出深度估计并自回归更新记忆。

Result: 仅3.83M参数，在RTX 4090上达到237 FPS，恢复基础模型精度差距的77%，同时实现25倍参数减少。在Jetson AGX Orin上通过TensorRT达到161 FPS，验证了实时边缘部署可行性。

Conclusion: AsyncMDE通过跨帧特征重用和异步处理，在保持有界精度下降的前提下，显著降低了计算成本，为机器人感知系统在边缘设备上的实时部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Foundation-model-based monocular depth estimation offers a viable alternative to active sensors for robot perception, yet its computational cost often prohibits deployment on edge platforms. Existing methods perform independent per-frame inference, wasting the substantial computational redundancy between adjacent viewpoints in continuous robot operation. This paper presents AsyncMDE, an asynchronous depth perception system consisting of a foundation model and a lightweight model that amortizes the foundation model's computational cost over time. The foundation model produces high-quality spatial features in the background, while the lightweight model runs asynchronously in the foreground, fusing cached memory with current observations through complementary fusion, outputting depth estimates, and autoregressively updating the memory. This enables cross-frame feature reuse with bounded accuracy degradation. At a mere 3.83M parameters, it operates at 237 FPS on an RTX 4090, recovering 77% of the accuracy gap to the foundation model while achieving a 25X parameter reduction. Validated across indoor static, dynamic, and synthetic extreme-motion benchmarks, AsyncMDE degrades gracefully between refreshes and achieves 161FPS on a Jetson AGX Orin with TensorRT, clearly demonstrating its feasibility for real-time edge deployment.

</details>


### [34] [KnowDiffuser: A Knowledge-Guided Diffusion Planner with LM Reasoning and Prior-Informed Trajectory Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10441)
*Fan Ding,Xuewen Luo,Fengze Yang,Bo Yu,HwaHui Tew,Ganesh Krishnasamy,Junn Yong Loo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KnowDiffuser是一个知识引导的运动规划框架，将语言模型的语义理解与扩散模型的生成能力相结合，用于自动驾驶系统，在nuPlan基准测试中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型具有强大的语义推理能力但无法生成连续的物理可行轨迹，而扩散模型能生成可靠轨迹但缺乏语义可解释性和场景级理解，需要弥合自动驾驶中的语义到物理的鸿沟。

Method: 提出KnowDiffuser框架：1) 使用语言模型从结构化场景表示中推断上下文感知的元动作；2) 将这些元动作映射到先验轨迹以锚定去噪过程；3) 采用两阶段截断去噪机制高效细化轨迹，保持语义对齐和物理可行性。

Result: 在nuPlan基准测试中，KnowDiffuser在开环和闭环评估中都显著优于现有规划器，建立了鲁棒且可解释的框架，有效弥合了自动驾驶系统中的语义到物理鸿沟。

Conclusion: KnowDiffuser成功地将语言模型的语义理解与扩散模型的生成能力紧密集成，为自动驾驶系统提供了一个既保持语义对齐又保证物理可行性的运动规划框架，在性能上超越了现有方法。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Language Models (LMs) have demonstrated strong semantic reasoning capabilities, enabling their application in high-level decision-making for autonomous driving (AD). However, LMs operate over discrete token spaces and lack the ability to generate continuous, physically feasible trajectories required for motion planning. Meanwhile, diffusion models have proven effective at generating reliable and dynamically consistent trajectories, but often lack semantic interpretability and alignment with scene-level understanding. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{KnowDiffuser}, a knowledge-guided motion planning framework that tightly integrates the semantic understanding of language models with the generative power of diffusion models. The framework employs a language model to infer context-aware meta-actions from structured scene representations, which are then mapped to prior trajectories that anchor the subsequent denoising process. A two-stage truncated denoising mechanism refines these trajectories efficiently, preserving both semantic alignment and physical feasibility. Experiments on the nuPlan benchmark demonstrate that KnowDiffuser significantly outperforms existing planners in both open-loop and closed-loop evaluations, establishing a robust and interpretable framework that effectively bridges the semantic-to-physical gap in AD systems.

</details>


### [35] [FAR-Dex: Few-shot Data Augmentation and Adaptive Residual Policy Refinement for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10451)
*Yushan Bai,Fulin Chen,Hongzheng Sun,Yuchuang Tong,En Li,Zhengtao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FAR-Dex是一个分层框架，通过少样本数据增强和自适应残差精炼实现灵巧的臂-手协同操作，在仿真和真实世界中都取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现类人的多指手与机械臂协同灵巧操作是机器人学中的长期挑战，主要由于高质量演示数据的稀缺和高维动作空间的复杂性。

Method: 提出FAR-Dex分层框架：1) FAR-DexGen利用IsaacLab仿真器从少量演示生成多样且物理约束的轨迹；2) FAR-DexRes引入自适应残差模块，通过结合多步轨迹段和观测特征来精炼策略。

Result: 实验表明：数据质量提升13.4%，任务成功率比最先进方法提高7%，在真实世界任务中达到80%以上的成功率，实现了具有强位置泛化能力的精细灵巧操作。

Conclusion: FAR-Dex通过集成少样本数据增强和自适应残差精炼，有效解决了臂-手协同操作中的数据稀缺和策略优化问题，为灵巧操作提供了鲁棒且精确的解决方案。

Abstract: Achieving human-like dexterous manipulation through the collaboration of multi-fingered hands with robotic arms remains a longstanding challenge in robotics, primarily due to the scarcity of high-quality demonstrations and the complexity of high-dimensional action spaces. To address these challenges, we propose FAR-Dex, a hierarchical framework that integrates few-shot data augmentation with adaptive residual refinement to enable robust and precise arm-hand coordination in dexterous tasks. First, FAR-DexGen leverages the IsaacLab simulator to generate diverse and physically constrained trajectories from a few demonstrations, providing a data foundation for policy training. Second, FAR-DexRes introduces an adaptive residual module that refines policies by combining multi-step trajectory segments with observation features, thereby enhancing accuracy and robustness in manipulation scenarios. Experiments in both simulation and real-world demonstrate that FAR-Dex improves data quality by 13.4% and task success rates by 7% over state-of-the-art methods. It further achieves over 80% success in real-world tasks, enabling fine-grained dexterous manipulation with strong positional generalization.

</details>


### [36] [BinWalker: Development and Field Evaluation of a Quadruped Manipulator Platform for Sustainable Litter Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10529)
*Giulio Turrisi,Angelo Bratta,Giovanni Minelli,Gabriel Fischer Abati,Amir H. Rad,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 四足机器人系统用于在具有挑战性的户外环境中自主收集垃圾，结合了腿式移动的优势和机械臂操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垃圾污染日益严重，特别是在难以进入的区域（如不平坦地形、海岸环境等）。传统人工清理方式劳动密集、耗时且成本高，需要机器人解决方案来支持环境清理任务。

Method: 开发了一个四足机器人系统，结合腿式移动的优势（适应不平坦地形）和操作系统（机械臂和车载垃圾容器）。系统能够检测、抓取和存储垃圾物品，同时在不平坦地形中导航。

Result: 在户外场景中的实验评估表明，该方法在具有挑战性的环境中有效，展示了将感知、移动和操作集成到腿式机器人平台上的可行性，能够协助大规模垃圾清理操作。

Conclusion: 该四足机器人系统展示了在传统机器人平台难以到达的环境中自主收集垃圾的潜力，为大规模环境清理操作提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Litter pollution represents a growing environmental problem affecting natural and urban ecosystems worldwide. Waste discarded in public spaces often accumulates in areas that are difficult to access, such as uneven terrains, coastal environments, parks, and roadside vegetation. Over time, these materials degrade and release harmful substances, including toxic chemicals and microplastics, which can contaminate soil and water and pose serious threats to wildlife and human health. Despite increasing awareness of the problem, litter collection is still largely performed manually by human operators, making large-scale cleanup operations labor-intensive, time-consuming, and costly. Robotic solutions have the potential to support and partially automate environmental cleanup tasks. In this work, we present a quadruped robotic system designed for autonomous litter collection in challenging outdoor scenarios. The robot combines the mobility advantages of legged locomotion with a manipulation system consisting of a robotic arm and an onboard litter container. This configuration enables the robot to detect, grasp, and store litter items while navigating through uneven terrains. The proposed system aims to demonstrate the feasibility of integrating perception, locomotion, and manipulation on a legged robotic platform for environmental cleanup tasks. Experimental evaluations conducted in outdoor scenarios highlight the effectiveness of the approach and its potential for assisting large-scale litter removal operations in environments that are difficult to reach with traditional robotic platforms. The code associated with this work can be found at: https://github.com/iit-DLSLab/trash-collection-isaaclab.

</details>


### [37] [TacLoc: Global Tactile Localization on Objects from a Registration Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10565)
*Zirui Zhang,Boyang Zhang,Fumin Zhang,Huan Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacLoc：一种新颖的触觉定位框架，将触觉姿态估计转化为单次点云配准任务，无需渲染数据或预训练模型，通过图论部分到完整配准方法实现高效准确估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人操作中，当夹爪与物体交互导致视觉感知被遮挡时，姿态估计至关重要。现有触觉方法通常依赖触觉仿真或预训练模型，这限制了其泛化能力和效率。

Method: 提出TacLoc框架，将问题形式化为单次点云配准任务。采用图论部分到完整配准方法，利用触觉感知获取的密集点云和表面法线。通过法线引导的图剪枝和假设-验证流程实现高效准确姿态估计。

Result: 在YCB数据集上进行了广泛评估，并在两种不同的视觉-触觉传感器上对真实世界物体进行了演示，显示出改进的性能。

Conclusion: TacLoc提供了一种无需渲染数据或预训练模型的触觉定位解决方案，通过图论部分到完整配准方法实现了高效准确的姿态估计，在机器人操作中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Pose estimation is essential for robotic manipulation, particularly when visual perception is occluded during gripper-object interactions. Existing tactile-based methods generally rely on tactile simulation or pre-trained models, which limits their generalizability and efficiency. In this study, we propose TacLoc, a novel tactile localization framework that formulates the problem as a one-shot point cloud registration task. TacLoc introduces a graph-theoretic partial-to-full registration method, leveraging dense point clouds and surface normals from tactile sensing for efficient and accurate pose estimation. Without requiring rendered data or pre-trained models, TacLoc achieves improved performance through normal-guided graph pruning and a hypothesis-and-verification pipeline. TacLoc is evaluated extensively on the YCB dataset. We further demonstrate TacLoc on real-world objects across two different visual-tactile sensors.

</details>


### [38] [Safety-critical Control Under Partial Observability: Reach-Avoid POMDP meets Belief Space Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10572)
*Matti Vahs,Joris Verhagen,Jana Tumova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于证书的分层控制架构，用于解决部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程中的到达-规避问题，将目标到达、信息收集和安全性解耦为模块化组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线POMDP求解器在单一信念树搜索中同时处理目标到达、安全性和主动信息收集三个相互冲突的目标，难以协调这些目标在不同时间尺度上的矛盾。

Method: 提出分层、基于证书的控制架构：1) 引入信念控制李雅普诺夫函数将信息收集形式化为信念空间的李雅普诺夫收敛问题，通过强化学习学习；2) 开发信念控制屏障函数，利用保形预测提供有限时间范围内的概率安全保证；3) 控制综合简化为轻量级二次规划，支持高维非高斯信念表示。

Result: 在仿真和空间机器人平台上验证了实时性能，相比最先进的约束POMDP求解器，在安全性和任务成功率方面均有提升，即使对于维度>10^4的非高斯信念表示也能实时求解。

Conclusion: 通过分层架构将POMDP中的冲突目标解耦，结合基于证书的控制方法和保形预测，实现了实时、安全且高效的机器人决策，为不确定性下的机器人控制提供了新框架。

Abstract: Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) provide a principled framework for robot decision-making under uncertainty. Solving reach-avoid POMDPs, however, requires coordinating three distinct behaviors: goal reaching, safety, and active information gathering to reduce uncertainty. Existing online POMDP solvers attempt to address all three within a single belief tree search, but this unified approach struggles with the conflicting time scales inherent to these objectives. We propose a layered, certificate-based control architecture that operates directly in belief space, decoupling goal reaching, information gathering, and safety into modular components. We introduce Belief Control Lyapunov Functions (BCLFs) that formalize information gathering as a Lyapunov convergence problem in belief space, and show how they can be learned via reinforcement learning. For safety, we develop Belief Control Barrier Functions (BCBFs) that leverage conformal prediction to provide probabilistic safety guarantees over finite horizons. The resulting control synthesis reduces to lightweight quadratic programs solvable in real time, even for non-Gaussian belief representations with dimension $>10^4$. Experiments in simulation and on a space-robotics platform demonstrate real-time performance and improved safety and task success compared to state-of-the-art constrained POMDP solvers.

</details>


### [39] [Recover to Predict: Progressive Retrospective Learning for Variable-Length Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10597)
*Hao Zhou,Lu Qi,Jason Li,Jie Zhang,Yi Liu,Xu Yang,Mingyu Fan,Fei Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出渐进式回顾框架（PRF）解决自动驾驶轨迹预测中可变长度不完整观测问题，通过级联回顾单元逐步对齐特征，并采用滚动启动训练策略提高数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹预测方法通常在固定长度观测下优化预测精度，但真实驾驶场景常产生可变长度、不完整观测。直接将不完整观测特征映射到完整观测的一次性映射方法存在显著信息差距，难以学习短轨迹的准确表示。

Method: 提出渐进式回顾框架（PRF），通过级联的回顾单元逐步对齐不完整观测与完整观测特征。每个单元包含回顾蒸馏模块（RDM）和回顾预测模块（RPM）：RDM蒸馏特征，RPM使用蒸馏特征恢复先前时间步。同时提出滚动启动训练策略（RSTS）提高PRF训练的数据效率。PRF可与现有方法即插即用。

Result: 在Argoverse 2和Argoverse 1数据集上的大量实验证明了PRF的有效性。

Conclusion: PRF通过渐进式特征对齐机制有效解决了可变长度不完整观测下的轨迹预测问题，提高了预测准确性，且具有良好的兼容性和数据效率。

Abstract: Trajectory prediction is critical for autonomous driving, enabling safe and efficient planning in dense, dynamic traffic. Most existing methods optimize prediction accuracy under fixed-length observations. However, real-world driving often yields variable-length, incomplete observations, posing a challenge to these methods. A common strategy is to directly map features from incomplete observations to those from complete ones. This one-shot mapping, however, struggles to learn accurate representations for short trajectories due to significant information gaps. To address this issue, we propose a Progressive Retrospective Framework (PRF), which gradually aligns features from incomplete observations with those from complete ones via a cascade of retrospective units. Each unit consists of a Retrospective Distillation Module (RDM) and a Retrospective Prediction Module (RPM), where RDM distills features and RPM recovers previous timesteps using the distilled features. Moreover, we propose a Rolling-Start Training Strategy (RSTS) that enhances data efficiency during PRF training. PRF is plug-and-play with existing methods. Extensive experiments on datasets Argoverse 2 and Argoverse 1 demonstrate the effectiveness of PRF. Code is available at https://github.com/zhouhao94/PRF.

</details>


### [40] [AdaClearGrasp: Learning Adaptive Clearing for Zero-Shot Robust Dexterous Grasping in Densely Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10616)
*Zixuan Chen,Wenquan Zhang,Jing Fang,Ruiming Zeng,Zhixuan Xu,Yiwen Hou,Xinke Wang,Jieqi Shi,Jing Huo,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO
