TL;DR: AdaClearGrasp是一个闭环决策执行框架，用于在密集杂乱环境中进行自适应清理和零样本灵巧抓取，通过VLM进行高层决策，结合RL策略实现零样本泛化，并在Clutter-Bench基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集杂乱环境中，物理干扰、视觉遮挡和不稳定接触常导致直接灵巧抓取失败，而激进的分离策略可能危及安全。因此，让机器人能自适应地决定是清理周围物体还是直接抓取目标，对于鲁棒操作至关重要。

Method: 提出AdaClearGrasp闭环决策执行框架：1) 将操作建模为可控高层决策过程，决定直接抓取或先清理；2) 使用预训练视觉语言模型(VLM)解释视觉观察和语言任务描述，推理抓取干扰并生成高层规划骨架；3) 通过统一动作接口调用结构化原子技能；4) 为灵巧抓取训练基于相对手-物距离表示的强化学习策略，实现零样本泛化；5) 执行中通过视觉反馈监控结果并在失败时触发重规划，形成闭环校正机制。

Result: 1) 引入Clutter-Bench首个具有分级杂乱复杂度的仿真基准，包含7个目标物体和3个杂乱级别，共210个任务场景；2) 在3个物体和3个杂乱级别下进行仿真到真实实验(18个场景)；3) 结果表明AdaClearGrasp在密集杂乱环境中显著提高了抓取成功率。

Conclusion: AdaClearGrasp通过结合高层决策、视觉语言推理和零样本灵巧抓取，实现了在密集杂乱环境中的自适应清理和鲁棒抓取，为解决复杂环境中的操作问题提供了有效框架。

Abstract: In densely cluttered environments, physical interference, visual occlusions, and unstable contacts often cause direct dexterous grasping to fail, while aggressive singulation strategies may compromise safety. Enabling robots to adaptively decide whether to clear surrounding objects or directly grasp the target is therefore crucial for robust manipulation. We propose AdaClearGrasp, a closed-loop decision-execution framework for adaptive clearing and zero-shot dexterous grasping in densely cluttered environments. The framework formulates manipulation as a controllable high-level decision process that determines whether to directly grasp the target or first clear surrounding objects. A pretrained vision-language model (VLM) interprets visual observations and language task descriptions to reason about grasp interference and generate a high-level planning skeleton, which invokes structured atomic skills through a unified action interface. For dexterous grasping, we train a reinforcement learning policy with a relative hand-object distance representation, enabling zero-shot generalization across diverse object geometries and physical properties. During execution, visual feedback monitors outcomes and triggers replanning upon failures, forming a closed-loop correction mechanism. To evaluate language-conditioned dexterous grasping in clutter, we introduce Clutter-Bench, the first simulation benchmark with graded clutter complexity. It includes seven target objects across three clutter levels, yielding 210 task scenarios. We further perform sim-to-real experiments on three objects under three clutter levels (18 scenarios). Results demonstrate that AdaClearGrasp significantly improves grasp success rates in densely cluttered environments. For more videos and code, please visit our project website: https://chenzixuan99.github.io/adaclear-grasp.github.io/.

</details>


### [41] [Interleaving Scheduling and Motion Planning with Incremental Learning of Symbolic Space-Time Motion Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10651)
*Elisa Tosello,Arthur Bit-Monnot,Davide Lusuardi,Alessandro Valentini,Andrea Micheli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合调度与运动规划的新框架，通过增量学习循环解决多物体导航中的时空约束问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动化仓库等现实场景中，任务通常是预定义的，主要挑战在于如何在资源、时间和运动约束下安全高效地执行这些任务，这需要同时考虑调度（何时执行）和运动规划（如何执行）

Method: 提出一个新颖的解决方案框架，将现成的调度器和运动规划器交织在一个增量学习循环中。调度器生成候选计划，运动规划器检查可行性并返回符号反馈（空间冲突和时间调整），指导调度器找到运动可行的解决方案

Result: 在增强了运动任务的物流和作业车间调度基准测试中验证了该框架，使用最先进的调度器和基于采样的运动规划器，结果表明该框架在复杂时空约束下能有效生成有效计划，特别是在需要同步运动的关键场景中

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了调度与运动规划的集成问题，为多物体在共享工作空间中的导航提供了有效的解决方案，能够处理复杂的时空约束并生成可行的执行计划

Abstract: Task and Motion Planning combines high-level task sequencing (what to do) with low-level motion planning (how to do it) to generate feasible, collision-free execution plans. However, in many real-world domains, such as automated warehouses, tasks are predefined, shifting the challenge to if, when, and how to execute them safely and efficiently under resource, time and motion constraints. In this paper, we formalize this as the Scheduling and Motion Planning problem for multi-object navigation in shared workspaces. We propose a novel solution framework that interleaves off-the-shelf schedulers and motion planners in an incremental learning loop. The scheduler generates candidate plans, while the motion planner checks feasibility and returns symbolic feedback, i.e., spatial conflicts and timing adjustments, to guide the scheduler towards motion-feasible solutions. We validate our proposal on logistics and job-shop scheduling benchmarks augmented with motion tasks, using state-of-the-art schedulers and sampling-based motion planners. Our results show the effectiveness of our framework in generating valid plans under complex temporal and spatial constraints, where synchronized motion is critical.

</details>


### [42] [STM32-Based Smart Waste Bin for Hygienic Disposal Using Embedded Sensing and Automated Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10660)
*Mohammed Aman Bhuiyan,Aritra Islam Saswato,Md. Misbah Khan,Anish Paul,Ahmed Faizul Haque Dhrubo,Mohammad Abdul Qayum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于STM32微控制器、超声波传感器和舵机的自动感应垃圾桶设计与实现，提供非接触式垃圾处理方案


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共场所和私人环境对卫生、非接触式解决方案的需求日益增长，推动了日常应用的自动化系统开发

Method: 使用STM32微控制器控制超声波传感器检测用户存在，通过舵机自动打开垃圾桶盖；额外超声波传感器监测内部垃圾量，OLED显示屏提供实时系统状态反馈

Result: 实验评估显示系统具有快速响应时间、稳定的传感性能和流畅的机械操作，可有效部署于家庭、教育机构、医院和公共设施

Conclusion: 该系统提供了一种低成本、可靠且易于部署的非接触式垃圾处理解决方案，能够改善卫生状况和用户便利性

Abstract: The increasing demand for hygienic and contactless solutions in public and private environments has encouraged the development of automated systems for everyday applications. This paper presents the design and implementation of a motion- sensing automatic waste bin using an STM32 microcontroller, ultrasonic sensors, and a servo motor. The system detects user presence through ultrasonic sensing and automatically opens the bin lid using a servo motor controlled by the microcontroller. An additional ultrasonic sensor is used to monitor the internal waste level of the bin, while an OLED display provides real- time feedback regarding system status. The proposed system offers a low-cost, reliable, and easily deployable solution for touch-free waste disposal. Experimental evaluation demonstrates fast response time, stable sensing performance, and smooth mechanical operation. The system can be effectively deployed in homes, educational institutions, hospitals, and public facilities to improve hygiene and user convenience.

</details>


### [43] [Dynamic Modeling and Attitude Control of a Reaction-Wheel-Based Low-Gravity Bipedal Hopper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10670)
*Shriram Hari,M Venkata Sai Nikhil,R Prasanth Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于低重力环境（如月球）的欠驱动双足跳跃机器人，通过内部反作用飞轮在弹道飞行阶段调节身体姿态，显著减少空中姿态偏差并确保稳定着陆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球和近地小行星等低重力天体由于接触力减小和空中停留时间延长，对运动控制提出了独特挑战。跳跃运动虽然能效高，但容易因不对称推力产生和地形不平整导致空中姿态不稳定。需要一种能够在弹道飞行阶段有效稳定姿态的解决方案。

Method: 设计了一个欠驱动双足跳跃机器人，采用内部反作用飞轮在弹道飞行阶段调节身体姿态。将系统建模为陀螺体，分析躯干旋转与反作用飞轮动量之间的动态耦合。运动周期包括三个阶段：腿部驱动的推进跳跃、通过主动动量交换控制器实现的空中姿态稳定、以及减震着陆。开发了降阶模型来捕捉躯干旋转与反作用飞轮动力学的关键耦合。在MuJoCo仿真环境中使用月球重力条件（g=1.625 m/s²）评估该框架。

Result: 激活反作用飞轮控制器后，峰值空中角度偏差减少了65%以上，着陆时的姿态误差被限制在3.5度以内。同时，每个跳跃周期的执行器饱和程度降低，确保了足够的控制能力。该方法显著减轻了飞行中的姿态偏移，实现了稳定的直立着陆。

Conclusion: 提出的欠驱动双足跳跃机器人通过内部反作用飞轮有效解决了低重力环境中跳跃运动的姿态稳定问题。该方法为不规则外星地形上的运动提供了一个实用且控制效率高的解决方案，显著改善了空中姿态控制和着陆稳定性。

Abstract: Planetary bodies characterized by low gravitational acceleration, such as the Moon and near-Earth asteroids, impose unique locomotion constraints due to diminished contact forces and extended airborne intervals. Among traversal strategies, hopping locomotion offers high energy efficiency but is prone to mid-flight attitude instability caused by asymmetric thrust generation and uneven terrain interactions. This paper presents an underactuated bipedal hopping robot that employs an internal reaction wheel to regulate body posture during the ballistic flight phase. The system is modeled as a gyrostat, enabling analysis of the dynamic coupling between torso rotation and reaction wheel momentum. The locomotion cycle comprises three phases: a leg-driven propulsive jump, mid-air attitude stabilization via an active momentum exchange controller, and a shock-absorbing landing. A reduced-order model is developed to capture the critical coupling between torso rotation and reaction wheel dynamics. The proposed framework is evaluated in MuJoCo-based simulations under lunar gravity conditions (g = 1.625 m/s^2). Results demonstrate that activation of the reaction wheel controller reduces peak mid-air angular deviation by more than 65% and constrains landing attitude error to within 3.5 degrees at touchdown. Additionally, actuator saturation per hop cycle is reduced, ensuring sufficient control authority. Overall, the approach significantly mitigates in-flight attitude excursions and enables consistent upright landings, providing a practical and control-efficient solution for locomotion on irregular extraterrestrial terrains.

</details>


### [44] [OnFly: Onboard Zero-Shot Aerial Vision-Language Navigation toward Safety and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10682)
*Guiyong Zheng,Yueting Ban,Mingjie Zhang,Juepeng Zheng,Boyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OnFly：一种完全机载的实时零样本航空视觉语言导航框架，采用共享感知双智能体架构，通过解耦高频目标生成与低频进度监控来稳定决策，使用混合关键帧-近期帧记忆保持全局轨迹上下文，结合语义-几何验证器和滚动时域规划器提升安全性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本航空视觉语言导航方法存在单流视觉语言模型决策不稳定、长时程进度监控不可靠、安全性与效率难以权衡等问题，需要开发能够在复杂3D环境中实时执行自然语言指令的完全机载解决方案。

Method: 1. 共享感知双智能体架构：解耦高频目标生成与低频进度监控；2. 混合关键帧-近期帧记忆：保持全局轨迹上下文和KV缓存前缀稳定性；3. 语义-几何验证器：利用VLM特征和深度线索优化目标预测；4. 滚动时域规划器：在几何安全约束下生成优化无碰撞轨迹。

Result: 在仿真环境中，任务成功率从最强基线的26.4%提升至67.8%；完全机载的真实世界飞行验证了实时部署的可行性。

Conclusion: OnFly通过创新的架构设计和算法组件，有效解决了零样本航空视觉语言导航中的关键挑战，实现了稳定决策、可靠长时程监控以及安全与效率的平衡，为完全机载实时导航系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Aerial vision-language navigation (AVLN) enables UAVs to follow natural-language instructions in complex 3D environments. However, existing zero-shot AVLN methods often suffer from unstable single-stream Vision-Language Model decision-making, unreliable long-horizon progress monitoring, and a trade-off between safety and efficiency. We propose OnFly, a fully onboard, real-time framework for zero-shot AVLN. OnFly adopts a shared-perception dual-agent architecture that decouples high-frequency target generation from low-frequency progress monitoring, thereby stabilizing decision-making. It further employs a hybrid keyframe-recent-frame memory to preserve global trajectory context while maintaining KV-cache prefix stability, enabling reliable long-horizon monitoring with termination and recovery signals. In addition, a semantic-geometric verifier refines VLM-predicted targets for instruction consistency and geometric safety using VLM features and depth cues, while a receding-horizon planner generates optimized collision-free trajectories under geometric safety constraints, improving both safety and efficiency. In simulation, OnFly improves task success from 26.4% to 67.8%, compared with the strongest state-of-the-art baseline, while fully onboard real-world flights validate its feasibility for real-time deployment. The code will be released at https://github.com/Robotics-STAR-Lab/OnFly

</details>


### [45] [MapGCLR: Geospatial Contrastive Learning of Representations for Online Vectorized HD Map Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10688)
*Jonas Merkert,Alexander Blumberg,Jan-Hendrik Pauls,Christoph Stiller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过引入地理空间一致性对比损失和多轨迹重叠分析，提出了一种半监督的在线高精地图构建方法，在减少标注需求的同时提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离线高精地图构建成本高昂，在线地图构建需要大量标注数据。为减少标注需求，探索自监督学习方法，通过增强鸟瞰图特征网格的地理空间一致性来提升表示质量。

Method: 1. 在向量化在线高精地图构建模型中，通过对比损失函数强制重叠鸟瞰图特征网格之间的地理空间一致性；2. 分析数据集中的轨迹重叠，生成符合多轨迹要求的数据子集；3. 采用半监督方法：在少量单轨迹标注数据上进行监督训练，在大量多轨迹无标注数据上进行自监督训练。

Result: 方法在各方面优于监督基线：1. 在向量化地图感知性能上定量提升；2. 在鸟瞰图特征空间的主成分分析可视化中，分割质量得到定性改善。

Conclusion: 通过地理空间一致性对比学习和多轨迹数据利用，实现了有效的半监督在线高精地图构建，在减少标注需求的同时提升了模型性能，为可扩展的自动驾驶地图构建提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles rely on map information to understand the world around them. However, the creation and maintenance of offline high-definition (HD) maps remains costly. A more scalable alternative lies in online HD map construction, which only requires map annotations at training time. To further reduce the need for annotating vast training labels, self-supervised training provides an alternative. This work focuses on improving the latent birds-eye-view (BEV) feature grid representation within a vectorized online HD map construction model by enforcing geospatial consistency between overlapping BEV feature grids as part of a contrastive loss function. To ensure geospatial overlap for contrastive pairs, we introduce an approach to analyze the overlap between traversals within a given dataset and generate subsidiary dataset splits following adjustable multi-traversal requirements. We train the same model supervised using a reduced set of single-traversal labeled data and self-supervised on a broader unlabeled set of data following our multi-traversal requirements, effectively implementing a semi-supervised approach. Our approach outperforms the supervised baseline across the board, both quantitatively in terms of the downstream tasks vectorized map perception performance and qualitatively in terms of segmentation in the principal component analysis (PCA) visualization of the BEV feature space.

</details>


### [46] [Parallel-in-Time Nonlinear Optimal Control via GPU-native Sequential Convex Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10711)
*Yilin Zou,Zhong Zhang,Fanghua Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出完全基于GPU的轨迹优化框架，通过并行化算法实现实时非线性约束自主系统优化，相比CPU基准获得4倍吞吐量提升和51%能耗降低


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时轨迹优化方法主要依赖CPU顺序求解器，受限于全局稀疏线性代数或动态规划算法的串行特性，无法充分利用GPU等大规模并行计算架构

Method: 结合序列凸规划与基于共识的交替方向乘子法，采用时间分割策略将优化时域解耦为独立的节点子问题，在GPU上大规模并行执行

Result: 在四旋翼敏捷飞行和火星动力下降任务中验证，相比12核CPU基准获得持续4倍吞吐量加速和51%能耗降低，GPU利用率超过96%，规划速率超过100Hz

Conclusion: 该GPU原生框架消除了昂贵的内存传输和大规模稀疏分解，自然扩展到多轨迹优化，并可扩展至鲁棒模型预测控制，实现可扩展的安全自主系统

Abstract: Real-time trajectory optimization for nonlinear constrained autonomous systems is critical and typically performed by CPU-based sequential solvers. Specifically, reliance on global sparse linear algebra or the serial nature of dynamic programming algorithms restricts the utilization of massively parallel computing architectures like GPUs. To bridge this gap, we introduce a fully GPU-native trajectory optimization framework that combines sequential convex programming with a consensus-based alternating direction method of multipliers. By applying a temporal splitting strategy, our algorithm decouples the optimization horizon into independent, per-node subproblems that execute massively in parallel. The entire process runs fully on the GPU, eliminating costly memory transfers and large-scale sparse factorizations. This architecture naturally scales to multi-trajectory optimization. We validate the solver on a quadrotor agile flight task and a Mars powered descent problem using an on-board edge computing platform. Benchmarks reveal a sustained 4x throughput speedup and a 51% reduction in energy consumption over a heavily optimized 12-core CPU baseline. Crucially, the framework saturates the hardware, maintaining over 96% active GPU utilization to achieve planning rates exceeding 100 Hz. Furthermore, we demonstrate the solver's extensibility to robust Model Predictive Control by jointly optimizing dynamically coupled scenarios under stochastic disturbances, enabling scalable and safe autonomy.

</details>


### [47] [FutureVLA: Joint Visuomotor Prediction for Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10712)
*Xiaoxu Xu,Hao Li,Jinhui Ye,Yilun Chen,Jia Zeng,Xinyi Chen,Linning Xu,Dahua Lin,Weixin Li,Jiangmiao Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FutureVLA提出了一种联合视觉-运动预测架构，通过解耦视觉和运动信息，然后联合编码广义物理先验，以改进视觉-语言-动作模型的未来预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型在联合建模视觉感知与运动执行方面存在困难：显式方法关注任务无关的视觉细节，隐式方法依赖稀疏帧对破坏时间连续性，且过度依赖视觉重建导致静态场景上下文与动态动作意图纠缠。

Method: 提出FutureVLA框架，包含联合视觉-运动预测架构。在预训练阶段使用异构操作数据集，引入联合视觉-运动门控机制，结构上分离视觉状态保持与时间动作建模。在后训练阶段采用潜在嵌入对齐策略，使下游VLA模型能够内化时间先验而不改变推理架构。

Result: 大量实验表明，FutureVLA能够持续改进各种VLA框架的性能，证明了其联合视觉-运动预测架构的有效性。

Conclusion: 有效的联合视觉-运动预测建模需要时间连续性和视觉条件监督解耦。FutureVLA通过解耦视觉和运动信息并联合编码物理先验，成功实现了这一目标，为智能体具身智能的未来预测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Predictive foresight is important to intelligent embodied agents. Since the motor execution of a robot is intrinsically constrained by its visual perception of environmental geometry, effectively anticipating the future requires capturing this tightly coupled visuomotor interplay. While recent vision-language-action models attempt to incorporate future guidance, they struggle with this joint modeling. Existing explicit methods divert capacity to task-irrelevant visual details, whereas implicit methods relying on sparse frame pairs disrupt temporal continuity. By heavily relying on visual reconstruction, these methods become visually dominated, entangling static scene context with dynamic action intent. We argue that effective joint visuomotor predictive modeling requires both temporal continuity and visually-conditioned supervision decoupling. To this end, we propose FutureVLA, featuring a novel Joint Visuomotor Predictive Architecture. FutureVLA is designed to extract joint visuomotor embeddings by first decoupling visual and motor information, and then jointly encoding generalized physical priors. Specifically, in the pretraining stage, we leverage heterogeneous manipulation datasets and introduce a Joint Visuomotor Gating mechanism to structurally separate visual state preservation from temporal action modeling. It allows the motor stream to focus on continuous physical dynamics while explicitly querying visual tokens for environmental constraints, yielding highly generalizable joint visuomotor embeddings. Subsequently, in the post-training stage, we employ a latent embeddings alignment strategy, enabling diverse downstream VLA models to internalize these temporal priors without modifying their inference architectures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FutureVLA consistently improves VLA frameworks.

</details>


### [48] [MAVEN: A Meta-Reinforcement Learning Framework for Varying-Dynamics Expertise in Agile Quadrotor Maneuvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10714)
*Jin Zhou,Dongcheng Cao,Xian Wang,Shuo Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAVEN：一种元强化学习框架，使单个策略能够在广泛的四旋翼动力学变化中实现鲁棒的端到端导航，通过预测性上下文编码器推断系统动力学，并在质量和单旋翼推力损失变化下实现零样本仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习训练的策略通常无法在显著动态变化中泛化，缺乏适应性。四旋翼导航需要在不同动力学条件下保持鲁棒性，特别是在质量和推力损失等实际变化中。

Method: 提出MAVEN元强化学习框架，包含新颖的预测性上下文编码器，从交互历史中学习推断系统动力学的潜在表示。使用GPU向量化模拟器在数千个并行环境中分布任务，克服元强化学习训练时间长的问题。

Result: 在敏捷航点遍历任务中验证方法，处理四旋翼质量大幅变化（高达66.7%）和严重单旋翼推力损失（高达70%）。实现零样本仿真到现实迁移，在真实世界中执行高速机动，收敛时间少于1小时。

Conclusion: MAVEN实现了卓越的适应性和敏捷性，单个策略能够在广泛的四旋翼动力学变化中实现鲁棒的端到端导航，解决了标准强化学习策略泛化能力不足的问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for achieving online agile navigation with quadrotors. Despite this success, policies trained via standard RL typically fail to generalize across significant dynamic variations, exhibiting a critical lack of adaptability. This work introduces MAVEN, a meta-RL framework that enables a single policy to achieve robust end-to-end navigation across a wide range of quadrotor dynamics. Our approach features a novel predictive context encoder, which learns to infer a latent representation of the system dynamics from interaction history. We demonstrate our method in agile waypoint traversal tasks under two challenging scenarios: large variations in quadrotor mass and severe single-rotor thrust loss. We leverage a GPU-vectorized simulator to distribute tasks across thousands of parallel environments, overcoming the long training times of meta-RL to converge in less than an hour. Through extensive experiments in both simulation and the real world, we validate that MAVEN achieves superior adaptation and agility. The policy successfully executes zero-shot sim-to-real transfer, demonstrating robust online adaptation by performing high-speed maneuvers despite mass variations of up to 66.7% and single-rotor thrust losses as severe as 70%.

</details>


### [49] [ASTER: Attitude-aware Suspended-payload Quadrotor Traversal via Efficient Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10715)
*Dongcheng Cao,Jin Zhou,Shuo Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ASTER强化学习框架，首次实现四旋翼缆绳悬挂系统的自主倒飞，通过混合动力学状态播种解决奖励稀疏问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼缆绳悬挂系统的敏捷机动受到其非光滑混合动力学的严重限制。虽然无模型强化学习避免了复杂模型的显式微分，但由于严格姿态要求下的极端奖励稀疏性，实现姿态约束或倒飞仍然是一个开放挑战。

Method: 提出ASTER鲁棒强化学习框架，包含混合动力学信息状态播种（HDSS）初始化策略。HDSS通过物理一致的动力学逆解在拉紧和松弛缆绳阶段反向传播目标配置，使策略能够发现标准探索无法达到的激进机动。

Result: 广泛的仿真和真实世界实验展示了卓越的敏捷性、精确的姿态对齐，以及在复杂轨迹上的鲁棒零样本仿真到现实迁移。实现了首个成功的缆绳悬挂系统自主倒飞。

Conclusion: ASTER框架通过混合动力学信息状态播种成功解决了四旋翼缆绳悬挂系统的奖励稀疏问题，实现了首个自主倒飞，展示了在复杂混合动力学系统中强化学习的有效性。

Abstract: Agile maneuvering of the quadrotor cable-suspended system is significantly hindered by its non-smooth hybrid dynamics. While model-free Reinforcement Learning (RL) circumvents explicit differentiation of complex models, achieving attitude-constrained or inverted flight remains an open challenge due to the extreme reward sparsity under strict orientation requirements. This paper presents ASTER, a robust RL framework that achieves, to our knowledge, the first successful autonomous inverted flight for the cable-suspended system. We propose hybrid-dynamics-informed state seeding (HDSS), an initialization strategy that back-propagates target configurations through physics-consistent kinematic inversions across both taut and slack cable phases. HDSS enables the policy to discover aggressive maneuvers that are unreachable via standard exploration. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate remarkable agility, precise attitude alignment, and robust zero-shot sim-to-real transfer across complex trajectories.

</details>


### [50] [Semantic Landmark Particle Filter for Robot Localisation in Vineyards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10847)
*Rajitha de Silva,Jonathan Cox,James R. Heselden,Marija Popović,Cesar Cadena,Riccardo Polvara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SLPF方法，通过整合树干和杆状地标检测与2D LiDAR，解决葡萄园中行级感知混淆问题，提高定位精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 葡萄园中的定位面临行级感知混淆的挑战：平行的作物行产生几乎相同的LiDAR观测，导致仅基于几何和视觉的SLAM系统在转向错误通道，特别是在地头转弯时

Method: 提出语义地标粒子滤波(SLPF)，将树干和杆状地标检测与2D LiDAR整合到概率定位框架中。检测到的树干被转换为语义墙，形成结构化的行边界嵌入测量模型，以改善相邻行之间的区分。GNSS作为轻量级先验，在语义观测稀疏时稳定定位

Result: 在10行葡萄园的现场实验中，相比仅几何方法(AMCL)、视觉方法(RTAB-Map)和GNSS基线，SLPF表现一致改进。与AMCL相比，SLPF在两个遍历方向上分别减少绝对姿态误差22%和65%；相比NoisyGNSS基线，APE分别减少65%和61%。行正确率从0.67提高到0.73，平均横向误差从1.40m减少到1.26m

Conclusion: 在测量模型中嵌入行级结构语义能够在高度重复的户外农业环境中实现鲁棒定位

Abstract: Reliable localisation in vineyards is hindered by row-level perceptual aliasing: parallel crop rows produce nearly identical LiDAR observations, causing geometry-only and vision-based SLAM systems to converge towards incorrect corridors, particularly during headland transitions. We present a Semantic Landmark Particle Filter (SLPF) that integrates trunk and pole landmark detections with 2D LiDAR within a probabilistic localisation framework. Detected trunks are converted into semantic walls, forming structural row boundaries embedded in the measurement model to improve discrimination between adjacent rows. GNSS is incorporated as a lightweight prior that stabilises localisation when semantic observations are sparse.
  Field experiments in a 10-row vineyard demonstrate consistent improvements over geometry-only (AMCL), vision-based (RTAB-Map), and GNSS baselines. Compared to AMCL, SLPF reduces Absolute Pose Error by 22% and 65% across two traversal directions; relative to a NoisyGNSS baseline, APE decreases by 65% and 61%. Row correctness improves from 0.67 to 0.73, while mean cross-track error decreases from 1.40 m to 1.26 m. These results show that embedding row-level structural semantics within the measurement model enables robust localisation in highly repetitive outdoor agricultural environments.

</details>


### [51] [GRACE: A Unified 2D Multi-Robot Path Planning Simulator & Benchmark for Grid, Roadmap, And Continuous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10858)
*Chuanlong Zang,Anna Mannucci,Isabelle Barz,Philipp Schillinger,Florian Lier,Wolfgang Hönig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRACE是一个统一的多智能体路径规划仿真+基准平台，通过在网格、路网和连续空间三个抽象层次上实例化相同任务，实现跨表示方法的可比较评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体路径规划工具要么在简化假设下扩展（网格、同质智能体），要么提供高保真度但缺乏可比性。需要透明、可复现的平台来比较不同建模选择。

Method: 开发GRACE统一2D仿真+基准平台，通过显式、可复现的操作符在网格、路网和连续空间三个抽象层次上实例化相同任务，采用共同的评估协议。

Result: 在公共地图和代表性规划器上的实证结果实现了共享实例集上的可比较评估，量化了表示-保真度权衡（连续空间MRMP解决高保真度实例但速度较慢，网格/路网规划器扩展性更好）。

Conclusion: GRACE通过整合表示、执行和评估，使跨表示研究更具可比性，为推进多机器人规划研究及其实际应用提供了工具。

Abstract: Advancing Multi-Agent Pathfinding (MAPF) and Multi-Robot Motion Planning (MRMP) requires platforms that enable transparent, reproducible comparisons across modeling choices. Existing tools either scale under simplifying assumptions (grids, homogeneous agents) or offer higher fidelity with less comparable instrumentation. We present GRACE, a unified 2D simulator+benchmark that instantiates the same task at multiple abstraction levels (grid, roadmap, continuous) via explicit, reproducible operators and a common evaluation protocol. Our empirical results on public maps and representative planners enable commensurate comparisons on a shared instance set. Furthermore, we quantify the expected representation-fidelity trade-offs (MRMP solves instances at higher fidelity but lower speed, while grid/roadmap planners scale farther). By consolidating representation, execution, and evaluation, GRACE thereby aims to make cross-representation studies more comparable and provides a means to advance multi-robot planning research and its translation to practice.

</details>


### [52] [FG-CLTP: Fine-Grained Contrastive Language Tactile Pretraining for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10871)
*Wenxuan Ma,Chaofan Zhang,Yinghao Cai,Guocai Yao,Shaowei Cui,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FG-CLTP框架，通过对比学习实现细粒度触觉-语言预训练，引入包含多维接触状态的触觉3D点云-语言配对数据集，显著提升接触状态分类和回归精度，并构建3D触觉-语言-动作架构用于接触密集型操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉感知集成到视觉-语言-动作模型的方法主要依赖定性描述符（如纹理），忽略了力大小、接触几何、主轴方向等定量接触状态，而这些对于细粒度操作至关重要。需要弥合这一差距以实现更精细的机器人感知和操作。

Method: 提出FG-CLTP（细粒度对比语言触觉预训练）框架：1）构建包含10万+触觉3D点云-语言配对的新数据集，从传感器视角捕获多维接触状态；2）实现离散化数值标记化机制，实现定量-语义对齐，将显式物理度量注入多模态特征空间；3）基于细粒度表示开发3D触觉-语言-动作架构，采用流匹配策略驱动多模态推理和控制。

Result: FG-CLTP模型达到95.9%的分类准确率，相比最先进方法将回归误差（MAE）降低52.6%。3D点云表示建立了传感器无关的基础，模拟到真实的差距仅为3.5%。在接触密集型操作任务中，该框架显著优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: FG-CLTP框架通过定量-语义对齐实现了细粒度触觉表示，为触觉-语言-动作模型提供了稳健且可泛化的基础，显著提升了接触状态感知精度和操作性能，为机器人精细操作开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Recent advancements in integrating tactile sensing into vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated transformative potential for robotic perception. However, existing tactile representations predominantly rely on qualitative descriptors (e.g., texture), neglecting quantitative contact states such as force magnitude, contact geometry, and principal axis orientation, which are indispensable for fine-grained manipulation. To bridge this gap, we propose FG-CLTP, a fine-grained contrastive language tactile pretraining framework. We first introduce a novel dataset comprising over 100k tactile 3D point cloud-language pairs that explicitly capture multidimensional contact states from the sensor's perspective. We then implement a discretized numerical tokenization mechanism to achieve quantitative-semantic alignment, effectively injecting explicit physical metrics into the multimodal feature space. The proposed FG-CLTP model yields a 95.9% classification accuracy and reduces the regression error (MAE) by 52.6% compared to state-of-the-art methods. Furthermore, the integration of 3D point cloud representations establishes a sensor-agnostic foundation with a minimal sim-to-real gap of 3.5%. Building upon this fine-grained representation, we develop a 3D tactile-language-action (3D-TLA) architecture driven by a flow matching policy to enable multimodal reasoning and control. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms strong baselines in contact-rich manipulation tasks, providing a robust and generalizable foundation for tactile-language-action models.

</details>


### [53] [A gripper for flap separation and opening of sealed bags](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10890)
*Sergi Foix,Jaume Oriol,Carme Torras,Júlia Borràs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一种新型夹持器设计，用于可靠分离和抓取薄柔性层（如无菌袋封口），以自动化医院中重复性高的袋装物品开封任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医院临床环境中需要大量开封无菌平袋用于手术室准备，护士每班需手动打开多达240个袋子，这是重复性高、体力消耗大的任务，与肌肉骨骼损伤相关。现有商用夹持器难以可靠分离和抓取薄柔性层。

Method: 设计了一种结合主动齿状滚轮指尖和柔性手指的新型夹持器，利用环境约束来可靠抓取薄柔性封口。通过两个夹持器副本同时抓取两个封口，系统能够承受打开密封所需的力量。

Result: 实验表明，该夹持器能够可靠抓取和分离密封袋封口以及其他医院中的薄层材料，影响性能的最敏感变量是施加的法向力。当两个夹持器同时抓取两个封口时，系统能够承受打开密封所需的力量。

Conclusion: 这是首批展示机器人辅助自动化这种重复性、低价值但关键的医院任务的成果之一，为解决临床环境中的薄柔性层分离挑战提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Separating thin, flexible layers that must be individually grasped is a common but challenging manipulation primitive for most off-the-shelf grippers. A prominent example arises in clinical settings: the opening of sterile flat pouches for the preparation of the operating room, where the first step is to separate and grasp the flaps. We present a novel gripper design and opening strategy that enables reliable flap separation and robust seal opening. This capability addresses a high-volume repetitive hospital procedure in which nurses manually open up to 240 bags per shift, a physically demanding task linked to musculoskeletal injuries. Our design combines an active dented-roller fingertip with compliant fingers that exploit environmental constraints to robustly grasp thin flexible flaps. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed gripper reliably grasps and separates sealed bag flaps and other thin-layered materials from the hospital, the most sensitive variable affecting performance being the normal force applied. When two copies of the gripper grasp both flaps, the system withstands the forces needed to open the seals robustly. To our knowledge, this is one of the first demonstrations of robotic assistance to automate this repetitive, low-value, but critical hospital task.

</details>


### [54] [Contact Coverage-Guided Exploration for General-Purpose Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10971)
*Zixuan Liu,Ruoyi Qiao,Chenrui Tie,Xuanwei Liu,Yunfan Lou,Chongkai Gao,Zhixuan Xu,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CCGE是一种用于灵巧操作任务的通用探索方法，通过接触覆盖引导探索，鼓励发现多样化的接触模式，显著提高训练效率和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在奖励结构明确的领域（如Atari游戏和运动控制）取得了显著成功，但灵巧操作缺乏通用奖励公式，通常依赖任务特定、手工设计的先验来指导手-物体交互。需要一种通用的探索方法来发现多样化的接触模式。

Method: CCGE将接触状态表示为物体表面点与预定义手部关键点之间的交集，鼓励灵巧手发现新颖的接触模式。它维护一个基于离散化物体状态的接触计数器，通过两种互补方式利用：1）基于计数的接触覆盖奖励，促进探索新颖接触模式；2）基于能量的到达奖励，引导智能体探索未充分探索的接触区域。

Result: 在多种灵巧操作任务（包括杂乱物体分离、受限物体抓取、手中重定向和双手操作）上的实验结果表明，CCGE相比现有探索方法显著提高了训练效率和成功率，且学习到的接触模式能够鲁棒地迁移到真实机器人系统。

Conclusion: CCGE是一种有效的通用探索方法，通过接触覆盖引导探索灵巧操作任务，能够发现多样化的接触模式并实现高效学习，具有良好的迁移性能。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement learning (DRL) has achieved remarkable success in domains with well-defined reward structures, such as Atari games and locomotion. In contrast, dexterous manipulation lacks general-purpose reward formulations and typically depends on task-specific, handcrafted priors to guide hand-object interactions. We propose Contact Coverage-Guided Exploration (CCGE), a general exploration method designed for general-purpose dexterous manipulation tasks. CCGE represents contact state as the intersection between object surface points and predefined hand keypoints, encouraging dexterous hands to discover diverse and novel contact patterns, namely which fingers contact which object regions. It maintains a contact counter conditioned on discretized object states obtained via learned hash codes, capturing how frequently each finger interacts with different object regions. This counter is leveraged in two complementary ways: (1) to assign a count-based contact coverage reward that promotes exploration of novel contact patterns, and (2) an energy-based reaching reward that guides the agent toward under-explored contact regions. We evaluate CCGE on a diverse set of dexterous manipulation tasks, including cluttered object singulation, constrained object retrieval, in-hand reorientation, and bimanual manipulation. Experimental results show that CCGE substantially improves training efficiency and success rates over existing exploration methods, and that the contact patterns learned with CCGE transfer robustly to real-world robotic systems. Project page is https://contact-coverage-guided-exploration.github.io.

</details>


### [55] [PPGuide: Steering Diffusion Policies with Performance Predictive Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10980)
*Zixing Wang,Devesh K. Jha,Ahmed H. Qureshi,Diego Romeres*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PPGuide：基于分类器的轻量级框架，通过性能预测引导预训练扩散策略在推理时避开失败模式，提升机器人操作任务的鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在机器人操作任务中表现出色，但生成的动作序列误差会随时间累积导致失败。现有方法通过增加专家演示或学习预测世界模型来缓解，但计算成本高。需要一种轻量级方法来在推理时引导策略避开失败模式。

Method: 提出性能预测引导（PPGuide）框架：1）使用基于注意力的多示例学习自动识别策略rollout中与成功/失败相关的观测-动作片段；2）基于这些自标注数据训练性能预测器；3）推理时，预测器提供实时梯度引导策略生成更鲁棒的动作。

Result: 在Robomimic和MimicGen基准的多样化任务上验证PPGuide，均表现出性能的稳定提升。

Conclusion: PPGuide是一种轻量级、自监督的框架，能够有效引导预训练扩散策略在推理时避开失败模式，提升机器人操作任务的鲁棒性和成功率，无需昂贵的专家演示或世界模型学习。

Abstract: Diffusion policies have shown to be very efficient at learning complex, multi-modal behaviors for robotic manipulation. However, errors in generated action sequences can compound over time which can potentially lead to failure. Some approaches mitigate this by augmenting datasets with expert demonstrations or learning predictive world models which might be computationally expensive. We introduce Performance Predictive Guidance (PPGuide), a lightweight, classifier-based framework that steers a pre-trained diffusion policy away from failure modes at inference time. PPGuide makes use of a novel self-supervised process: it uses attention-based multiple instance learning to automatically estimate which observation-action chunks from the policy's rollouts are relevant to success or failure. We then train a performance predictor on this self-labeled data. During inference, this predictor provides a real-time gradient to guide the policy toward more robust actions. We validated our proposed PPGuide across a diverse set of tasks from the Robomimic and MimicGen benchmarks, demonstrating consistent improvements in performance.

</details>

Abstract: We propose a novel indicator function for reconstructing acoustic sources from multi-frequency near-field measurements. The theoretical basis is established by a formula relating the scattered field to the source function through the Radon transform. Such a representation enables us to recover the source function directly. The efficiency and robustness of the novel indicator function are verified by several numerical examples.

*Dario Benedetti,Ilya Chevyrev,Razvan Gurau*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提供了两种新证明，表明标量欧几里得量子场论的路径积分与随机量子化方法是等价的。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明路径积分与随机量子化这两种量子场论基本表述方法之间的等价性，这是量子场论基础理论中的重要问题。

Method: 使用基于森林的泰勒插值方法，借鉴构造性场论的技术。第一种证明在费曼展开的单个项层面工作，森林表现为费曼图中的生成森林；第二种证明在路径积分层面工作，避免了费曼微扰级数的完全展开。

Result: 成功证明了通用标量欧几里得量子场论中路径积分与随机量子化的等价性，提供了两种不同层面的证明方法。

Conclusion: 通过基于森林的泰勒插值技术，为路径积分与随机量子化的等价性提供了两种新颖且严格的证明，深化了对量子场论不同表述之间关系的理解。

Abstract: We give two novel proofs that the path integral and stochastic quantizations of generic scalar Euclidean quantum field theories are equivalent. Our proofs rely on Taylor interpolations indexed by forests, in the fashion of constructive field theory. The first proof works at the level of individual terms in the Feynman expansion, with the forests appearing as spanning forests in Feynman graphs. The second one works at the level of the path integral and avoids the full expansion of the Feynman perturbation series.

</details>

*Michael Roop*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想磁流体动力学方程在湍流、天体物理和等离子体物理研究中具有重要作用，它们具有显著的几何结构和对称性。现有的Zeitlin模型仅适用于平面环面和二维球面上的MHD，需要将其扩展到三维情况，特别是轴对称三维流，以保持与底层几何结构的一致性。

Method: 推导三维球面上轴对称磁流体动力学的约化模型，给出其哈密顿表述。将有限维Zeitlin矩阵模型从2D扩展到轴对称3D磁化流体流动，建立与底层李-泊松结构兼容的离散模型。

Result: 获得了三维球面上轴对称磁流体动力学的约化模型及其哈密顿表述。成功将Zeitlin矩阵模型扩展到轴对称3D情况，得到了第一个与底层李-泊松结构兼容的3D磁流体离散模型。

Conclusion: 该研究首次实现了从2D到轴对称3D磁流体动力学的Zeitlin矩阵模型扩展，建立了与几何结构兼容的离散模型，为3D磁流体动力学的数值模拟提供了保持底层几何特性的新方法。

Abstract: Equations of ideal magnetohydrodynamics (MHD) play an important role in the studies of turbulence, astrophysics, and plasma physics. These equations possess remarkable geometric structures and symmetries. Indeed, they admit a geodesic formulation in the sense of Arnold, as a Lie--Poisson flow on the dual of an infinite-dimensional Lie algebra. Zeitlin's model, previously developed for MHD on the flat torus and the two-sphere, is a matrix approximation of MHD consistent with the underlying geometric structures. In this paper, we derive the reduced model of axially symmetric magnetohydrodynamics on the three-sphere and give its Hamiltonian formulation. We further extend finite dimensional Zeitlin's matrix model for MHD from 2D to axially symmetric 3D flows of magnetized fluids, yielding the first discrete model for 3D magnetohydrodynamics compatible with the underlying Lie--Poisson structure.

</details>

### [59] [On the discrete convolution of the Liouville and Möbius functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10241)
*Marco Cantarini,Alessandro Gambini,Alessandro Zaccagnini*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究刘维尔函数离散卷积S(n)的性质，特别是加权平均的显式公式及其在狄利克雷级数和幂级数中的应用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究刘维尔函数λ(n)的离散卷积S(n)的性质，这是一个类似哥德巴赫型计数函数的表示。目标是理解这种卷积的加权平均行为，并推导出显式公式来研究其解析性质。

Method: 采用近期论文中引入的一般方法，推导出满足特定条件的权重函数f(w)下S(n)加权平均的显式公式。该方法允许处理任意多个刘维尔函数因子的离散卷积。

Result: 得到了加权平均的显式公式，该公式能够提供关于S(n)的狄利克雷级数和幂级数的重要信息，并能推广到任意多个刘维尔函数因子的离散卷积情况。

Conclusion: 通过一般方法成功推导出刘维尔函数离散卷积加权平均的显式公式，这为研究相关级数的解析性质提供了有力工具，并可推广到更一般的卷积情形。

Abstract: In this article we study some properties of the discrete convolution of Liouville function $S(n):=\sum_{m_{1}+m_{2}=n}λ\left(m_{1}\right)λ\left(m_{2}\right)$, which is a Goldbach-type counting function of representations. In particular, using the general approach introduced in a recent paper \cite{CGZ}, we will give an explicit formula for weighted averages of $S(n)$ with a general weights $f(w)$ that verify suitable conditions. This formula allows us to obtain interesting information about the Dirichlet and power series of $S(n)$ and the discrete convolution with an arbitrary numbers of factors $λ(n)$.

</details>


### [60] [On the Existence of Integers with at Most 3 Prime Factors Between Every Pair of Consecutive Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10356)
*Peter Campbell*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了Legendre猜想的一个显式几乎素数版本：对于每个整数n≥1，区间(n²,(n+1)²)包含一个至多3个素因子的整数，改进了Dudek和Johnston的4个素因子结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Legendre猜想断言在任意两个连续平方数之间存在至少一个素数，但该猜想尚未被证明。研究几乎素数版本是向原始猜想逼近的重要步骤，通过减少区间内整数所需的素因子数量来改进现有结果。

Method: 结合有限验证和筛法论证：1) 对n²≤10³¹进行有限验证，利用连续平方数间短区间内的素数计算和最大素数间隙的显式界；2) 对大n使用筛法理论，将Richert的对数权重方法适配到连续平方数区间，并采用Bordignon、Johnston和Starichkova的显式线性筛。

Result: 证明了对于每个整数n≥1，区间(n²,(n+1)²)包含一个至多3个素因子的整数，改进了之前Dudek和Johnston的4个素因子结果。

Conclusion: 成功将Legendre猜想的几乎素数版本中的素因子数量从4个减少到3个，这是向原始猜想迈进的重要进展。证明方法结合了计算验证和筛法理论，为未来进一步改进奠定了基础。

Abstract: We prove an explicit almost-prime analogue of Legendre's conjecture. Namely, for every integer $n \geq 1$, the interval $(n^2,(n+1)^2)$ contains an integer having at most $3$ prime factors, counted with multiplicity. This improves the previous best result of Dudek and Johnston, who showed that every such interval contains an integer with at most $4$ prime factors. The proof combines a finite verification for $n^2 \leq 10^{31}$, obtained from computations on primes in short intervals between consecutive squares together with explicit bounds on maximal prime gaps, with a fully explicit sieve-theoretic argument for the remaining range. For large $n$, we adapt Richert's logarithmic weights to intervals between consecutive squares and employ an explicit linear sieve of Bordignon, Johnston, and Starichkova.

</details>


### [61] [Algebra Structures of Multiple Eisenstein Series in Positive Characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10376)
*Ting-Wei Chang,Song-Yun Chen,Fei-Jun Huang,Hung-Chun Tsui*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了多艾森斯坦级数的线性独立性结果，证明了多ζ值的q-洗牌代数嵌入到多艾森斯坦级数空间的逆极限中，并证明多艾森斯坦级数的q-洗牌代数同构于多ζ值代数的张量平方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在[CCHT25]中引入了正特征域上任意秩的多艾森斯坦级数及其相关的q-洗牌代数。本文旨在建立这些结构的代数性质，特别是验证[CCHT25]中提出的猜想。

Method: 通过建立多艾森斯坦级数的线性独立性结果，构造多ζ值q-洗牌代数到多艾森斯坦级数空间逆极限的嵌入映射，并证明两个代数结构之间的同构关系。

Result: 证明了多艾森斯坦级数的一类线性独立性；建立了多ζ值q-洗牌代数𝒪到多艾森斯坦级数空间逆极限的嵌入；证明了多艾森斯坦级数的q-洗牌代数ℰ同构于𝒪的张量平方𝒪⊗𝒪。

Conclusion: 作为应用，证明了ℰ是结合代数，从而验证了[CCHT25]中提出的猜想。这些结果为正特征域上多艾森斯坦级数的代数结构提供了完整的描述。

Abstract: In [CCHT25], the authors introduced multiple Eisenstein series of arbitrary rank in positive characteristic and the $q$-shuffle algebra $\mathcal{E}$ associated with them. In the present paper, we establish a class of linear independence results for multiple Eisenstein series. We also prove that the $q$-shuffle algebra $\mathcal{R}$ of multiple zeta values embeds into the inverse limit of the spaces of multiple Eisenstein series with respect to the rank $r$, and that $\mathcal{E}$ is isomorphic to the tensor square of $\mathcal{R}$. As an application, we show that $\mathcal{E}$ is an associative algebra, thereby verifying the conjecture proposed in [CCHT25]

</details>


### [62] [Transcendence of $p$-adic continued fractions and a quantitative $p$-adic Roth theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10561)
*Anne Kalitzin,Nadir Murru*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文改进了p-adic连分数的超越性结果，证明了回文和准周期p-adic连分数收敛于超越数或二次无理数，并提供了Ridout定理的定量版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进p-adic连分数的超越性结果，消除其他工作中对部分商（或收敛子）p-adic范数的限制，建立更一般的超越性判别准则。

Method: 1. 证明回文和准周期p-adic连分数收敛于超越数或二次无理数；2. 提供Ridout定理（p-adic版本的Roth定理）的定量版本；3. 研究代数数收敛子分母的增长，建立p-adic版本的Davenport-Roth结果。

Result: 1. 去除了对部分商p-adic范数的限制，建立了更一般的超越性判别法；2. 获得了Ridout定理的定量版本；3. 建立了p-adic连分数收敛子分母增长的Davenport-Roth类型结果。

Conclusion: 该论文显著改进了p-adic连分数的超越性理论，为p-adic数论中的超越性判别提供了更强大和通用的工具，特别是在处理回文和准周期连分数方面取得了重要进展。

Abstract: In this paper, we improve some transcendence results for $p$--adic continued fractions. In particular, we prove that palindromic and quasi--periodic $p$--adic continued fractions converge either to transcendental numbers or quadratic irrationals, removing any restriction on the $p$--adic norm of the partial quotients (or convergents) considered in other works. Moreover, we provide a quantitative version of Ridout's theorem (the $p$--adic analogue of Roth's theorem), and we study the growth of denominators of convergents of algebraic numbers, establishing a $p$--adic version of a well--known result of Davenport and Roth.

</details>

*Ki-Seng Tan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文引入了一个与函数域上普通椭圆曲线相关的p进L函数，建立了其插值性质、函数方程，并证明了若干情况下的岩泽主猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究函数域上椭圆曲线在Zp^d-扩张中的p进L函数理论，建立类似于数域情形的岩泽理论框架，特别是要构造具有良好插值性质和函数方程的p进L函数。

Method: 构造与普通椭圆曲线相关的p进L函数，证明其插值扭曲Hasse-Weil L函数的特殊值，建立函数方程和特殊化公式，并将其与对偶p^∞-Selmer群的特征理想联系起来。

Result: 成功构造了p进L函数并证明了其插值性质、函数方程和特殊化公式。在d≥3情况下，证明了岩泽主猜想对于A/L成立当且仅当对于Grassmannian中非空Zariski开子集内所有中间Zp^2-扩张A/L'成立。

Abstract: We introduce a $p$-adic $L$-function $\mathscr L_{A/L}$ associated to an ordinary elliptic curve $A$ over a global function field $K$ of characteristic $p$ together with a $\mathbb{Z}_{p}^{d}$-extension $L/K$, $d=0$ allowed, unramified outside a finite set of places where $A$ has ordinary (good ordinary or multiplicative) reductions. This $\mathscr L_{A/L}$ is characterized by its interpolation of the special values of twisted Hasse-Weil $L$-functions, we show that it satisfies the desired functional equation and specialization formula in connection with the characteristic ideal of the dual $p^\infty$-Selmer group of $A/L$. The Iwasawa main conjecture having $\mathscr{L}_{A / L}$ as the analytic side is proven in several cases. In the $d\geq 3$ case, %and $A/K$ has semi-stable reductions everywhere, the conjecture holds for $A/L$ if and only if it holds for all intermediate $\Z_p^2$-extensions $A/L'$ belonging to a given non-empty Zariski open subset of the Grassmannian $\mathrm{Gr}(d-2,d)(\Z_p)$.

Abstract: Generalizing our previous work on ``toroidal averages'', we study the average of special values of $L$-functions of the form $L(1/2,χ^a)L(1/2,χ^b)L(1/2,χ^c)$ for integers $a$, $b$ and $c$, where $χ$ varies over Dirichlet characters of a given prime modulus. Abstract: Chromatic redshift phenomena suggest that algebraic K-theory increases the height of a commutative ring spectra by one. In many cases, the chromatic redshift is already detected by negative topological cyclic homology. This paper explores higher chromatic redshift via homotopy fixed points spectrum of higher topological Hochschild homology. Specifically, starting from a commutative ring spectrum that detects $v_n$-elements, the homotopy fixed points spectrum of higher topological Hochschild homology of it detects $v_{n+k}$-elements, with $k$ greater than one.

These computations are then applied to the study of lifts of cohomology operations via the Bredon cohomology of equivariant complex projective spaces.

Abstract: We consider $(\infty,d)$-categories in the limit $d\to \infty$ via the core or localization functors that forget or invert higher non-invertible arrows, respectively. We compare the two resulting $(\infty,1)$-categories of $(\infty,\infty)$-categories and exhibit the localization-limit as a reflective localization of the core-limit.
  On the side, we study intermediate localizations that arise from notions of invertibility that only emerge at $d=\infty$ such as the one defined by coinduction.

</details>

### [68] [Estimands and the Choice of Non-Inferiority Margin under ICH E9(R1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10889)
*Tobias Mütze,Helle Lynggaard,Sunita Rehal,Oliver N. Keene,Marian Mitroiu,David Wright*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了ICH E9(R1)估计量框架下非劣效性试验中非劣效性界值的确定问题，强调界值选择必须反映所选的估计量，并通过模拟研究展示了不同估计量对界值的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICH E9(R1)估计量框架在非劣效性试验中的应用远少于优效性试验。当前监管指南早于ICH E9(R1)，未能反映估计量如何影响用于推导非劣效性界值的历史证据和恒定性假设（检测灵敏度）。需要研究非劣效性界值在多大程度上依赖于估计量。

Method: 使用模拟患者旅程方法，在体重管理背景下，展示不同中间事件策略和中间事件频率变化如何影响估计量，进而影响治疗效果估计。通过两个理论体重管理治疗的非劣效性试验示例，说明确定非劣效性界值的过程。

Result: 研究结果表明，参考治疗相对于安慰剂的历史治疗效果（即界值M1）是特定于某个估计量的，即使试验形式上针对相似问题，界值也可能不同。当历史试验使用不同估计量或未采用估计量框架时，确定非劣效性界值具有挑战性。

Conclusion: 非劣效性界值的选择必须与估计量保持一致。历史试验的估计量框架使用情况（是否采用、是否与计划研究相同）直接影响非劣效性界值的确定。监管指南需要更新以反映ICH E9(R1)估计量框架对非劣效性试验设计的影响。

Abstract: Since the release of the ICH E9(R1) addendum on estimands, its application in non-inferiority trials has received far less attention than in superiority settings. A key conclusion from Lynggaard et al. was that the "choice of non-inferiority margin must reflect the chosen estimand." However, current regulatory guidance predates ICH E9(R1) and therefore does not reflect how the estimand influences the historical evidence and constancy assumption (assay sensitivity) used to derive the non-inferiority margin. This paper investigates the degree to which the non-inferiority margin depends on the estimand. Using simulated patient journeys in a weight-management setting, we illustrate how different intercurrent event strategies and variations in the intercurrent event frequency affect the estimand, and consequently the estimated treatment effect. These results emphasize that the historical treatment effect of the reference treatment versus placebo, and thus the margin $M_{1}$, is specific to an estimand and may differ even when trials formally target similar questions. We further illustrate the process of determining the non-inferiority margin using two examples in non-inferiority trials for a new theoretical weight management treatment. In the first example, we focus on the setting where the historical clinical trials use the estimand framework highlighting that even when they include the estimand framework, determining the non-inferiority margin can be challenging in case the historical trials target an estimand different from the one in the planned study. A second example highlights challenges when historical trials did not employ the estimand framework and the targeted estimand cannot be fully reconstructed.

</details>

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于复杂几何体上移动接触线模拟的锐利保守3D数值方法，结合几何VOF和嵌入式边界框架，实现了严格的质量守恒和接触角条件准确施加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在复杂三维几何体上模拟移动接触线时面临挑战：难以在嵌入式边界存在时保持严格质量守恒，小切割单元导致严重时间步长限制，以及在非规则固体表面上准确施加接触角条件困难。

Method: 1) 针对包含液、气、固三相的混合单元，设计了改进的VOF输运和重构方案；2) 引入重新分布输运策略消除小切割单元的CFL约束；3) 基于高度函数框架开发了新型3D接触角施加技术，采用预拟合抛物面程序；4) 建模接触角滞后效应以捕捉更真实的润湿动力学。

Result: 通过一系列具有挑战性的基准测试验证了方法的准确性、鲁棒性和优越性。该方法首次建立了完全几何且保守的VOF方案，能够准确解析任意复杂3D表面上的接触线动力学。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了复杂几何体上移动接触线模拟的关键难题，实现了严格质量守恒、无CFL约束的时间步长、以及非规则表面上准确的接触角施加，为三维润湿动力学研究提供了强大工具。

Abstract: We propose a sharp and conservative 3D numerical method for simulating moving contact lines on complex geometries, developed within a coupled geometric Volume-of-Fluid (VOF) and embedded boundary framework. The first major contribution is a modified VOF advection and reconstruction scheme specifically designed for mixed cells containing liquid, gas, and solid phases. This formulation ensures strict local mass conservation in the presence of arbitrarily shaped embedded boundaries. To overcome the severe time-step limitation caused by small cut cells, a redistribution advection strategy is introduced, which completely removes the CFL constraint while preserving both local and global volume conservation. The second key contribution is a novel 3D contact angle imposition technique built upon the height function framework. By incorporating a pre-fitting paraboloid procedure, the method achieves robust curvature estimation and accurate enforcement of contact angle conditions on irregular solid surfaces. In addition, contact angle hysteresis is modeled to capture more realistic wetting dynamics. A series of challenging benchmark tests have been conducted to demonstrate the accuracy, robustness, and superiority of the proposed method compared with existing sharp-interface approaches. This study, for the first time, establishes a fully geometric and conservative VOF-based scheme capable of accurately resolving contact line dynamics on arbitrarily complex 3D surfaces.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, a novel QR decomposition-based compression scheme is combined with a volume integral equations method for the fast and efficient numerical computation of the scattering of electromagnetic fields from large scale metasurfaces, via an iterative approach.
  The underlying problem is of a multiscale nature. Indeed, these metasurfaces are made of a large collection of interacting sub-wavelength scatterers, thus making the numerical computation of the solution very challenging.
  More specifically, the paper proposes a tailored version of a QR decomposition-based compression for a volume integral equation, together with a proper preconditioner that exploits the geometrical structure of the array, in order to achieve a fast and accurate iterative solver, in view of realistic applications. Numerical examples prove the effectiveness of the method in efficiently modeling metasurfaces made by thousands of particles.

</details>

*Edoardo Di Napoli,Xinzhe Wu*

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于Chebyshev滤波子空间迭代算法中QR分解条件数上界估计的方法，并将其集成到ChASE库中实现自动QR算法选择，提升性能而不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 通过引入精确的条件数上界估计和相应的QR算法选择机制，成功优化了Chebyshev滤波子空间迭代算法的实现，在保证精度的前提下提升了计算效率。

Abstract: Chebyshev filtered subspace iteration is a well-known algorithm for the solution of (symmetric/Hermitian) algebraic eigenproblems which has been implemented in several application codes~\cite{Kronik:2006ff, abinit:2020} or in stand alone libraries~\cite{ChASE}. An essential part of the algorithm is the QR-factorization of the array of vectors spanning the active subspace that have been filtered by the Chebyshev filter. Typically such an array has an a-priori unknown high condition number that directly influences the choice of QR-factorization algorithm. In this work we show how such condition number can be bound from above with precise and inexpensive estimates. We then proceed to use these estimates to implement a mechanism for the choice of QR-factorization in the ChASE library. We show how such mechanism enhance the performance of the library without compromising on its accuracy.

</details>

Conclusion: 成功建立了Banach空间中带扰动鞍点问题的离散可解性分析框架，为处理非正则数据下的混合形式对流-扩散-反应问题提供了有效的数值方法，扩展了Stenberg后处理的应用范围。

Abstract: In this work, we develop the discrete solvability analysis for perturbed saddle-point problems in Banach spaces with forcing terms regularised by means of a projector constructed using the adjoint of a weighted Clément quasi-interpolation. We take as driving example the linearised Poisson--Boltzmann (an advection-diffusion-reaction problem) in mixed form. We use perturbation arguments on the continuous and discrete levels and then derive a priori estimates that remain valid when the load that appears on the right-hand side of the "second" equation is in $\mathrm{H}^{-1}$. Further, we show a supercloseness result and {analyse convergence} of an adequate adaptation of Stenberg postprocessing for mixed advection equations with non-regular data. We provide numerical results that illustrate the convergence of the proposed scheme.

*Paul Moujaes,Dmitri Kuzmin,Christian Bäumer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出基于能量相关M1矩模型的确定性质子治疗剂量计算框架，采用有限元离散化和凸限制策略确保物理可容许性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We present a deterministic framework for proton therapy dose calculation based on finite element discretizations of the energy-dependent $M_1$ moment model. The nonlinear $M_1$ system is derived from the Fokker--Planck equation for charged particles and closed using an entropy-based approximation of the second moment. Energy is treated as a pseudo-time coordinate. The zeroth and first moments of the proton fluence are evolved backward in energy. To ensure hyperbolicity and physical admissibility, we employ a monolithic convex limiting (MCL) strategy. Representing the standard continuous Galerkin discretization in terms of auxiliary `bar' states, we construct a nonlinear scheme that is provably invariant domain preserving (IDP) w.r.t. convex realizable sets consisting of all admissible states. The realizability of the bar states is enforced using the MCL technology for homogeneous hyperbolic systems. The forcing induced by stiff scattering is incorporated using Strang-type operator splitting. We use an explicit strong-stability-preserving Runge--Kutta method for the radiation transport subproblem and exact integration in the forcing steps, which guarantees the IDP property. The deposited dose is defined as the integral of a weighted zeroth moment over a bounded energy range. It is accumulated during the backward-in-energy evolution. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed Strang-MCL method produces accurate and physically consistent dose distributions.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Full-physics modeling of multiphase flow in porous media, e.g., for carbon storage and groundwater management, requires the nonlinear coupling of various physical processes. Industry standard nonlinear solvers, typically of Newton-type, are not unconditionally convergent and computationally expensive. Homotopy continuation solvers have recently been studied as a robust and versatile alternative. They tackle challenging nonlinear problems by first solving a simple auxiliary problem and then tracing a solution curve towards the more complex target problem. Robustness and efficiency of the method depends on the iterative numerical curve tracing algorithm as well as on careful design of the auxiliary problem. We assess the traceability of the solution curve for different choices of the auxiliary problem. For the Buckley-Leverett equation, modeling two-phase flow in one dimension, we exemplarily compare the previously introduced vanishing-diffusion and linear constitutive laws homotopy continuation, and a new approach based on the entropy solution of the problem. This provides insight toward systematically and robustly designing homotopy continuation methods for solving complex multiphase flow in porous media.

</details>

Motivation: 针对时间延迟系统（特别是半显式延迟微分代数方程）的H²范数计算与优化问题，需要高效、稳定的数值方法。现有方法在处理多延迟、中性型系统时存在收敛性挑战，需要开发能够同时保证稳定性和收敛性的近似方法。

Method: 采用Lanczos tau方法对时间延迟系统的H²范数进行近似和优化。方法基于有理指数函数近似，要求离散化是稳定性保持的。推导了系统参数和延迟相对于近似H²范数的梯度显式公式。进一步探讨了基于样条的改进方法，特别是基于Legendre正交多项子的样条方法。

Result: 证明了方法在有限强H²范数假设下的正确性。数值结果表明：多延迟情况下，延迟型系统以三次速率收敛，中性型系统以线性速率收敛；单延迟情况下，选择对称基时，延迟型和中性型系统都呈现几何收敛。梯度计算时间仅为单独计算H²范数的约两倍。基于样条的近似方法将收敛速率提高了约两个数量级。

Conclusion: 提出的Lanczos tau方法为时间延迟系统的H²范数近似和优化提供了有效工具，特别适用于鲁棒反馈控制器合成和稳定近似模型构建。基于Legendre正交多项子的样条方法进一步保证了稳定性和收敛性，显著提高了计算效率。

Abstract: We present a Lanczos tau method for the approximation and optimization of the $H^2$-norm of time-delay systems described by semi-explicit delay differential algebraic equations. The soundness of this approach is proven under the assumption of a finite strong $H^2$-norm. Furthermore, we prove convergence if the rational approximation of the exponential underlying the discretization is well-behaved and the discretization is stability preserving. Numerical results suggest that, for multiple delays, the method converges at cubic rate in the discretization degree for systems of retarded type and linearly for those of neutral type. In the single delay case, we note geometric convergence of the $H^2$-norm for systems of both retarded and neutral type when a symmetric basis is chosen.
  Explicit formulas are derived for the gradient of the approximation with respect to system parameters and delays. These allow us to compute the entire gradient using only about double the computational time of approximating the $H^2$-norm alone. We illustrate how these can be used to synthesize robust feedback controllers and stable approximate models.
  The article is concluded by a discussion of how the presented results extend and improve for approximations based on splines. We note acceleration of the convergence rate by about two orders for such a choice. Finally, we prove that a Lanczos tau method using a spline based on Legendre orthogonal polynomials preserves stability and guarantees convergence of the $H^2$-norm.

</details>

Result: 二维和三维数值实验显示显著计算收益：运行时间从数小时减少到数十分钟，内存节省约3倍，同时保持全精细尺度精度。该策略能够在普通笔记本电脑上在几分钟内计算涉及数千个单元（即数百万自由度）的问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法通过利用晶格结构的内在自相似性，在域分解框架内应用降阶建模，实现了非线性超弹性晶格结构全体积精细尺度模拟的高效求解，显著降低了计算成本和内存需求，使大规模晶格结构非线性分析在普通计算设备上成为可能。

Abstract: With the growing maturity of additive manufacturing, the fabrication of architected or lattice-based metamaterials has become a reality for industrial applications. These materials combine lightweight design with tailored mechanical properties, most of which exhibit pronounced nonlinear, especially large-deformation, behaviors. The main numerical challenge therefore lies in performing nonlinear simulations of such lattice structures, which may contain thousands of geometrically intricate unit cells, while lacking sufficient scale separation for multiscale homogenization schemes to be applicable straightforwardly. In this work, we propose a dedicated solver for the full volumetric fine-scale simulation of nonlinear hyperelastic lattice structures that drastically reduces both memory and computational costs. The key idea is to exploit the intrinsic self-similarity of the cells through a reduced-order modeling strategy applied within a domain-decomposition framework. At each Newton iteration, a limited set of principal cells is identified through a dedicated, weakly intrusive, EIM-like approach, allowing all local tangent operators to be expressed as linear combinations of a few principal ones. This enables fast and memory-efficient operator assembly, and then feeds an efficient inexact FETI-DP based preconditioner at the solution stage, resulting in a quasi matrix-free algorithm for the nonlinear analysis. Numerical experiments in two and three dimensions demonstrate significant computational gains, with runtime reductions from several hours to a few tens of minutes and memory savings by factors of about three, while maintaining full fine-scale accuracy. Notably, the proposed strategy enables the computation of problems involving thousands of cells (i.e., millions of degrees of freedom) within a few minutes on an off-the-shelf laptop.

</details>


### [77] [On the consistency of the Domain of Dependence cut cell stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10754)
*Gunnar Birke,Christian Engwer,Jan Giesselmann,Sandra May*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 论文证明了Domain of Dependence (DoD)稳定化方法在任意多项式阶数下的理论一致性，为高阶情况下的误差分析奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 笛卡尔切割网格在双曲问题中应用显式时间步进时，由于可能存在的极小切割单元会导致时间步长受到不切实际的限制。DoD稳定化方法允许基于底层笛卡尔网格的时间步长，但其高阶精度特性仅在数值上得到验证，缺乏理论分析。

Method: 针对DoD稳定化方法，在任意多项式阶数k下，对具有足够正则性的精确解，证明了该方法的一致性结果。这是不连续伽辽金(DG)方法的扩展，但需要专门处理切割网格带来的稳定性问题。

Result: 证明了DoD稳定化方法在任意多项式阶数下的理论一致性，这为后续高阶情况下的误差分析提供了基础。虽然数值结果已经支持该方法具有与纯DG方法相似的精度特性，但此前仅在k=0时进行过深入分析。

Conclusion: DoD稳定化方法的一致性证明为该方法在高阶情况下的更精细分析开辟了道路，有助于建立完整的理论框架来支持其在实际应用中的有效性。

Abstract: So called cartesian cut cell meshes provide efficient ways to generate meshes but do require tailored numerical methods to not suffer from stabilization issues, especially in the hyperbolic regime where the application of explicit time stepping schemes is common. In this scenario, due to potentially arbitrarily small cut cells, an infeasible restriction is imposed on the time step size. The Domain of Dependence (DoD) stabilization allows for a time step size based on the underlying Cartesian mesh. Being an extension of a discontinuous Galerkin (DG) method, one would expect similar accuracy properties as in the pure DG case. While numerical results do support this expectation, on the analytical level this has only been investigated thoroughly for $k=0$. Error analysis typically hinges on a consistency result. In this contribution we prove such a result for the DoD stabilization given an arbitrary polynomial degree and an exact solution of sufficient regularity. This in turn could open the way towards a more refined analysis of the method even in the high-order case.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 获得了误差界的三个组成部分：指数衰减的初始值差异部分、与空间和时间网格尺寸成比例的部分、与测量误差和推动参数成比例的部分，后两部分的常数与时间和网格尺寸无关。

Abstract: We study the convergence of a discrete Luenberger observer for the barotropic Euler equations in one dimension, for measurements of the velocity only. We use a mixed finite element method in space and implicit Euler integration in time. We use a modified relative energy technique to show an error bound comparing the discrete observer to the original system's solution. The bound is the sum of three parts: an exponentially decaying part, proportional to the difference in initial value, a part proportional to the grid sizes in space and time and a part that is proportional to the size of the measurement errors as well as the nudging parameter. The proportionality constants of the second and third parts are independent of time and grid sizes. To the best of our knowledge, this provides the first error estimate for a discrete observer for a quasilinear hyperbolic system, and implies uniform-in-time accuracy of the discrete observer for long-time simulations.

</details>


### [79] [Linear-Scaling Tensor Train Sketching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11009)
*Paul Cazeaux,Mi-Song Dupuy,Rodrigo Figueroa Justiniano*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: BSTT sketch是一种针对张量列车格式的结构化随机投影，通过参数P和R在Khatri-Rao sketch和高斯TT sketch之间插值，提供线性依赖张量阶数d和子空间维度r的OSE和OSI保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有张量列车格式的随机投影方法存在指数级依赖张量阶数d的问题，需要开发更高效的随机投影算子来改善计算复杂度。

Method: 提出块稀疏张量列车(BSTT)草图，通过两个整数参数P和R控制结构，统一了现有的TT适应草图算子，在Khatri-Rao草图(R=1)和高斯TT草图(P=1)之间插值。

Result: BSTT满足参数R=O(d(r+log1/δ))和P=O(ε^{-2})的OSE性质，以及R=O(d)和P=O(ε^{-2}(r+log r/δ))的OSI性质。这些保证仅线性依赖于d和r，而非指数依赖。

Conclusion: BSTT草图提供了高效的TT格式随机投影，推导出QB分解和随机TT舍入的准最优误差界，理论结果得到合成张量、Hadamard积和量子化学应用的数值实验支持。

Abstract: We introduce the Block Sparse Tensor Train (BSTT) sketch, a structured random projection tailored to the tensor train (TT) format that unifies existing TT-adapted sketching operators. By varying two integer parameters $P$ and $R$, BSTT interpolates between the Khatri-Rao sketch ($R=1$) and the Gaussian TT sketch ($P=1$). We prove that BSTT satisfies an oblivious subspace embedding (OSE) property with parameters $R = \mathcal{O}(d(r+\log 1/δ))$ and $P = \mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$, and an oblivious subspace injection (OSI) property under the condition $R = \mathcal{O}(d)$ and $P = \mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-2}(r + \log r/δ))$. Both guarantees depend only linearly on the tensor order $d$ and on the subspace dimension $r$, in contrast to prior constructions that suffer from exponential scaling in $d$. As direct consequences, we derive quasi-optimal error bounds for the QB factorization and randomized TT rounding. The theoretical results are supported by numerical experiments on synthetic tensors, Hadamard products, and a quantum chemistry application.

</details>

### [80] [XMM-Newton Observations of Flares and a Possible Pulse Dropout in the Supergiant X-ray binary 4U 1909+07](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10111)
*Joel B. Coley,Ralf Ballhausen,McKinley Brumback,Robin H. D. Corbet,Camille M. Diez,Felix Fuerst,Nazma Islam,Gaurava K. Jaisawal,Peter Kretschmar,Christian Malacaria,Katja Pottschmidt,Pragati Pradhan*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超巨星X射线双星4U 1909+07的脉冲特性变化，特别是首次观测到的脉冲丢失现象，以理解其物理机制

Method: 使用XMM-Newton望远镜于2021年10月3日和8日进行两次观测，进行时序分析和时间分辨光谱分析，测量中子星自转周期和脉冲轮廓变化

Result: 中子星自转周期持续减小至602.62秒，首次观测到单脉冲周期内的脉冲丢失现象，光谱在脉冲丢失期间显著软化，未发现吸收柱密度增加

Abstract: We report on a pair of X-ray Multi-Mirror Mission (XMM-Newton) observations of the Supergiant X-ray binary 4U 1909+07, which were performed on 2021 October 3 and 2021 October 8, respectively. We measure the neutron star rotation period in each observation to be $\sim$602.62 s. This continues a long spin-up trend that has persisted since 2001 where the neutron star spin period was found to be $\sim$604.66 s. In our timing analysis, we observe strong variations in the amplitude of the 1--10 keV pulse profile as a function of time, and for the first time we find a low flux interval extending for a single pulse period in which pulsations are no longer detected. We interpret this low flux interval as a pulse dropout similar to those observed in Vela X-1 and GX 301-2, which were each explained by a low-density cavity in the wind driving the propeller effect. In our time-resolved spectral analysis, we observed the spectral continuum, which can be described as an absorbed power law modified by a high-energy cutoff, to significantly soften during the pulse-dropout phase. No evidence of an increasing absorption column density was found. The observed softening in 4U 1909+07 also supports an interpretation that the observed pulse dropout may be driven by the propeller effect, but the quasi-spherical settling accretion regime cannot be ruled out.

</details>


### [81] [On the angular localization of gravitational-wave signals by pulsar timing arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10120)
*Stephen R. Taylor*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究系统分析了脉冲星计时阵列中引力波信号定位的影响因素，推导了Cramér-Rao定位精度的解析表达式，揭示了脉冲星距离精度和角位置对定位性能的影响机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解脉冲星计时阵列中引力波信号定位的物理机制和影响因素，为优化脉冲星计时阵列的定位性能提供理论指导。

Method: 推导Cramér-Rao定位精度的解析表达式，分析地球项和脉冲星项干涉效应，研究脉冲星距离精度对定位的影响，比较不同处理策略（相位解耦与天线响应驱动）的定位性能。

Result: 1. 定位精度受脉冲星与源角距离ξ和脉冲星距离精度σ_L共同影响；2. 当σ_L≤λ_GW/(1-cosξ)时，提高距离精度可改善定位(ΔΩ_sky∝σ_L²)；3. 距离精度较差时，增加源附近的脉冲星数量是改善定位的最佳途径；4. 相位解耦处理策略的定位性能与天线响应驱动情况相似。

Conclusion: 脉冲星计时阵列的引力波定位性能由脉冲星距离精度和角位置共同决定，优化策略需根据距离精度情况选择：高精度时提高距离测量精度，低精度时增加源附近的脉冲星数量。当前使用的相位解耦方法定位性能有限。

Abstract: We provide a complete study of the factors influencing gravitational-wave signal localization using pulsar timing arrays. We derive analytical expressions for the Cramér-Rao sky localization precision that delineate the impact of the angular proximity, $ξ$, between the pulsar and the gravitational wave source, and the precision, $σ_L$, with which pulsar distances are known. Interference between the Earth and pulsar terms creates rapid angular oscillations for sky-coordinate Fisher matrix elements that aids localization, which is complemented by more broadly varying antenna response gradient information. The relative importance of these factors depends on whether pulsar distances are known precisely [i.e., $σ_L\leqλ_\mathrm{GW}/(1-\cosξ)$] or imprecisely, respectively. If the former, tightening pulsar distance precisions improves signal localization according to $ΔΩ_\mathrm{sky}\proptoσ_L^2$ until the Earth-pulsar system reaches its diffraction limit. If the latter, localization precision is degraded, but more pulsars in close proximity to the source is the best means of improving. With $α$ indexing pulsars, this scales as $ΔΩ_\mathrm{sky}~\propto~(\sum_α\mathrm{SNR}_α^2/ξ_α^2)^{-1}$ in the small-angle limit of the unmarginalized Fisher matrix, and we derive the analytic generalization to any angle between a pulsar and the source. Finally, we study a scenario where pulsar-term phases are treated as nuisance variables that are unconnected to binary or PTA properties. This phase-decoupled scenario, which is how all PTA continuous wave searches are currently conducted, delivers localization performance similar to the antenna-response--driven case, and does not exhibit significant improvement as pulsar distance precisions are tightened.

</details>


### [82] [The baryon content of magnetically arrested black hole disks and jets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10122)
*Anthony Chow,Lorenzo Sironi,Bart Ripperda,Amir Levinson*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究通过GRMHD模拟结合被动欧拉示踪剂，分析了磁滞吸积流和相对论喷流中的重子输运，揭示了喷流重子加载的周期性特征及其与磁通量喷发循环的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究磁滞吸积流和相对论喷流中重子输运的物理机制，特别是喷流重子加载的时间演化特征，这对于理解喷流成分、电荷饥饿状态以及粒子加速和非热辐射过程至关重要。

Method: 采用广义相对论磁流体动力学（GRMHD）模拟，结合被动欧拉示踪剂来重建物理重子密度，同时排除数值注入的质量以维持高磁化低密度区域的稳定性。对具有不同自旋的轴对称黑洞进行模拟，分析重子加载的时空演化。

Result: 喷流重子加载本质上是周期性的，由内吸积流中的磁通量喷发循环调控。每次喷发都会从最内赤道区域清除重子，驱动扩展电流片中的重联，并沿漏斗壁排出中等磁化的盘物质，建立循环的质量加载通道。对于自旋黑洞，喷流边界处的剪切驱动波进一步增强了重子夹带，而在非自旋情况下该机制被抑制。对于M87黑洞吸积流参数，电磁功率主要由重子贫乏等离子体携带，存在长时间的电荷饥饿状态。

Conclusion: 该研究为诊断磁滞黑洞系统中的喷流成分、电荷饥饿和重联驱动的质量加载提供了理论框架，对低光度吸积流中的粒子加速和非热辐射具有直接意义。重子加载的周期性特征和自旋依赖的夹带机制是理解喷流物理的关键要素。

Abstract: We study the transport of baryons in magnetically arrested accretion flows and relativistic jets using general relativistic magnetohydrodynamic simulations that incorporate a passive Eulerian tracer. The tracer allows us to reconstruct a proxy for the physical baryon density supplied by the accretion disk while excluding the mass injected numerically to maintain stability in highly magnetized, low-density regions. Applying this method to axisymmetric black hole simulations with varying spin, we show that baryon loading of the jet is intrinsically episodic and regulated by magnetic flux eruption cycles occurring in the inner accretion flow. Each eruption evacuates baryons from the innermost equatorial region, drives reconnection in extended current sheets, and expels moderately magnetized disk material along the funnel wall, establishing a recurrent mass-loading channel. In spinning black holes, shear-driven waves along the jet boundary further enhance baryon entrainment, whereas this mechanism is suppressed in the non-spinning case. For parameters representative of the black hole accretion flow in M87, we map the global structure and time evolution of the Goldreich-Julian screening boundary, defined as the surface separating regions where the plasma density is sufficient to supply the charges required to screen electric fields parallel to the magnetic field from regions that are charge starved. For spinning black holes, we find that the electromagnetic power of the jet is predominantly carried by baryon-poor plasma, with extended time intervals of charge starvation. Our results provide a framework for diagnosing jet composition, charge starvation, and reconnection-driven mass loading in magnetically arrested black hole systems, with direct implications for particle acceleration and non-thermal emission in low-luminosity accretion flows.

</details>


### [83] [Quantifying the impact of relativistic precession on tidal disruption event light curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10124)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞潮汐场可将恒星撕裂成气体流，当黑洞快速旋转时该流会发生进动。需要研究这种进动如何影响潮汐瓦解事件的光变曲线观测特征

Method: 采用二维辐射流体动力学模拟，研究潮汐瓦解事件产生的星风与光度与环绕黑洞的进动流之间的相互作用。同时探讨在弯曲时空下自洽模拟潮汐恒星流流体动力学演化的方法

Result: 对于约10^6太阳质量黑洞且无轨道-自旋倾角的情况，视线方向对光变曲线影响很小，因为进动局限于轨道平面，流仅覆盖小部分立体角。对于大于10^7太阳质量黑洞且高倾角（i~90°）的情况，进动流在事件早期遮挡辐射，可使光变曲线峰值延迟约100天

Conclusion: 黑洞自旋引起的潮汐流进动对潮汐瓦解事件光变曲线有显著影响，特别是对大质量高倾角黑洞，进动流对辐射的遮挡效应可导致观测延迟，这对解释观测数据具有重要意义

Abstract: The tidal field of a black hole can turn a star into a gas stream whose orbit can precess, especially if the a black hole is rapidly spinning. In this work, we investigate the impact of precession on the light curves of tidal disruption events (TDE). To do so, we perform two-dimensional radiation-hydrodynamic simulations of the interaction of the TDE wind and luminosity with the precessed stream wrapped around the black hole. Our results show that in events with black holes of $\sim10^6~\text{M}_{\odot}$ and no orbit-spin inclination, the line of sight has little effect on the light curves, since the stream covers a small fraction of the solid angle as the precession is confined to the orbital plane. In the case of black holes of $\gtrsim10^7~\text{M}_{\odot}$ and high inclination ($i\sim90^{\circ}$), the light curve peaks can be delayed by $\sim$100 days due to presence of the precessed stream blocking the radiation in the early phase of the event. We also discuss our efforts to model self-consistently the hydrodynamic evolution of a tidal stellar stream on curved spacetimes by the presence of a massive black hole.

</details>


### [84] [Accreting White Dwarfs: An Unreview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10150)
*Simone Scaringi,Christian Knigge,Domitilla de Martino*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文是一篇关于吸积白矮星（AWDs）的"非综述"，重点讨论该领域尚未解决的关键科学问题，而非已确认的知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 吸积白矮星是研究盘吸积物理的理想天然实验室，但尽管经过数十年研究，许多基本物理问题仍未解决。论文旨在通过识别这些开放性问题来推动新的研究努力。

Method: 采用"非综述"方法，不总结已知知识，而是系统性地提出关键未解问题，包括：中性盘中的粘滞驱动机制、强风的产生与反馈效应、持续逆行进动的原因、磁化AWDs爆发机制等。

Result: 论文识别了吸积白矮星研究中的四个核心开放问题：1) 中性盘中粘滞和吸积的驱动机制；2) 强风的产生及其对盘和双星演化的反馈；3) 系统持续逆行进动的原因；4) 磁化AWDs爆发机制。

Conclusion: 通过明确这些未解问题并提出解决途径，论文旨在激励新的观测、数值模拟和理论研究，从而推进对所有质量尺度（从白矮星到黑洞）吸积物理的理解。

Abstract: Accreting white dwarfs (AWDs) are among the best natural laboratories for understanding disk accretion. Their proximity, brightness, and purely classical nature make them ideal systems in which to probe the fundamental physics that governs the transport of angular momentum, the generation of outflows, and the coupling between disks, magnetospheres, and accretors. Yet despite decades of study, many critical questions remain unresolved. In this ``unreview'', we therefore focus not on what is known, but on what is unknown. What drives viscosity and sustains accretion in largely neutral disks? How are powerful winds launched, and how do they feed back on the disk and binary evolution? Why do so many systems show persistent retrograde precession, and what drives bursts in magnetic AWDs? By identifying these open problems -- and suggesting ways to resolve them -- we aim to motivate new observational, numerical, and theoretical efforts that will advance our understanding of accretion physics across all mass scales, from white dwarfs to black holes.

</details>

Result: 在暴发早期阶段检测到从约37MeV演化到约6MeV的时变谱线，与镍158keV衰变分支的多普勒加速一致；在290-300秒发现约24MeV的高能过剩，与镍270keV衰变分支一致；观测到的运动学和通量演化与放射性衰变预期相符。

Conclusion: GRB 221009A早期伽马射线暴中的时变谱线为超新星核合成的镍元素被相对论性喷流加速提供了直接光谱证据，支持伽马暴起源于大质量恒星核心坍缩并伴随超新星的理论框架。

Abstract: Long Gamma Ray Bursts are thought to originate from the core collapse of massive stars that give rise to energetic broad-lined Type Ic supernovae. The brightest burst ever recorded, GRB 221009A, has been linked to a broad-lined Type Ic supernova through late-time observations by the James Webb Space Telescope. An emission line evolving from $\sim$37 to $\sim$6~MeV is detected during the prompt phase. We propose that this time-evolving line is consistent with Doppler-boosted radioactive decay of nickel synthesized in the associated supernova and entrained in the relativistic jet, corresponding to the boosted 158~keV decay branch. We also report evidence for an additional higher-energy excess near $\sim$24~MeV at 290--300~s, detected at moderate statistical significance and consistent with the boosted 270~keV decay branch. The observed kinematics and flux evolution are compatible with expectations from radioactive decay, providing direct spectroscopic evidence linking prompt emission to supernova nucleosynthesis.

</details>


### [86] [Neutrinos from extreme astrophysical sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10167)
*Xavier Rodrigues*

Method: 采用文献综述方法，系统梳理和评述多个国际大型中微子观测项目（IceCube、KM3NeT等）的最新测量结果。通过对比分析不同能段（TeV-PeV、超高能）的观测数据，结合多信使天文学方法，评估各类潜在中微子源（塞弗特星系、耀变体、潮汐瓦解事件等）的观测证据和理论模型。

Result: 1. IceCube成功测量了TeV-PeV能段的弥散宇宙中微子谱，并建立了超高能区的流量上限；2. KM3NeT观测到超高能中微子事件；3. 在NGC 1068等邻近塞弗特星系中探测到TeV信号；4. γ射线耀变体在PeV以上能段显示出作为中微子源的潜力；5. 潮汐瓦解事件等瞬变源的中微子关联证据仍在积累中；6. 当前源模型存在局限性，需要更多观测数据验证。

Conclusion: 高能中微子天文学正处于快速发展阶段，多个潜在源类别已显示出观测信号。然而，当前统计数据有限，源关联仍需进一步确认。下一代多信使设施（如IceCube-Gen2）对于巩固现有源关联、探测超高能区、识别大量未确认的瞬变源群体具有不可或缺的作用，将推动该领域实现突破性进展。

Abstract: In this paper I review recent results on high-energy neutrino astronomy and what they can reveal about some of the most extreme cosmic accelerators. I discuss recent measurements of the diffuse TeV-PeV cosmic neutrino spectrum by the IceCube observatory and the current flux limits in the ultra-high-energy regime, contextualizing the recent detection of an ultra-high-energy neutrino by the KM3NeT observatory. I review the recent emergence of a TeV signal from nearby Seyfert galaxies such as NGC 1068, the potential of $γ$-ray blazars as neutrino sources above the PeV regime, and the current status of tidal disruption events and other transient classes as possible neutrino sources. For each of these topics, I discuss ongoing developments in source models and their current limitations. I argue for the indispensable role of next-generation multi-messenger facilities, such as IceCube-Gen2, in solidifying current source associations, probing the ultra-high-energy regime, and resolving vast transient populations that remain unidentified with current statistics.

</details>
