Conclusion: 更精确的反冲速度模型对球状星团中层次合并黑洞的演化有重要影响，这有助于理解大质量双星系统（如GW231123）的形成机制，并强调了在群体合成研究中采用先进反冲模型的重要性。

Abstract: In globular clusters, hierarchical mergers are among the most promising pathways to forming massive black holes such as GW231123. A key factor determining whether a merger-remnant black hole will be retained in these environments and thus participate in subsequent hierarchical mergers is the recoil kick velocity. Analytic models for the recoil velocity are currently employed in nearly all population-synthesis frameworks. We instead use a state-of-the-art recoil-kick model gwModel_flow_prec developed from a combination of numerical-relativity and black-hole perturbation-theory data, together with data-driven techniques such as normalizing flows and the post-Newtonian structure of the kick. Employing both back-of-the-envelope estimates and detailed N-body as well as semi-analytical cluster simulations, we show that gwModel_flow_prec leads to a noticeable increase in the retention probability of hierarchical-merger remnants compared to the previously used analytic model and changes the mass and spin distribution of the black holes formed through hierarchical mergers. Additionally, we discuss the implications of our results in the context of massive binaries such as GW231123.

</details>


### [88] [Unexpectedly Weak General Relativistic Effects in Strongly Relativistic Tidal Disruption Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10208)

Main category: astro-ph.HE

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统观点：传统认为当恒星近心点小于约10倍引力半径时，强相对论效应会迅速耗散轨道能量并产生快速盘形成。本研究旨在通过广义相对论流体动力学模拟验证这一假设。

Method: 使用广义相对论流体动力学模拟，研究围绕10^6太阳质量黑洞的强相对论性TDE。模拟从恒星初始接近开始，跟踪碎片演化至峰值质量返回时间后35天（约23天）。分析相对论性进动、近心点压缩、流自相互作用等机制。

Result: 1. 整体演化与弱相对论性TDEs相似：碎片保持高偏心率，大部分返回质量停留在轨道远心点附近（约初始近心点距离的250倍）；2. 冲击波而非吸积主导事件；3. 早期冲击波仅持续约一周（约峰值质量返回时间的0.3倍）；4. 流自相互作用增加流入流的角动量，扩大近心点距离，减弱进动和冲击波，减少耗散。

Conclusion: 圆化过程可能本质缓慢，与相对论效应强度无关。在光学/紫外光度峰值期间，流动保持高偏心率且延展。这挑战了强相对论性TDEs会快速形成吸积盘的传统观点。

Abstract: Tidal disruption events (TDEs) occur when stars are destroyed by supermassive black holes and are among the brightest nuclear transients. It has been thought that strong relativistic effects rapidly dissipate orbital energy and produce prompt disk formation when the stellar pericenter is smaller than $\sim 10$ gravitational radii. Using a general relativistic hydrodynamic simulation of a strongly relativistic TDE around a $10^{6}\,M_{\odot}$ black hole, we find instead that the overall evolution is similar to weakly relativistic TDEs: the debris remains highly eccentric, with most of the returned mass residing near the orbital apocenter ($\sim 250\times$ the initial pericenter distance), and shocks, rather than accretion, power the event. The simulation starts from the initial stellar approach and follows the debris evolution up to $35\,\text{days}$ after the peak mass-return time ($\simeq$ $23\,\text{days}$). Although early shocks driven by strong relativistic apsidal precession and pericenter nozzle compression dissipate orbital energy efficiently, they last only about a week ($\sim 0.3$ of the peak mass-return time). Stream self-interactions increase the incoming stream's angular momentum, thereby expanding its pericenter distance, weakening precession and shocks, and reducing dissipation. These results, along with previous work on weakly relativistic TDEs, suggest that circularization may be intrinsically slow regardless of the strength of relativistic effects, and the flow remains highly eccentric and extended during the peak of optical/UV luminosity.

</details>

Method: 建立线性模式转换的统一理论，结合磁场几何和局部等离子体响应，分析磁场线曲率对A-X和O-X转换的影响，推导控制转换效率的无量纲参数，并与多能级量子系统的非绝热跃迁概率进行类比

Result: 磁场线曲率在低频诱导A-X转换，在高频诱导O-X转换；等离子体梯度单独不驱动X模式耦合；单个无量纲参数控制两种转换通道；转换效率遵循多能级量子系统的普遍非绝热跃迁概率；有效转换发生在波矢与磁场之间的狭窄角度窗口内

Conclusion: 该线性转换机制能够自然解释脉冲星和某些快速射电暴中观测到的复杂偏振特征，并将中子星磁层中可能的转换位置局部化，为理解这些天体物理现象提供了理论基础

Abstract: In neutron star (NS) magnetospheres, plasma waves propagate as normal modes with distinct propagation dynamics that strongly influence observable signals. This letter presents a unified theory of linear mode conversion between Alfv'en (A), superluminal ordinary (O), and extraordinary (X) modes, incorporating the effect of magnetic-field geometry and local plasma response. Magnetic field-line curvature induces A-X conversion for low frequencies and O-X conversion at high frequencies, whereas plasma gradients alone do not drive X-mode coupling. We show that a single dimensionless parameter controls both conversion channels. The conversion efficiency follows the universal nonadiabatic transition probability of a multilevel quantum system. Efficient conversion occurs within a narrow angular window between the wave vector and magnetic field, localizing potential conversion sites in the NS magnetosphere. This linear mechanism naturally accounts for complex polarization features observed in pulsars and some fast radio bursts.

</details>


### [90] [A variable ADAF disk model for X-ray binary systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10311)
*Chun Xu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 提出可变ADAF盘模型解释X射线双星系统观测现象，将ADAF湍流区大小变化与爆发周期、状态演化、铁线观测等统一起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型难以统一解释X射线双星系统的多种观测特征，如爆发周期、硬度-强度图状态演化、铁发射线观测等，需要建立更统一的物理框架。

Method: 提出可变ADAF盘模型，吸积流由外部薄盘和内部光学厚的ADAF环组成，ADAF湍流区大小可变化，通过ADAF收缩、向薄盘转变、再膨胀的完整循环解释观测现象。

Result: 模型成功解释黑洞双星X射线爆发的快速上升、峰值和衰减阶段，对应硬度-强度图中的低硬态-高软态-低硬态演化，统一了GX 339-4的铁线观测与截断盘范式，更好地解释了Her X-1的35天周期。

Conclusion: 可变ADAF框架为X射线双星系统提供了统一的物理解释，可扩展到活动星系核中类似现象的解释，建立了吸积流结构变化与观测特征之间的直接联系。

Abstract: We propose a variable ADAF disk model for X-ray binary systems. In this model, the accretion flow consists of an outer thin disk and an inner optically thick advection-dominated accretion flow (ADAF) torus. The size of the turbulent ADAF is variable. A complete cycle of ADAF contraction, transition to a thin disk, and subsequent re-expansion corresponds to the rapid rise, peak, and decay phases observed in the X-ray outbursts of black hole binaries. This cycle also tracks the canonical evolution through the low-hard, high-soft, and back to the low-hard state in the hardness-intensity diagram. The model unifies the presence of near-ISCO Fe emission lines with the truncated disk paradigm, as observed in the black hole system GX 339-4. It explains the 35-day period in the neutron star system Her X-1 more effectively through variable ADAF sizes than through a precessing disk. This variable ADAF framework may be extended to explain similar phenomena in active galactic nuclei.

</details>


### [91] [A candidate proton cyclotron feature in the ultraluminous X-ray source NGC 4656 ULX-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10331)
*Nelson Cruz-Sanchez,Enzo A. Saavedra,Federico A. Fogantini,Federico García,Jorge A. Combi,Matteo Bachetti,Matteo Imbrogno,Lara Sidoli,Alessio Marino*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 在NGC 4656 ULX-1中探测到3.29 keV窄吸收线，结合0.9736 Hz候选脉动，支持该超亮X射线源为强磁场中子星，局部磁场达6-7×10¹⁴ G。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超亮X射线源代表极端超爱丁顿吸积状态，其中一部分已知包含强磁化中子星，但对其表面磁场的直接观测证据仍然稀缺。

Method: 使用XMM-Newton/EPIC-pn观测NGC 4656 ULX-1，进行光谱分析探测吸收特征，并进行时序分析寻找脉动信号。通过蒙特卡洛模拟验证特征的统计显著性。

Result: 探测到3.29±0.02 keV的窄X射线吸收特征，统计显著性≥3σ；发现候选脉动信号频率约0.9736 Hz，局部显著性5.5σ，脉动分数约11%。

Conclusion: 吸收特征解释为质子回旋共振散射特征，表明线形成区域的局部磁场为6-7×10¹⁴ G，与中子星表面附近的强磁场一致，即使大尺度偶极场较弱。

Abstract: Ultraluminous X-ray sources represent extreme super-Eddington accretion regimes, and a subset is now known to host highly magnetized neutron stars. However, direct observational probes of their surface magnetic fields remain scarce. In this Letter, we report the detection of a narrow X-ray absorption feature at $3.29\pm0.02$ keV in the XMM$-$Newton/EPIC-pn spectrum of NGC 4656 ULX-1. The source exhibits a hard-ultraluminous state, while our timing analysis reveals a candidate pulsation at $\sim$0.9736 Hz, with a local significance of $5.5σ$ and a pulsed fraction of $\sim11\%$. The feature is robust against changes in continuum modeling and data-selection criteria, retaining a statistical significance of $\gtrsim3σ$ in Monte Carlo simulations. Interpreting the absorption as a proton cyclotron resonant scattering feature implies a local magnetic field of $B\sim(6-7)\times10^{14}$ G in the line-forming region. This value is consistent with strong magnetic fields anchored near the neutron star surface, even if the large-scale dipole is substantially weaker. Although we discuss electron cyclotron features and atomic transitions as possible alternatives, they appear less consistent with the observed phenomenology.

</details>


### [92] [The FAST Discovery of a binary millisecond pulsar PSR~J1647-0156B (M12B) with a candidate cross matching algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10347)
*Qiuyu Yu,Yujie Wang,Zhichen Pan,Zhongli Zhang,Lei Qian,Zhongzu Wu,Ralph P. Eatough,Dejiang Yin,Baoda Li,Yujie Chen,Yinfeng Dai,Yifeng Li*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 提出一种脉冲星候选体交叉匹配算法，用于从同一天空位置（如球状星团、高能源或超新星遗迹）的重复观测中筛选射电脉冲星搜索候选体。该方法利用候选体的自转周期和色散量值进行匹配，并通过FAST望远镜发现脉冲星M12B验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从同一天空位置的重复观测数据中高效识别脉冲星候选体，特别是针对球状星团、高能源和超新星遗迹等目标区域。传统方法可能遗漏微弱或间歇性信号，需要更系统的方法来交叉匹配不同观测中的候选体。

Method: 提出基于自转周期（P）和色散量（DM）的交叉匹配算法。匹配标准：不同观测中的两个或多个候选体，若自转周期差异在1%以内且色散量值差异在10%以内，则判定为同一候选体的检测。该方法应用于FAST望远镜的观测数据。

Result: 成功发现脉冲星M12B，验证了算法的有效性。该脉冲星特征：自转周期2.76ms，色散量42.70±0.05 cm⁻³ pc，三峰轮廓，信号微弱且显示闪烁现象，轨道周期约0.53天。表明算法能有效识别重复观测中的真实脉冲星信号。

Conclusion: 该交叉匹配算法能有效筛选重复观测中的脉冲星候选体，通过M12B的发现证明了其可行性。将该算法应用于其他球状星团观测档案的搜索结果，有望发现更多脉冲星，提高脉冲星探测效率。

Abstract: We propose a pulsar candidate cross matching algorithm to sift radio pulsar search candidates from repeated observations of the same sky location such as globular clusters, high energy sources, or supernova remnants. Our method uses both the candidate spin period ($P$) and dispersion measure (DM) value; if two or more candidates from different observations have similar spin periods to within 1\%, and dispersion measure values within 10\%, they are likely to correspond to the same candidate detection. We have demonstrated the effectiveness of our method through the discovery of the pulsar M12B with the Five-hundred-meter Aperture Spherical radio Telescope (FAST). This pulsar has a spin period of 2.76\,ms and a dispersion measure of $42.70 \pm 0.05\,\mathrm{cm}^{-3}~\mathrm{pc}$. This pulsar has a profile with three peaks, being faint, showing scintillation. It is in an approximately 0.53-day orbit. Our discovery indicates that more pulsars might be effectively discovered if the algorithm is applied to the search results from other archival globular cluster observations.

</details>


### [93] [Magnetic field strength constraints on $γ$-ray flaring regions in the flat spectrum radio quasar PKS 1222+216](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10393)
*Yeji Jo,Sang-Sung Lee*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 对PKS 1222+216的多波段研究显示，2014年γ射线耀发后射电流量呈指数衰减，喷流物理参数分析表明γ射线产生于距中心引擎约9.2pc处，磁場随距离呈B∝r^{-0.3}变化，支持移动与静止成分相互作用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究平谱射电类星体PKS 1222+216的长期多波段变化特征，特别是2014年γ射线耀发事件与射电波段变化的关联，探究喷流物理参数、γ射线产生机制及其在喷流中的位置。

Method: 使用iMOGABA、MOJAVE和VLBA-BU-BLAZAR项目2013-2020年的多频射电数据（15、22、43、86 GHz）以及Fermi-LAT的γ射线数据。通过VLBA 43 GHz数据的Gaussian模型拟合识别喷流成分，分析流量变化、冷却时标、喷流视角、磁场强度等物理参数。

Result: 2014年11月γ射线耀发后，射电流量在15-86 GHz范围内呈指数衰减37%-56%；识别出10个喷流成分，冷却时标43-222天；喷流视角约8°，磁场强度77-134 mG；磁场随距离变化为B∝r^{-0.3±0.04}；γ射线产生区位于距中心引擎9.2±1.0 pc处，与移动成分C9和静止特征A1相互作用时间相符。

Conclusion: γ射线产生于喷流下游（距中心引擎约9.2 pc），通过移动与静止成分相互作用机制产生，逆康普顿散射的种子光子可能来自喷流自身、宇宙微波背景或周围鞘层。研究提出了一种基于同步辐射冷却时标估算活动星系核磁场强度的新方法。

Abstract: We present a multi-wavelength study of the Flat Spectrum Radio Quasar PKS 1222+216, analyzing its long-term variability of radio data obtained in 2013-2020 from the iMOGABA, MOJAVE, and VLBA-BU-BLAZAR programs, along with $γ$-ray data from Fermi-LAT. We found that the radio flux densities at 15, 22, 43, and 86 GHz declined exponentially by 37%-56% over a year following a $γ$-ray flare in November 2014. We estimated jet physical parameters through Gaussian model fitting of VLBA 43 GHz data, identifying 10 jet components. The cooling timescales of the jet emission regions, i.e., newly ejected components C9, C10, and C11, range from 43 to 222 days, with the estimated jet viewing angles of approximately 8 degrees and magnetic field strengths of 77-134 mG in the jet emission regions. Additionally, by determining the magnetic field strength at different frequencies, we found that the magnetic field scales as $B\propto r^{-0.3\pm0.04}$, indicating a non-equipartition condition ($k_\text{r}\gtrsim 1$) or a slow decline in magnetic field strength profile ($m<1$). By analyzing component ejection times, we discovered that the $γ$-ray flare in 2014 coincided with the interaction between the moving component C9 and the stationary feature A1. We estimated that the $γ$-ray emission region is located at $9.2\pm1.0$ pc from the central engine, beyond the BLR and dusty torus, suggesting that the seed photons for inverse Compton scattering originate from the jet itself, external CMB radiation, or a surrounding sheath. Our results favor a scenario where $γ$-ray emission originates further downstream from the central engine through interactions between moving and stationary components. Additionally, our study presents an alternative method for estimating magnetic field strengths in AGNs undergoing long-term synchrotron cooling based on the associated timescale.

</details>


### [94] [Multi-wavelength emission modelling of PSR~J0437$-$4715](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10536)
*J. Pétri,P. Stammler,L. Guillemot,S. Guillot,D. González-Caniulef,F. Jankowski,N. Webb*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过结合X射线热点、射电和γ射线脉冲轮廓拟合，确定了毫秒脉冲星J0437-4715的磁场几何结构，发现一个略微偏离中心的偶极子加上一个小尺度偶极子能同时解释所有观测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫秒脉冲星的光变曲线和偏振特性多样性源于其表面附近的磁场结构复杂性。本研究旨在通过结合多种观测数据来揭示PSR J0437-4715的磁场几何结构，以理解其发射特性。

Method: 结合NICER观测的软X射线热点几何建模、射电和γ射线脉冲轮廓拟合，使用条纹风模型处理γ射线光变曲线，旋转矢量模型处理射电偏振数据，通过偶极力自由磁层模拟获取磁层结构。

Result: 发现一个略微偏离中心的偶极子加上位于一个极冠的小尺度偶极子能同时解释热点形状、射电和γ射线数据，得到磁倾角α≈(42±5)°和视线倾角ζ≈(136±5)°。该简单偶极模型能再现所有射电和γ射线特性。

Conclusion: 研究证实了PSR J0437-4715的磁场几何结构，表明射电发射可能产生于磁场主要为偶极性质的区域，为理解毫秒脉冲星的复杂发射特性提供了重要线索。

Abstract: The diversity of pulsar light-curves and radio polarisation properties originates in the structure of the magnetic field close to the stellar surface. For millisecond pulsars, this complexity is particularly puzzling. Fortunately, some means exist to uncover the magnetic field topology which indeed impacts the emission within the magnetosphere but also on the surface through its hot spot thermal radiation. We aim at deducing a plausible magnetic field geometry for the millisecond pulsar J0437$-$4715 by using combined information from the soft X-ray hot spot geometry deduced from NICER observations by pulse profile modelling and from radio and $γ$-ray pulse profile fitting. We also check the consistency between the geometry obtained and the radio polarisation data. Our $γ$-ray light-curve shapes rely on the striped wind model, whereas the radio polarisation fits rely on the rotating vector model. The magnetosphere structure is obtained from dipolar force-free magnetosphere simulations. We demonstrate that a slightly off-centred dipole augmented by a small scale dipole located on one polar cap explains simultaneously the shape of the hot spot and the radio and $γ$-ray data with a magnetic obliquity of $α\approx (42\pm5) \degr$ and a line-of-sight inclination angle of $ζ\approx (136 \pm5) \degr$. Our simple dipole model reproduces all the radio and $γ$-ray characteristics of PSR~J0437$-$4715, including its radio polarisation data. It shows that the radio emission could be produced in regions where the magnetic field is mainly of dipolar nature.

</details>


### [95] [Mass measurements of the double neutron star system PSR J0641+0448](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10788)
*Z. L. Yang,J. L. Han,P. F. Wang,C. Wang,N. N. Cai,W. C. Jing,W. Q. Su,T. Wang,J. Xu,Yi Yan,D. J. Zhou*

TL;DR: FAST望远镜发现双中子星系统PSR J0641+0448，通过脉冲星计时获得精确质量测量，验证中子星质量与轨道偏心率的关联性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双中子星系统是研究中子星质量分布的最佳方法之一，通过精确测量这类系统的参数可以更好地理解中子星的形成和演化机制

Method: 利用FAST望远镜的GPPS巡天发现PSR J0641+0448，通过FAST观测获得相位连接计时解，检测到近星点进动和夏皮罗延迟，采用DDGR模型进行χ²分析

Result: 测得脉冲星质量为1.319^{+0.021}_{-0.035} M⊙，伴星质量为1.269^{+0.022}_{-0.016} M⊙（68.3%置信水平），伴星质量较低且轨道偏心率适中，符合中子星质量与轨道偏心率的关联性

Conclusion: FAST望远镜成功发现并精确测量了双中子星系统PSR J0641+0448的质量参数，为研究中子星质量分布和双星演化提供了重要数据支持

Abstract: Pulsar timing of double neutron star (DNS) systems is one of the best methodologies to study the neutron star masses distribution. Here we report the discovery of a double neutron star system PSR J0641+0448 in the Five-hundred-meter Aperture Spherical radio Telescope (FAST) Galactic Plane Pulsar Snapshot (GPPS) survey. This pulsar has a 25.7 ms spin period and moves in a 3.73-days eccentric orbit with an eccentricity of 0.145. Using FAST observations, we obtained its phase-connected timing solution with periastron advance and Shapiro delay detected. Using $χ^2$ analysis based on DDGR model, we constrain the pulsar mass to $1.319^{+0.021}_{-0.035}~M_\odot$, and the companion mass to $1.269^{+0.022}_{-0.016}~M_\odot$ with a 68.3\% confidence level. The low companion mass and mild orbital eccentricity is consistent with the correlation between neutron masses and orbital eccentricities.

</details>


### [96] [Electromagnetic Signatures of Supermassive Binary Black Holes. I. Thermal Synchrotron, Self-Lensing Flares, and Jet Precession](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10792)
*Hong-Xuan Jiang,Xinyu Li,Yosuke Mizuno,Ziri Younsi,Christian M. Fromm*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究通过3D GRMHD模拟探索超大质量双黑洞系统的电磁对应体特征，发现MAD湍流可能掩盖激波诱导的耀斑，但自透镜效应可产生独特快速耀斑，建议协调亚毫米波和近红外监测以识别双黑洞特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星计时阵列探测到纳赫兹引力波背景，迫切需要识别超大质量双黑洞的电磁对应体，以确认引力波源并研究双黑洞系统物理特性。

Method: 采用全局3D广义相对论磁流体动力学模拟，研究质量比q=0.1的次级黑洞与主黑洞磁滞吸积盘的相互作用，使用随时间演化的叠加Kerr-Schild度规，并进行基于热电子分布函数的后处理广义相对论辐射转移计算，探索三种轨道构型：垂直撞击轨道、共面嵌入轨道、高自旋偏心倾斜轨道。

Result: 1) 尽管存在清晰的轨道周期性和反复激波形成，热同步辐射光变曲线常缺乏预期的激波诱导耀斑；2) 垂直撞击中激波增亮通常弱于主黑洞MAD的随机变化，除非在特定对齐相位观测；3) 共面轨道产生由引力自透镜驱动的独特快速耀斑；4) 发现频率依赖的发射层次：主黑洞主导亚毫米波通量，次级黑洞因热电子分布函数中更高电子温度主导近红外发射；5) 自旋轨道耦合驱动Lense-Thirring进动，产生类似OJ~287的扭曲摆动喷流。

Conclusion: 固有MAD湍流可能轻易掩盖射电频率的激波诱导耀斑，强烈建议协调亚毫米波和近红外监测以稳健分离超大质量双黑洞自透镜特征，为识别引力波源的电磁对应体提供关键观测策略。

Abstract: The recent evidence for a nanohertz gravitational wave background from Pulsar Timing Arrays highlights the urgent need to identify electromagnetic counterparts to supermassive binary black holes. Here, we perform global 3D general relativistic magnetohydrodynamic (GRMHD) simulations of a secondary black hole (mass ratio $q=0.1$) interacting with a Magnetically Arrested Disk around a primary black hole using a time-dependent superposed Kerr-Schild metric and post-processed general relativistic radiation transfer calculations based on thermal electron distribution function (eDF). We explore three orbital configurations: a vertical impact orbit, a coplanar embedded orbit, and a high-spin, eccentric, inclined scenario. Despite clear orbital periodicity and recurrent shock formation, the thermal synchrotron light curves frequently lack expected shock-induced flares. In vertical impacts, shock brightenings are typically sub-dominant to the stochastic MAD variability of the primary black hole, unless viewed at specific alignment phases. Conversely, coplanar orbits produce distinctive, rapid flares driven by gravitational self-lensing. We identify a frequency-dependent emission hierarchy: the primary black hole dominates sub-millimeter flux, while the secondary dominates near-infrared emission due to higher electron temperatures in thermal eDF. Finally, spin-orbit coupling drives Lense-Thirring precession, yielding twisted, wobbling jets reminiscent of OJ~287. Crucially, we demonstrate that intrinsic MAD turbulence may easily mask shock-induced flares at radio frequencies. We strongly advocate coordinated sub-millimeter and near-infrared monitoring to robustly isolate supermassive binary black hole self-lensing signatures.

</details>


### [97] [X-ray polarization in the soft state of Cyg X-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10870)
*A. Niedzwiecki,M. Szanecki,A. Veledina,A. A. Zdziarski,A. Chakraborty,J. Poutanen,P. Lubinski,A. Salganik*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究通过多卫星X射线观测数据，结合新开发的retBB模型，确定了天鹅座X-1软态X射线发射和偏振的物理机制：来自以约0.3c速度准相对论性外流冕区的康普顿化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在确定天鹅座X-1在软态下观测到的X射线发射和偏振的物理机制。虽然已有多种模型解释黑洞双星系统的X射线谱，但偏振观测为区分不同物理过程提供了关键约束。

Method: 1. 使用NICER、NuSTAR、INTEGRAL和IXPE在2023年6月20日的同步观测数据，辅以CGRO档案数据；2. 开发新模型retBB描述热盘发射及其返回反射；3. 结合精确的康普顿化和相对论性展宽反射模型进行光谱分析；4. 基于光谱解计算预期偏振信号，并与IXPE测量值比较。

Result: 1. 观测到的偏振由以约0.3c速度准相对论性外流冕区的康普顿化过程产生；2. 光谱解允许低或高黑洞自旋值，取决于采用的模型设置；3. 观测偏振强烈支持低自旋，因为高自旋会导致偏振角在能带内显著旋转，与观测不符；4. 除偏振角旋转外，广义相对论效应对观测偏振产生的影响不显著；5. 返回盘辐射贡献最多仅为次要量。

Conclusion: 天鹅座X-1软态的X射线偏振主要由准相对论性外流冕区的康普顿化过程主导，而非广义相对论效应或返回盘辐射。偏振观测为约束黑洞自旋提供了独立证据，强烈支持低自旋解。

Abstract: We aim to identify the physical mechanism responsible for the observed X-ray emission and polarization of Cyg X-1 in the soft state. We perform a detailed spectral analysis of X-ray data obtained by NICER, NuSTAR, and INTEGRAL during observations simultaneous with IXPE on 2023 June 20, supplemented at higher energies with archival CGRO data. We develop a new model, retBB, that describes thermal disk emission and its returning reflection, and apply it together with accurate models of Comptonization and relativistically broadened reflection. Using the resulting spectral solution, we compute the expected polarization signal and compare it with the IXPE measurements. Our spectropolarimetric modeling shows that the observed polarization is produced by Comptonization in a corona undergoing a semi-relativistic outflow with a velocity of ~0.3c. Our spectral solutions admit either low or high black-hole spin values, depending on the adopted model setup. However, the observed polarization strongly favors a low spin. At high spin, the polarization angle would inevitably rotate significantly across the energy band, which is not consistent with the observations. Apart from this rotation of the polarization angle, general relativistic effects do not play a significant role in producing the observed polarization. In particular, we find that, at most, returning disk radiation contributes only a minor amount.

</details>


### [98] [Universal behaviour of $α$-viscosity in black hole accretion discs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10997)
*Marek A. Abramowicz,Axel Brandenburg,Jiří Horák,Debora Lančová,John C. Miller,Ewa Szuszkiewicz,Maciek Wielgus*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文分析了吸积盘理论中α粘滞系数的演化，从最初的常数假设到现代GRMHD模拟揭示其在吸积盘中的变化规律，并提出了基于"回转半径"的α公式来描述这一普遍行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shakura-Sunyaev的α粘滞系数在吸积盘理论发展中起重要作用，但原始假设α=常数并不符合实际情况。现代GRMHD模拟显示α在吸积盘中变化显著，需要建立更准确的描述模型来改进理论分析。

Method: 通过分析不同研究者的GRMHD模拟结果，发现应力在黑洞视界处为零，在光子圆轨道附近达到最大值。基于这一观测，提出使用"回转半径"这一牛顿力学和Kerr时空几何中的物理特征量来构建α的解析公式。

Result: 发现α在吸积盘中变化约一个数量级：远离黑洞时较小，在靠近黑洞的坠入区域较大。应力在黑洞视界处为零，在光子圆轨道附近达到最大值。提出的α公式能够准确描述这一普遍行为。

Conclusion: GRMHD模拟不应取代解析模型，而是应帮助改进它们。从GRMHD模拟推导出的α公式可用于构建更现实的薄盘和厚盘模型，为黑洞吸积盘理论研究提供更准确的工具。

Abstract: The Shakura-Sunyaev $α$-viscosity coefficient, defined as the ratio of total stress to total pressure, $α= \mathbb{T}/p$, played an important role in the development of the accretion disc theory in the early 1970s. The origin of turbulence that causes the stress $\mathbb{T}$ was unknown at that time. Shakura and Sunyaev assumed $α=$ const. Today we know that this was not quite realistic - the modern general relativistic magneto-hydrodynamic simulations (GRMHD) of black hole accretion discs revealed that $α$ changes by about an order of magnitude within the disc, being smaller far away from the black hole and larger in the plunging region close in. It was found that the behaviour of $α$ reflects some underlying, fundamental properties of the stress $\mathbb{T}$ itself. In particular, as argued by several authors, the stress must be zero at the black hole horizon. We notice that the stress calculated in GRMHD simulations by different authors, including us, has a maximum rather close to the location of the circular photon orbit. We propose a formula that accurately describes this universal behaviour of $α$ in terms of the "gyration radius'', a physical characteristic of rotation well known in Newtonian dynamics and in the black hole case uniquely defined by the Kerr space-time geometry. Analytic and semi-analytic models of black hole accretion discs provide an invaluable insight into fundamental physics, and the GRMHD simulations do not aspire to replace them. Rather, simulations could help to improve analytic models by making them more realistic. For example, our $α$-formula, deduced from the GRMHD simulations, may be handy in the construction of improved versions of thin and slim disc models.

</details>


### [99] [XMM-Newton Observation and Optical Monitoring of the Candidate Redback Millisecond Pulsar 1FGL J0523.5$-$2529](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11028)
*J. P. Halpern,S. Bogdanov*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 对1FGL J0523.5-2529（费米选定的红背毫秒脉冲星候选体）进行首次覆盖其完整16.5小时轨道的同步X射线和U波段观测，发现X射线处于中间态并存在频繁耀发，U波段主要受伴星椭球调制支配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1FGL J0523.5-2529是一个在2020-2021年表现出明亮光学和X射线耀发的红背毫秒脉冲星候选体。先前对其轨道相位的射电脉冲搜索均未成功，且缺乏覆盖完整轨道的同步多波段观测数据，需要深入研究其X射线和光学特性以理解其物理机制。

Method: 使用XMM-Newton在2025年对1FGL J0523.5-2529进行了首次覆盖其完整16.5小时轨道的同步X射线和U波段观测。同时利用10年的ATLAS监测数据，通过伴星椭球调制效应精确测量了轨道周期。

Result: X射线光度处于中间态，幂律光子谱指数Γ=1.53±0.02。X射线光变曲线在宽的单峰调制上叠加了频繁的耀发，这种单峰调制特征符合脉冲星周围激波包裹的模型。U波段光变主要受伴星椭球调制支配。通过ATLAS数据将轨道周期精度提高到0.6881366(19)天。

Conclusion: 观测支持激波包裹脉冲星的模型，耀发可能由激波伴星风中的密度增强引起。尽管射电脉冲搜索未成功，但激波风通常包围着脉冲星。伴星几乎充满洛希瓣，其椭球调制主导了光学波段的光变。

Abstract: 1FGL J0523.5$-$2529 is a Fermi selected redback millisecond pulsar candidate that exhibited luminous optical and X-ray flares in 2020-2021. We obtained a simultaneous X-ray and $U$-band observation with XMM-Newton in 2025, the first to cover the 16.5 hr orbit of 1FGL J0523.5$-$2529. The X-ray luminosity was in an intermediate state with a power-law photon spectral index of $Γ=1.53\pm0.02$. Frequent flares were superposed on a broad, single-peaked modulation, the latter characteristic of intrabinary shock models in which the shock front is wrapped around the pulsar. We speculate that density enhancements in the shocked companion wind cause flares, as well as variable optical recombination lines. The $U$-band light curve was dominated by ellipsoidal modulation of the nearly Roche lobe filling companion star, similar to that seen in ground-based optical photometry. We also used this effect in 10 years of ATLAS monitoring to improve the precision of the orbital period to 0.6881366(19) days. Considering that searches for radio pulsations from 1FGL J0523.5$-$2529 at all orbital phases have been unsuccessful, the shocked wind usually surrounds the pulsar.

</details>

### [100] [Large chirotopes with computable numbers of triangulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10251)
*Mathilde Bouvel,Valentin Féray,Xavier Goaoc,Florent Koechlin*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 该论文研究手性拓扑的分解方法及其在计算平面点集三角剖分数目中的应用，通过推广Rutschmann和Wettstein的凸凹和操作，利用函数方程和核方法获得了双圆点集三角剖分数的精确渐近估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究手性拓扑的分解方法，旨在解决计算给定平面点集支持的三角剖分数目这一组合几何问题。手性拓扑作为平面点集的组合抽象，其分解方法有助于分析复杂点集的三角剖分计数。

Method: 推广Rutschmann和Wettstein为特定手性拓扑族（称为链）定义的凸凹和操作，将分解方法扩展到更一般的手性拓扑。使用函数方程和核方法进行数学分析，以获得三角剖分数的渐近估计。

Result: 获得了双圆点集三角剖分数的精确渐近估计。通过推广的分解方法和核方法的应用，成功计算了这一特定点集配置的三角剖分数量渐近行为。

Conclusion: 手性拓扑的分解方法为计算平面点集三角剖分数提供了有效的组合工具。推广的凸凹和操作以及核方法的应用使得能够精确估计双圆点集等复杂配置的三角剖分数量，为组合几何中的计数问题提供了新的分析框架。

Abstract: Chirotopes are a common combinatorial abstraction of (planar) point sets. In this paper we investigate decomposition methods for chirotopes, and their application to the problem of counting the number of triangulations supported by a given planar point set. In particular, we generalize the convex and concave sums operations defined by Rutschmann and Wettstein for a particular family of chirotopes (which they call chains), and obtain a precise asymptotic estimate for the number of triangulations of the double circle, using a functional equation and the kernel method.

</details>

### [101] [A brief history of Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10762)
*N. Cartiglia*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该综述追溯了粒子物理实验中精密定时技术的发展历程，从1990年代闪烁体和光电倍增管系统的大规模应用，到未来对撞机皮秒级精度探测器，识别了四个技术代际。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梳理粒子物理实验中精密定时技术的演进脉络，识别关键技术发展阶段，为未来探测器研发提供历史视角和方向指引。

Method: 采用历史回顾和代际划分的方法，将精密定时技术发展分为四个阶段：离散电子学与PMT时代、硅探测器革命、4D跟踪过渡期、以及未来设施需求。

Result: 识别出四个明确的技术代际：(i)离散电子学与PMT时代，建立定时三大应用；(ii)硅探测器革命(SiPMs、LGADs、ASICs)；(iii)向4D跟踪过渡，HL-LHC等系统实现30-50ps精度；(iv)未来设施需要10ps级精度，设定未来十年研发议程。

Conclusion: 精密定时技术经历了从PMT到硅探测器再到4D跟踪的演进，当前技术已成熟至30-50ps精度，未来需要向10ps精度迈进以满足Muon Collider和FCC等设施需求。

Abstract: This review traces the evolution of precision timing in particle physics experiments, from the first large-scale applications of scintillator and photomultiplier tube (PMT) systems in the 1990s to the picosecond-precision detectors of future colliders. Four technological generations are identified: (i) the era of discrete electronics (NIM, CAMAC, VME) and PMTs, which established the three canonical uses of timing -- particle identification via time-of-flight, background and pile-up rejection, and directionality triggering; (ii) the silicon revolution enabled by Silicon Photomultipliers (SiPMs), Low-Gain Avalanche Diodes (LGADs), and Application-Specific Integrated Circuits (ASICs); (iii) the current transition to ubiquitous four-dimensional (4D) tracking, in which time is a coordinate measured at every point along a particle trajectory. Under-construction systems at the HL-LHC (CMS MTD, ATLAS HGTD, CMS HGCAL) demonstrate the maturity of 30--50\,ps silicon timing at the million-channel scale. The EIC, LHCb VELO Upgrade~II, and ALICE~3 push this technology into new regimes of radiation hardness, material budget, and granularity. (iv) The Far-future facilities such as the Muon Collider and FCC require a further leap to $\sim$10\,ps, setting the agenda for the next decade of detector R\&D.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

Method: 利用KEKB对撞机上的Belle探测器收集的1.58亿个Υ(2S)衰变事件，搜索χ_{bJ}(1P)介子衰变为带电轻子对(e^±μ^∓, e^±τ^∓, μ^±τ^∓)的过程

Result: 未观察到显著信号，为χ_{bJ}(1P)衰变到e^±μ^∓设定了约10^{-6}量级的分支比上限，对e^±τ^∓或μ^±τ^∓设定了约10^{-5}量级的上限。将χ_{b0}(1P)衰变的限制转化为标量算符Wilson系数的约束

Conclusion: 首次对χ_{bJ}(1P)介子衰变中的带电轻子味破坏过程进行了实验搜索，为相关新物理模型提供了重要约束，为进一步探索轻子味破坏现象奠定了基础

Abstract: We report the first searches for charged-lepton-flavor violation in decays of $χ_{bJ}(1P)$ ($J=0, 1,$ and $2$) to a pair of charged leptons using 158 million $Υ(2S)$ decays collected with the Belle detector in $e^+e^-$ collisions at the KEKB collider. No significant signal is observed, and we set upper limits on the branching fractions for $χ_{bJ}(1P)$ decays to $e^\pmμ^\mp$ at the level of $10^{-6}$ and to $e^\pmτ^\mp$ or $μ^\pmτ^\mp$ at the level of $10^{-5}$. Limits on $χ_{b0}(1P)$ decays are translated into bounds on the corresponding Wilson coefficients of scalar operators that mediate charged-lepton-flavor violation.

</details>


### [103] [Why (and How) LGADs Work: Ionization, Space Charge, and Gain Saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10841)
*N. Cartiglia,A. R. Altamura,R. Arcidiacono,M. Durando,S. Galletto,M. Ferrero,L. Lanteri,A. Losana,L. Massaccesi,L. Menzio,F. Siviero,V. Sola,R. White*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: LGAD/UFSD时间分辨率受抖动和朗道噪声影响，研究发现仅模拟初始电离会高估朗道噪声，需要加入漂移过程中的空间电荷效应和倍增过程中的增益饱和效应才能准确模拟实验数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低增益雪崩探测器（LGAD/UFSD）的时间分辨率受两种因素影响：电子噪声和信号斜率引起的抖动，以及最小电离粒子非均匀能量沉积引起的朗道噪声。现有模拟方法仅考虑初始电离会显著高估朗道噪声，需要更精确的物理模型来解释实验观测。

Method: 在快速模拟程序Weightfield2（WF2）中实现了完整的物理模型，包括：1）初始电离模拟；2）电子/空穴漂移过程中的空间电荷效应，平滑初始电荷分布；3）倍增过程中的增益饱和效应，抑制大振幅涨落。模型通过多个独立实验验证。

Result: 模型成功再现了实验观测：1）电荷分布随增益的演化；2）朗道尾事件的时间分辨率；3）厚度对时间性能的依赖性。同时提出了基于增益饱和的数据驱动增益测量方法，并讨论了增益层设计的影响。

Conclusion: 准确模拟LGAD/UFSD的时间分辨率需要同时考虑空间电荷效应和增益饱和效应。仅模拟初始电离会高估朗道噪声。该模型为探测器设计和性能优化提供了重要指导，特别是对增益层设计和时间分辨率改进具有重要意义。

Abstract: The temporal resolution of Low-Gain Avalanche Detectors (LGADs), also known as Ultra-Fast Silicon Detectors (UFSDs), is governed by two contributions: jitter, arising from electronic noise and signal slew rate, and the Landau noise term, arising from the non-uniform energy deposition of minimum ionizing particles (MIPs). We show that a correct simulation of the initial ionization alone significantly overestimates the measured Landau noise. Two additional physical mechanisms are necessary to reproduce the data: space charge effects during electron/hole drift, which smooth the granularity of the initial charge distribution, and gain saturation during multiplication, which preferentially suppresses large-amplitude fluctuations. All steps of the model have been implemented in the fast simulation program Weightfield2 (WF2). The model is validated against several independent experimental observations: the evolution of the measured charge distribution with gain, the temporal resolution of events in the Landau tail, and the thickness dependence of timing performance. We also discuss a data-driven gain measurement method based on gain saturation, and implications for gain layer design.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量B_s^0→D_s^-π^+衰变中的未标记CP不对称性，直接约束树图级b强子衰变中的新物理效应

Method: 使用LHCb实验在2016-2018年收集的质子-质子对撞数据，质心能量13 TeV，总积分亮度5.4 fb^{-1}，分析两种D_s^-衰变模式：D_s^-→K^-K^+π^-和D_s^-→π^-π^+π^-

Result: 测量得到的未标记CP不对称性为⟨A^s_untagged⟩ = (-1.4±5.9(stat)±1.1(syst))×10^{-3}，与标准模型预期一致

Conclusion: 测量结果与标准模型预期相符，为树图级b强子衰变中的新物理提供了直接约束

Abstract: A measurement of the flavour-untagged decay-time-integrated ${C\!P}$ asymmetry in the flavour-specific decay ${B_s^0 \to D_s^-π^+}$, ${\langle A^s_{\rm untagged}\rangle}$, is performed using proton-proton collision data collected by the LHCb experiment between 2016 and 2018 at a center-of-mass energy of ${13\,{\rm TeV}}$, corresponding to a total integrated luminosity of ${5.4\,{\rm fb}^{-1}}$. The ${C\!P}$ asymmetry is measured in two $D_s^-$ meson decay modes, ${D_s^- \to K^-K^+π^-}$ and ${D_s^- \to π^-π^+π^-}$. The combined result, $\langle A^s_{\rm untagged}\rangle = ( -1.4 \pm 5.9\,\rm{(stat)} \pm 1.1\,\rm{(syst)}) \times 10^{-3}$, is consistent with the Standard Model expectation and provides a direct constraint on new physics in tree-level $b$-hadron decays.

</details>


### [105] [First Axion Search Results of the SUPAX Prototype Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10943)
*Tim Schneemann,Hendrik Bekker,Dmitry Budker,Kristof Schmieden,Matthias Schott,Malavika Unni,Arne Wickenbrock*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: Supax原型实验使用铜腔在2K温度和12T磁场下探测约34μeV质量的轴子，排除了特定范围的轴子-光子耦合常数和暗光子混合参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为未来Supax实验（探测8-30μeV质量范围的轴子类粒子）开发原型系统，验证实验方案并获取初步物理结果。

Method: 采用haloscope型探测器原型，使用铜腔冷却至2K温度，在12T磁场中运行，探测约34μeV质量的轴子类粒子。

Result: 排除了轴子-光子耦合常数|g_{aγγ}| > 1.6×10^{-13} GeV^{-1}，同时排除了暗光子的动能混合参数χ > 1.4×10^{-12}。

Conclusion: 原型实验验证了Supax实验方案的可行性，为未来更大规模的实验奠定了基础，并获得了对轴子和暗光子的物理限制。

Abstract: The SUPerconduction AXion search experiment (Supax) is a future haloscope-type detector designed to probe axion-like particles (ALPs) as candidates for dark matter and solutions to the strong-CP problem in the mass range between $8\,μ$eV and $30\,μ$eV. In the course of the preparation of Supax, a prototype experiment was built and operated. Using a copper cavity, cooled down to a temperature of 2 K and operated in a magnetic field of 12 T, we probe axion masses around $34\,μ$eV and exclude axion-photon couplings down to $|g_{aγγ}|> 1.6\cdot 10^{-13}$GeV$^{-1}$. The data was also used to exclude dark photons in the same mass range with a kinetic mixing parameter of $χ> 1.4\cdot 10^{-12}$. Details of the experimental setup and the analysis strategy are summarized in this paper.

</details>

### [106] [Symmetric localization of $ν_{\text{tot}}=4/3$ fractional topological insulator edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10103)
*Yang-Zhi Chou,Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究基于扭曲MoTe₂实验，构建了ν_tot=4/3分数量子拓扑绝缘体的无序相互作用边缘理论，揭示了边缘输运不足以识别该拓扑相


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受最近扭曲MoTe₂实验的启发，研究分数量子拓扑绝缘体在ν_tot=4/3时的边缘物理，探索其输运特性与拓扑识别的关系

Method: 构建无序相互作用边缘理论，考虑S_z守恒和S_z破坏扰动（如Rashba自旋轨道耦合），通过精确映射到非相互作用费米子理论展示安德森局域化

Result: 在S_z守恒边缘发现三种相，长边缘极限下每边有两种可能电导值：2/3 e²/h和4/3 e²/h；存在相互作用诱导的绝缘边缘态而不破坏时间反演或电荷守恒对称性；边缘输运测量不足以识别ν_tot=4/3分数量子拓扑绝缘体

Conclusion: 该工作展示了分数量子拓扑绝缘体边缘物理的复杂性，强调仅靠两终端输运测量无法完全表征拓扑相，为实验识别提供了重要理论指导

Abstract: Motivated by the recent twisted MoTe$_2$ experiment [arXiv:2601.18508], we develop a disordered interacting edge theory of a fractional topological insulator at $ν_{\text{tot}}=4/3$, consisting of two time-reversal-conjugated $ν=2/3$ fractional quantum Hall states. For an $S_z$-conserving edge, we uncover three distinct phases with two possible conductance values per edge in the long-edge limit: $\frac{2}{3}\frac{e^2}{h}$ and $\frac{4}{3}\frac{e^2}{h}$. In the presence of $S_z$-changing perturbations (e.g., Rashba spin-orbit coupling), an interaction-induced insulating edge state can emerge without breaking time-reversal or charge-conservation symmetry, corresponding to the absence of a topologically protected edge state. We further provide an exact mapping to a noninteracting fermionic theory exhibiting Anderson localization. Our results showcase an explicit, experimentally relevant example that the edge-state two-terminal transport is insufficient to identify the $ν_{\text{tot}}=4/3$ fractional topological insulators.

</details>


### [107] [Classical Kitaev model in a magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10112)
*Paul A. McClarty,Roderich Moessner,Karlo Penc,Jeffrey G. Rau*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 经典Kitaev蜂窝模型在磁场中的分析：存在有限磁场窗口的自旋液体相，揭示了基态约束条件，解释了热容和磁化率的精确低温行为，发现有限磁场下自旋和四极关联均为短程，并证明了弱位点稀释不影响自旋液体中的磁化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受具有Kitaev交换的自旋轨道耦合磁体在磁场中实验的启发，研究经典Kitaev蜂窝模型在磁场中的行为，探索自旋液体相在磁场中的存在性和特性。

Method: 对经典Kitaev蜂窝模型在磁场存在下的理论分析，包括基态约束条件的推导、热容和磁化率低温行为的精确计算、关联函数的分析，以及通过粗粒化理论解释四极关联的有效质量，最后研究弱位点稀释效应。

Result: 发现存在从零场到有限阈值的磁场窗口内的自旋液体相；揭示了基态自旋需要满足的约束条件，这些约束决定了自旋液体中热容和磁化率作为磁场函数的精确低温行为；有限磁场下自旋和四极关联均为短程（与零场情况不同）；通过具有涨落有效电荷自由度的粗粒化理论合理化四极关联的有效质量；弱位点稀释不改变自旋液体中的磁化，表现出"完美"补偿效应。

Conclusion: 经典Kitaev蜂窝模型在有限磁场下存在自旋液体相，其热力学性质由基态约束精确决定；有限磁场改变了关联性质，使自旋和四极关联变为短程；系统对弱位点稀释具有鲁棒性，磁化不受影响，这为实验观测提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Motivated by experiments on spin-orbit coupled magnets with Kitaev exchange in magnetic fields, we present an analysis of the classical Kitaev honeycomb model in the presence of a magnetic field. We show that there is a spin liquid regime that exists within a finite window of fields from zero up to a finite threshold before transitioning into the polarized paramagnet. We uncover constraints that spins need to satisfy in the ground state and show that they determine the exact limiting zero temperature behavior of the heat capacity and magnetic susceptibility within the spin liquid as a function of field. When the field is finite, both the two-point spin and the quadrupolar correlations are short-ranged, in contrast to the zero-field case. We rationalize an effective mass for the quadrupolar correlations in terms of a coarse-grained theory with fluctuating effective charge degrees of freedom. Finally, we show that weak site-dilution does not change the magnetization within the spin liquid -- a kind of "perfect" compensation of the site dilution.

</details>


### [108] [Disorder-induced localisation in the Mott-Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10238)
*Ričards Kristers Knipšis,Friedemann Queisser,Jesumony Jayabalan,Gael Reecht,Manuel Gruber,Uwe Bovensiepen,Ralf Schützhold*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究无序对Mott绝缘体相中Fermi-Hubbard模型的双子-空穴准粒子激发的影响，分析电荷无序和自旋无序两种类型对局域化行为的不同效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无序如何影响Mott绝缘体相中的准粒子激发，特别是比较电荷无序和自旋无序对双子(doublon)和空穴(holon)准粒子局域化行为的差异。

Method: 采用关联层次方法研究无序系统，分析两种无序类型：1)电荷无序（在位势随机性），2)自旋无序（固定随机背景自旋排列）。通过分析准粒子本征谱并量化相应本征态的局域化程度，同时使用强耦合微扰理论进行计算并与关联层次方法结果比较。

Result: 电荷无序导致局域态和退局域态在能量和空间上分离，而自旋无序在整个准粒子能带中产生局域态。两种方法（关联层次和强耦合微扰理论）的计算结果进行了比较。

Conclusion: 不同类型的无序对Mott绝缘体相中准粒子激发产生显著不同的局域化行为：电荷无序引起局域态和退局域态的分离，而自旋无序导致整个能带的局域化。

Abstract: For the Fermi-Hubbard model in the Mott insulator phase, we employ the hierarchy of correlations to study how doublon and holon quasi-particle excitations are affected by adding disorder to the system. We study two types of disorder: charge disorder, in the form of on-site potential randomness; and spin disorder, in the form of a fixed, randomly generated background spin arrangement. By analysing the quasi-particle eigen-spectra and quantifying the degree to which the corresponding eigen-states localise, we find both an energetic and spatial separation between localised and delocalised states in the charge disorder. In contrast, the spin disorder results in localised states throughout the quasi-particle bands. Finally, we repeat our calculations using strong-coupling perturbation theory, and compare the results obtained from both methods.

</details>


### [109] [Long-range magnetic order with disordered spin orientations in a high-entropy antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10412)
*Yao Shen,Guangkai Zhang,Qinghua Zhang,Xuejuan Gui,Yu Zhang,Heemin Lee,Cheng-Tai Kuo,Jun-Sik Lee,Ronny Sutarto,Feng Ye,Zhao Pan,Xiaomei Qin,Jinchen Wang,Tianping Ying,Youwen Long*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 高熵材料(Mn1/4Fe1/4Co1/4Ni1/4)PS3中，尽管存在显著原子无序，但仍观察到长程zigzag反铁磁有序，所有四种过渡金属元素参与统一相变，但各元素自旋取向不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高熵材料中长程磁有序的微观特征和机制，特别是在原子无序背景下，各元素的具体磁行为如何影响集体磁有序的形成。

Method: 结合中子衍射和共振软X射线散射技术，对高熵蜂窝晶格范德华材料(Mn1/4Fe1/4Co1/4Ni1/4)PS3进行元素特异性磁有序研究。

Result: 在72K以下观察到长程zigzag反铁磁有序，所有四种过渡金属元素参与统一相变，但各元素自旋取向不同，这是单离子各向异性和交换相互作用竞争的结果。

Conclusion: 发现了一种具有无序自旋取向的新型长程磁有序，由高熵磁体中不同的磁性元素协同稳定，为理解复杂磁系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Disorder in magnetic systems typically suppresses long-range order, promoting short-range states such as spin glasses and magnetic clusters. This is particularly prominent in high-entropy materials, characterized by the random distributions of local magnetic entities and exchange interactions. However, in rare exceptions, long-range magnetic order can persist in high-entropy systems, while the microscopic characters and underlying mechanisms remain elusive, especially the magnetic behaviors of individual elements. Here, combining neutron diffraction and resonant soft x-ray scattering, we have conducted an element-specific investigation into the magnetic order of a high-entropy honeycomb-lattice van der Waals material (Mn1/4Fe1/4Co1/4Ni1/4)PS3. Despite significant atomic disorder, long-range zigzag antiferromagnetic order is observed below 72 K, with all four transition-metal elements participating in a unified phase transition. However, the spin orientations of various elements are distinct, attributed to the competition between single-ion anisotropies and exchange interactions. Our findings showcase a novel form of long-range magnetic order with disordered spin orientations, which is synergically stabilized by distinct magnetic elements in a high entropy magnet, offering a new paradigm for understanding complex magnetic systems.

</details>


### [110] [Ab initio quantum embedding description of magic angle twisted bilayer graphene at even-integer fillings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10433)
*Raehyun Kim,Woochang Kim,Kevin D. Stubbs,Steven G. Louie,Lin Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种从头算量子嵌入工作流，用于推导魔角扭曲双层石墨烯的相互作用平带哈密顿量，揭示了电荷中性点和电子掺杂时的绝缘KIVC态，以及空穴掺杂时的脆弱半金属态，并追踪了粒子-空穴不对称性的来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 魔角扭曲双层石墨烯的窄莫尔能带具有meV量级的能量分裂，使其相关相敏感于材料参数和低能下折叠的建模选择。需要建立从头算方法连接微观电子结构、屏蔽相互作用和扫描隧道显微镜特征。

Method: 开发了从头算量子嵌入工作流：从弛豫无应变结构的Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论出发，结合约束随机相位近似屏蔽、受控的双计数减除，以及基于密度矩阵选择列的自动规范固定程序。使用Hartree-Fock和耦合簇单双激发方法求解得到的模型。

Result: 在电荷中性点(ν=0)和电子掺杂(ν=+2)处恢复了稳健的绝缘Kramers谷间相干态。在空穴掺杂侧(ν=-2)观察到脆弱的半金属态，具有弱√3×√3 Kekulé调制和增强的谷间散射峰。尽管基础KS-DFT能带结构近乎粒子-空穴对称，但有效相互作用哈密顿量在ν=±2处表现出明显的粒子-空穴不对称性，这源于由与KS-DFT填充一致的参考密度构建的减除项产生的动量依赖单粒子重整化。

Conclusion: 该工作为连接魔角扭曲双层石墨烯中的微观电子结构、屏蔽相互作用、减除选择和扫描隧道显微镜特征提供了第一性原理路径，揭示了粒子-空穴不对称性的物理起源。

Abstract: Magic angle twisted bilayer graphene (MATBG) hosts narrow moiré bands with meV-scale energy splittings, making its correlated phases sensitive to both material parameters and modeling choices in low-energy downfolding. We develop an ab initio quantum-embedding workflow that derives interacting flat-band Hamiltonians from Kohn-Sham density functional theory (KS-DFT) of a relaxed, unstrained structure. Our model combines constrained random phase approximation (cRPA) screening, controlled double-counting subtraction, and an automated gauge-fixing procedure based on the selected columns of the density matrix (SCDM) that is compatible with symmetry-resolved many-body calculations. Solving the resulting models using Hartree-Fock (HF) and coupled cluster singles and doubles (CCSD), we recover robust insulating Kramers intervalley coherent (KIVC) states at charge neutrality ($ν=0$) and at electron doping ($ν=+2$). The main new physical effect appears on the hole-doped side: at $ν=-2$ we observe a fragile semimetal with a weak $\sqrt{3}\times\sqrt{3}$ Kekulé modulation and enhanced intervalley-scattering peaks in the Fourier-transformed local density of states. Although the underlying KS-DFT band structure is nearly particle-hole symmetric, the effective interacting Hamiltonian exhibits a pronounced particle-hole asymmetry at $ν=\pm 2$ that we trace to momentum-dependent single-particle renormalizations generated by subtraction terms constructed from reference densities consistent with the KS-DFT filling. Our work provides a first-principles route for connecting microscopic electronic structure, screened interactions, subtraction choices, and scanning tunneling microscopy signatures in MATBG.

</details>


### [111] [Observation of Kondo hybridization wave in UTe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10552)
*Xin Yu,Shuikang Yu,Zheyu Wu,Alexander G. Eaton,Andrej Cabala,Michal Vališka,Jun Li,Rui Zhou,Yi-feng Yang,Zhenyu Wang,Peijie Sun,Rui Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 首次在UTe2表面观测到Kondo杂化波的有序相，表现为周期性调制的Fano晶格，伴随电荷密度波和费米能级附近的能隙打开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kondo晶格作为强关联电子系统的原型，通常表现出杂化演化的宽交叉而非相变，此前从未观测到有序的杂化相。研究旨在探索是否存在Kondo杂化的有序相，并理解其在强关联物理中的作用。

Method: 使用扫描隧道显微镜（STM）在自旋三重态重费米子超导体UTe2表面进行观测，识别Kondo杂化波的平移对称性破缺有序相。

Result: 首次观测到Kondo杂化波的有序相，表现为周期性调制的Fano晶格，伴随共格的电荷密度波和费米能级附近的显著能隙打开。该CDW具有重f电子和传导电荷互补占据的有趣实空间纹理，形成Kondo超晶格。

Conclusion: Kondo杂化波与UTe2中的超导性共存，可能为其有争议的自旋三重态配对对称性和潜在机制提供重要见解。首次实验证据为Kondo晶格系统的强关联物理提供了新视角。

Abstract: Condensed matter systems with strong electronic correlations often manifest a variety of intertwined ordered phases of charge, spin, orbital and other degrees of freedom. As a prototypical strongly correlated electronic system, the Kondo lattice provides fertile soil for many fascinating quantum states, including quantum criticality, unconventional superconductivity, hidden order and topological Kondo insulator/semimetal. The foundation of Kondo physics lies in the hybridization between localized moments and itinerant electrons. Generally, the evolution of Kondo hybridization is characterized as a broad crossover rather than a phase transition. Thus far, an ordered hybridization phase has not been observed. Here, we use scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) to identify a translational-symmetry-breaking order of Kondo hybridization wave(KHW) for the first time on the surface of the spin-triplet heavy-fermion superconductor UTe2. The unprecedented phase of KHW manifests as a periodically modulated Fano lattice, accompanied by a commensurate charge density wave (CDW) and a pronounced energy gap opening near the Fermi level. This KHW-imprinted CDW has an intriguing real-space texture of complementary occupation of the heavy f and conduction charges, thereby forming a Kondo superlattice. The KHW is coexistent with superconductivity in UTe2, which may provide valuable insight into its controversial spin-triplet pairing symmetry and the underlying mechanism. Our first experimental evidence for an ordered hybridization state potentially sheds new light on the strong correlation physics of Kondo lattice system.

</details>


### [112] [Formulation of intrinsic nonlinear thermal conductivity for bosonic systems using quantum kinetic equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10605)
*Aoi Kuwabara,Joji Nasu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文建立了玻色子系统中非线性热输运的量子几何理论框架，揭示了量子度量和热贝里联络极化率对非线性热霍尔效应的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性响应在输运现象中具有重要意义，因为它们可以在线性响应被对称性禁止时出现。虽然电输运中的量子几何效应已被广泛研究，但非线性热输运的理论描述仍然具有挑战性，主要困难在于高阶响应中能量磁化的处理。

Method: 采用量子动力学方程方法，避免了Luttinger引力势方法，自然地包含了能量磁化的贡献。该方法识别了非线性热导率的三个不同贡献：两个用量子几何量（量子度量和热贝里联络极化率）表示，第三个仅由能带色散决定。

Result: 在线性自旋波理论框架下应用于特定量子自旋模型，发现在缺乏三重对称性时，热贝里联络极化率项主导非线性热霍尔效应。结果与半经典理论在定量上存在差异，突出了超越半经典图像的量子修正的重要性。

Conclusion: 该研究为玻色子系统中的本征非线性热响应建立了通用理论框架，揭示了超越线性响应理论的量子几何机制，为理解中性准粒子（如磁振子和声子）的热输运提供了新视角。

Abstract: Nonlinear responses in transport phenomena have attracted significant attention because they can arise even when linear responses are forbidden by symmetry, with the quantum geometry of Bloch wave functions playing an essential role. While such effects have been extensively studied in electric transport, similar quantum-geometric mechanisms are also expected to govern nonlinear thermal transport. In particular, thermal responses are crucial in bosonic systems such as magnons and phonons, which are charge-neutral quasiparticles. However, a consistent theoretical description of nonlinear thermal transport remains challenging because of the difficulty in the treatment of energy magnetization in higher-order responses with Luttinger's gravitational potential method. Here, we formulate the intrinsic nonlinear thermal conductivity of bosonic systems using a quantum kinetic equation approach that avoids Luttinger's method and naturally incorporates contributions from energy magnetization. We identify three distinct contributions to the nonlinear thermal conductivity: two expressed in terms of quantum-geometric quantities, namely the quantum metric and the thermal Berry-connection polarizability (TBCP), and a third determined solely by the band dispersions. Applying our formalism to a specific quantum spin model within linear spin-wave theory, we show that the TBCP term dominates the nonlinear thermal Hall effect in the absence of threefold symmetry. Our results differ quantitatively from those obtained using semiclassical theory, thereby highlighting the importance of quantum corrections beyond the semiclassical picture. These findings establish a general framework for intrinsic nonlinear thermal responses in bosonic systems and reveal quantum-geometric mechanisms underlying thermal transport beyond linear response theory.

</details>


### [113] [Theory of Many-Body Multipole Operators in Single-Centered Electron Systems: Two-Body Toroidal Monopoles in Spinless Orbitals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10620)
*Shingo Kuniyoshi,Rikuto Oiwa,Satoru Hayami*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 将单体能级的多极子形式扩展到多体能级，通过球张量表示和Grassmann代数构建包含费米子反对称化的多体算符不可约分解，并应用于自旋无关多体算符的单极子分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单体能级的多极子算符形式已成功建立，但多体能级算符空间（特别是在相互作用系统中）的系统分类仍是一个开放挑战。需要将单体能级的多极子形式扩展到多体能级，以系统分类多体算符并描述复合序参量。

Method: 将费米子产生和湮灭算符表示为球张量，结合Clebsch-Gordan耦合和Grassmann外代数，构建完全包含费米子反对称化的多体算符不可约分解。具体应用中对自旋无关多体算符的单极子进行分类。

Result: 成功将多极子形式从单体能级扩展到多体能级。在自旋无关相互作用多体系统中，发现了电环形单极子（破坏空间反演对称性的赝标量）和磁环形单极子（时间反演奇标量）变得活跃，而这些在自旋无关单体能级混合轨道空间中不存在。

Conclusion: 通过球张量表示和Grassmann代数，成功构建了多体算符的不可约分解框架，为多体算符的系统分类提供了理论基础。该框架揭示了在相互作用多体系统中出现的新奇多极子类型，扩展了多极子物理在多体系统中的应用范围。

Abstract: One-body multipole operators are defined as irreducible representations of rotational symmetry together with spatial-inversion and time-reversal symmetries, providing a systematic framework for classifying electronic internal degrees of freedom and for describing a wide variety of composite order parameters. While this formalism has been successfully established for the one-body operator space, a systematic classification of the many-body operator space, especially in interacting systems, remains an open challenge. In this paper, we extend the multipole formalism in the one-body operator space to the many-body operator space. By formulating fermionic creation and annihilation operators as spherical tensors and employing Clebsch-Gordan coupling combined with the exterior (Grassmann) algebra, we construct an irreducible decomposition of many-body operators that fully incorporates fermionic antisymmetrization. As a concrete application, we classify monopoles appearing in spinless many-body operators. In particular, we show that the electric toroidal monopole, a pseudoscalar breaking spatial-inversion symmetry, and the magnetic toroidal monopole, a time reversal-odd scalar, become active in spinless interacting many-body systems, although they are absent in the spinless one-body hybrid orbital space.

</details>


### [114] [Non-Collinear and Non-Coplanar Magnetic Orders in 1/1 Periodic Approximant to the Icosahedral Quasicrystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10716)
*Shinji Watanabe,Tatsuya Iwasaki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该理论研究揭示了稀土基1/1周期近似晶体中八种非共线非共面磁结构的基态相图，解释了实验观测并阐明了拓扑性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在从理论上阐明稀土基二十面体准晶1/1周期近似晶体的基态性质，特别是考虑晶体电场产生的单轴各向异性后的磁性行为，以解释实验观测的磁结构并理解其拓扑特性。

Method: 基于考虑晶体电场单轴各向异性的有效磁性模型，对晶格常数a=14.725 Å的1/1近似晶体进行数值精确计算，确定铁磁相互作用的基态相图，识别磁空间群并分析每个基态的简并度。

Result: 确定了八种非共线非共面磁结构，磁空间群分别为I_Pm'3'、C2'/m'和R3，阐明了每个基态的简并度（反映在畴的数量上），揭示了磁性和拓扑性质，结果能解释1/1近似晶体中测量的磁结构。

Conclusion: 该有效模型对于理解广泛稀土基1/1近似晶体的磁结构及其拓扑性质具有实用性，研究结果为解释实验观测提供了理论框架，并揭示了这些系统中的复杂磁序和拓扑特性。

Abstract: Ground-state properties of rare-earth based 1/1 periodic approximants of icosahedral quasicrystal are clarified theoretically on the basis of an effective model for magnetism taking into account uniaxial anisotropy arising from crystalline electric field. By performing numerically-exact calculation on the 1/1 approximant crystal with a lattice constant $a$=14.725 Å, we have determined the ground-state phase diagram for ferromagnetic interactions. The result shows that eight kinds of noncollinear and noncoplanar magnetic structures are stabilized, whose magnetic space groups are identified as $I_{\rm P}m'\bar{3}'$, $C2'/m'$ and $R\bar{3}$. We have clarified the degeneracy of each ground state, which is expected to be reflected in the numbers of the domains. By analyzing each state, the magnetic as well as topological properties are revealed. Our results are shown to explain the measured magnetic structures in the 1/1 approximants and the effective model is discussed to be useful for understanding the magnetic structures and their topological properties in broad range of rare-earth based 1/1 approximants.

</details>


### [115] [Pairing and charge distribution in Emery ladders preserving the ratio of Cu to O atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10755)
*Gökmen Polat,Eric Jeckelmann*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究三种不同梯子状晶格上的Emery模型（三带Hubbard模型），这些晶格是CuO₂平面的超晶胞，保持铜氧原子比例不变。使用密度矩阵重整化群方法确认这些Emery梯子在未掺杂时是电荷转移绝缘体，掺杂后转变为具有增强配对关联的Luther-Emery液体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超导铜酸盐中电荷分布、配对强度和相互作用之间的关系。通过保持铜氧原子比例不变的梯子状晶格，可以研究Luther-Emery相中电荷在铜氧原子间的分布情况，并与二维簇和实验观测结果进行比较。

Method: 使用密度矩阵重整化群方法研究三种不同梯子状晶格上的Emery模型（三带Hubbard模型）。这些晶格是CuO₂平面的超晶胞，保持铜氧原子比例不变。研究未掺杂和掺杂情况下的电荷分布、配对关联和相互作用。

Result: 1. Emery梯子在未掺杂时是电荷转移绝缘体；2. 掺杂后转变为具有增强配对关联的Luther-Emery液体；3. 保持铜氧原子比例允许研究Luther-Emery相中电荷在铜氧原子间的分布；4. 这些Emery梯子可以描述在二维簇和实验中观察到的电荷分布、配对强度和相互作用之间的关系。

Conclusion: Emery梯子模型能够有效描述超导铜酸盐中电荷分布、配对强度和相互作用之间的关系。保持铜氧原子比例的梯子状晶格为研究Luther-Emery相中的电荷分布提供了有效平台，并与二维簇和实验观测结果一致。

Abstract: We study the Emery model (three-band Hubbard model) for superconducting cuprates on three distinct ladder-like lattices that are supercell of the CuO$_2$ plane and thus preserve the ratio of copper to oxygen atoms. Using the density-matrix renormalization group method we confirm that these Emery ladders are charge-transfer insulators for the hole concentration corresponding to undoped cuprates but become Luther-Emery liquids with enhanced pairing correlations upon doping. The preservation of the Cu to O ratio allows us to study the distribution of charges between these atoms in the Luther-Emery phase. We show that these Emery ladders can describe the relations between charge distribution, pairing strength, and interactions that have been observed in the Emery model on two-dimensional clusters and in experiments.

</details>


### [116] [Electronic Structure and Resonant Circular Dichroism of La$_{0.7}$Sr$_{0.3}$MnO$_3$ from Soft X-ray Angle-Resolved Photoemission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10794)
*Øyvind Finnseth,Damian Brzozowski,Anders Christian Mathisen,Stefanie Suzanne Brinkman,Xin Liang Tan,Fabian Gohler,Benjamin A. D. Williamson,Kristoffer Eggestad,Meng-Jie Huang,Jens Buck,Moritz Hoesch,Kai Rossnagel,Sverre M. Selbach,Hendrik Bentmann,Ingrid Hallsteinsen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过软X射线角分辨光电子能谱研究(111)取向La0.7Sr0.3MnO3薄膜的电子能带结构和磁圆二色性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡金属氧化物中自旋、轨道、电荷和晶格自由度的耦合产生多种电子和磁现象，这些现象对未来技术具有重要意义。本研究旨在探索La0.7Sr0.3MnO3薄膜的电子能带结构及其磁性特征。

Method: 采用软X射线角分辨光电子能谱(ARPES)研究(111)取向的La0.7Sr0.3MnO3薄膜。测量结果与使用Hubbard U修正的密度泛函理论计算的电子能带结构进行比较。同时探测ARPES中的圆二色性，观察Mn L边共振光电子发射中的动量分辨磁圆二色性。

Result: 测量结果与使用Hubbard U修正的密度泛函理论计算的电子能带结构一致。在Mn L边共振光电子发射中观察到显著的动量分辨磁圆二色性。

Conclusion: 该方法结合了ARPES的动量选择性和X射线磁圆二色性的自旋选择性，为研究非常规磁性提供了一种有用的方法。

Abstract: Coupling between spin, orbital, charge, and lattice degrees of freedom in transition-metal oxides produces a variety of electronic and magnetic phenomena of importance for future technologies. Here, we explore the electronic band structure of a (111)-oriented La0.7Sr0.3MnO3 thin film through soft X-ray angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES). The measurements agree with the electronic band structure calculated with density functional theory using Hubbard U correction. Furthermore, we probe the circular dichroism in ARPES, and observe a pronounced momentum- resolved magnetic circular dichroism in resonant photoemission from the Mn L-edge. The approach combines the momentum- and spin-selectivity of ARPES and X-ray magnetic circular dichroism, respectively, which could provide a useful approach for the study of unconventional magnetism.

</details>


### [117] [Generalized Reduced-Density-Matrix Quantum Monte Carlo Gives Access to More](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10948)
*Zhiyan Wang,Zhe Wang,Bin-Bin Mao,Zheng Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出用量子蒙特卡洛模拟广义约化密度矩阵而非配分函数的新范式，解决非对角可观测量测量瓶颈，扩展约化描述优势到动力学可观测量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统QMC模拟配分函数时，大量非对角可观测量无法直接测量，存在测量瓶颈。需要突破这一限制，扩展QMC的信息提取能力

Method: 将模拟对象从配分函数替换为广义约化密度矩阵(GRDM)，从根本上消除测量瓶颈，将约化描述维度缩减优势扩展到动力学可观测量

Result: 该框架使定向环算法能够测量等时和虚时非对角可观测量，后者可直接获取动力学谱；还能测量诊断混合态强-弱对称性破缺的Rényi-1关联函数

Conclusion: 建立QMC中全息表征的统一框架，通过模拟GRDM而非配分函数，显著扩展QMC可测量范围，为量子多体系统提供更丰富的信息提取能力

Abstract: In quantum Monte Carlo (QMC), what can be measured efficiently is largely determined by what is sampled. When the sampled object is the partition function, a broad class of observables, including general off-diagonal operators, is typically unavailable as direct estimators. In this article, we introduce a paradigm shift by replacing the partition function with a generalized reduced density matrix (GRDM) as the simulated object. This reformulation removes the measurement bottleneck at its source and extends the dimensional-reduction advantage of reduced descriptions from static quantities to dynamical observables, thereby enabling much richer information extraction. As substantial demonstrations, the framework allows the directed-loop algorithm to measure both equal-time and imaginary-time off-diagonal observables, with the latter giving direct access to dynamical spectra. It also enables measurements of Rényi-1 correlators that diagnose strong-to-weak symmetry breaking in mixed states. This work establishes a unified framework for holographic characterization within QMC.

</details>


### [118] [Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya-driven instabilities in square-kagome quantum antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10998)
*Leonid S. Taran,Arnaud Ralko,Fedor V. Temnikov,Vladimir V. Mazurenko,Sergey V. Streltsov,Yasir Iqbal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过结合第一性原理计算和广义Schwinger-boson自洽平均场理论，揭示了装饰型方晶格Kagome量子反铁磁体Na₆Cu₇BiO₄(PO₄)₄Cl₃中，装饰位点耦合J₁₀稳定了能隙量子顺磁态，而Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用则抑制自旋子能隙并驱动系统向磁凝聚相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究装饰型方晶格Kagome量子反铁磁体中强阻挫、晶格装饰和自旋轨道诱导各向异性之间的竞争，特别是在Na₆Cu₇BiO₄(PO₄)₄Cl₃材料中，理解这些相互作用如何影响量子顺磁态和磁凝聚相变。

Method: 结合第一性原理计算Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya矢量与广义Schwinger-boson自洽平均场理论，该理论平等处理单重态和三重态的跳跃/配对通道。首先以各向同性方晶格Kagome海森堡模型为基准，然后引入最小DM扰动，最后应用于包含装饰位点的实际哈密顿量。

Result: 各向同性模型显示四个竞争的低能鞍点，具有不同的Wilson环通量和特征性静态/动态结构因子指纹。最小DM扰动不改变竞争格局但增强有序趋势。对于实际装饰哈密顿量，J₁₀是能隙态的控制参数，而完整的对称允许DM模式进一步推动系统向凝聚相变。Na₆Cu₇BiO₄(PO₄)₄Cl₃位于磁不稳定性附近。

Conclusion: 装饰位点耦合J₁₀稳定了量子顺磁能隙态，而Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用通过抑制自旋子能隙驱动系统向磁凝聚相变。该研究为装饰型方晶格Kagome材料中各向异性增强的软模提供了实验可验证的预测，并揭示了材料接近磁不稳定的特性。

Abstract: Decorated square-kagome quantum antiferromagnets provide a natural setting in which strong frustration, lattice decoration, and spin-orbit-induced anisotropy compete on comparable energy scales. Here we show that in Na$_6$Cu$_7$BiO$_4$(PO$_4$)$_4$Cl$_3$ the coupling ($J_{10}$) which links the decorating Cu(3) sites to the square-kagome backbone, stabilizes the gapped quantum-paramagnetic regime, while symmetry-allowed Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) interactions systematically suppress the minimum spinon gap $Δ_{\mathrm{spinon}}$ and drive the system toward magnetic condensation. To establish this, we combine ab initio calculation of the DM vectors with a generalized Schwinger-boson self-consistent mean-field theory that treats singlet and triplet hopping/pairing channels on equal footing. As a benchmark, the isotropic square-kagome Heisenberg model exhibits four competing low-energy saddle points distinguished by their Wilson-loop fluxes and by characteristic static and dynamical structure-factor fingerprints. A minimal DM perturbation does not qualitatively reshape this competing landscape, but already enhances the tendency towards order. For the realistic decorated Hamiltonian, finite-size scaling of $Δ_{\mathrm{spinon}}$ together with momentum-resolved structure factors identifies $J_{10}$ (exchange with decorating Cu) as the control parameter of the gapped regime and shows that the full symmetry-allowed DM pattern shifts the system further toward condensation. Our results place Na$_6$Cu$_7$BiO$_4$(PO$_4$)$_4$Cl$_3$ in close proximity to a magnetic instability and provide experimentally testable predictions for anisotropy-enhanced soft modes in decorated square-kagome materials.

</details>


### [119] [Two-Body Solution and Instabilities along Streda Lines in Moire Flat Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11007)
*Guopeng Xu,Chunli Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究扭曲双层半导体莫尔平带在磁场下的拓扑性质，分析了沿Streda线的多体基态相变、自旋翻转激发不稳定性，并发展了不等磁场下两体问题的精确解法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭曲双层半导体莫尔平带可近似为具有相反陈数的朗道能级对，这为在时间反演对称哈密顿量中寻找新拓扑态提供了最小模型。研究外部磁场对该模型的影响，特别是沿Streda线的多体基态性质，有助于理解莫尔平带中的拓扑相变和激发特性。

Method: 1. 使用Hartree-Fock近似研究密度-磁场平面沿Streda线的多体基态；2. 用时变平均场理论分析自旋翻转激发；3. 提出"电荷中心基"精确求解不等磁场下的两体问题，将各向同性相互作用参数化为相对角动量量子数，扩展了Haldane赝势到不等磁场情况。

Result: 1. 远离电荷中性点时，陈绝缘体（不可压缩）态非常稳定；2. 接近电荷中性点时，随着相互作用强度κ减小，出现从不可压缩相到可压缩相的转变；3. 沿Streda线向电荷中性点方向，即使在大κ下，当磁场足够大时不可压缩态也会因自旋翻转激发而变得不稳定；4. 不等磁场下的赝势随磁场差变化显示一系列能级交叉，呈现普通朗道能级系统中不存在的非单调结构。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解莫尔平带在磁场下的拓扑性质提供了系统框架，发展的不等磁场两体问题精确解法为研究弱场物理提供了有用起点，特别是当磁布洛赫态基由于基矢过大而计算不可行时。

Abstract: Moire minibands in twisted homobilayer semiconductors can, under suitable approximations, be modeled as a pair of Landau levels with opposite Chern numbers. This provides a minimal model for searching novel topological states in a time-reversal-symmetric Hamiltonian. In this work, we investigate the effects of an external magnetic field in this model. We study the many-body ground state in the density-magnetic-field (n-B) plane along the dn/dB = \pm1/Phi0 Streda line with Hartree-Fock approximation. Away from charge neutrality, we find the Chern-insulating (incompressible) state is very robust while towards charge neutrality, we find a transition from incompressible phase to compressible phase as the interaction strength kappa decreases. Using time-dependent mean-field theory, we further analyze spin-flip excitations and find that the incompressible state along Streda line toward charge neutrality becomes unstable even at large kappa when magnetic field is sufficiently large. Finally, we solve the two-body problem in a given Landau level exactly where the two particles experience unequal magnetic fields using a new basis called center-of-charge basis. This basis allows any isotropic interaction to be parameterized by a single quantum number, the relative angular momentum, thereby extending the Haldane pseudopotentials to the unequal-magnetic-fields case. As the difference of the two magnetic fields varies, these pseudopotentials show a sequence of level crossings, leading to non-monotonic structure of pseudopotentials that is absent in ordinary Landau level systems. Our formulation provides a useful starting point for studying weak-field physics in moire flat bands, where magnetic Bloch-state basis becomes computationally impossible due to the large basis sizes.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.PE'></div>

# q-bio.PE [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [SDSR: A Spectral Divide-and-Conquer Approach for Species Tree Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10215)
*Ortal Reshef,Ofer Glassman,Or Zuk,Yariv Aizenbud,Boaz Nadler,Ariel Jaffe*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: SDSR是一种基于谱图理论的可扩展分治方法，用于从多基因标记数据重建物种树，通过递归分区和子树合并显著提高计算效率，同时保持重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决系统发育学中物种树重建的两个关键挑战：1) 单个基因进化历史与物种进化历史之间的不一致性（基因树-物种树不一致）；2) 当代研究涉及数千个物种时的计算复杂性。

Method: 提出SDSR（基于谱图理论的可扩展分治方法）：1) 递归地将物种分区为子集，直到子集大小低于给定阈值；2) 使用用户选择的物种树重建算法为每个子集构建子树；3) 将这些子树合并形成完整树。该方法在多重物种溯祖模型下具有理论恢复保证，并进行了运行时复杂度分析。

Result: 理论分析表明，SDSR与物种树重建算法结合使用时，相比在全数据上应用相同算法可显著节省运行时间。实证评估显示，在包含不完全谱系分选和水平基因转移的合成基准数据集上，SDSR与CA-ML或ASTRAL等方法结合可获得高达10倍的加速，同时保持与在全数据上应用这些方法相当的树重建精度。

Conclusion: SDSR是一种高效且可扩展的物种树重建方法，通过分治策略有效解决了大规模系统发育分析中的计算挑战，在保持重建精度的同时显著提高了计算效率，适用于包含数千个物种的当代系统发育研究。

Abstract: Recovering a tree that represents the evolutionary history of a group of species is a key task in phylogenetics. Performing this task using sequence data from multiple genetic markers poses two key challenges. The first is the discordance between the evolutionary history of individual genes and that of the species. The second challenge is computational, as contemporary studies involve thousands of species. Here we present SDSR, a scalable divide-and-conquer approach for species tree reconstruction based on spectral graph theory. The algorithm recursively partitions the species into subsets until their sizes are below a given threshold. The trees of these subsets are reconstructed by a user-chosen species tree algorithm. Finally, these subtrees are merged to form the full tree. On the theoretical front, we derive recovery guarantees for SDSR, under the multispecies coalescent (MSC) model. We also perform a runtime complexity analysis. We show that SDSR, when combined with a species tree reconstruction algorithm as a subroutine, yields substantial runtime savings as compared to applying the same algorithm on the full data. Empirically, we evaluate SDSR on synthetic benchmark datasets with incomplete lineage sorting and horizontal gene transfer. In accordance with our theoretical analysis, the simulations show that combining SDSR with common species tree methods, such as CA-ML or ASTRAL, yields up to 10-fold faster runtimes. In addition, SDSR achieves a comparable tree reconstruction accuracy to that obtained by applying these methods on the full data.

</details>


### [121] [Macroscopic Dominance from Microscopic Extremes: Symmetry Breaking in Spatial Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10911)
*Stuti Guha,Shawn D. Ryan,Bhargav R. Karamched*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该研究探讨竞争种群如何将空间优势转化为宏观主导地位，提出了一个资源竞争的随机模型，将瞬时发现阶段与垄断阶段解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究竞争种群如何将空间优势转化为宏观主导地位，理解对称性破缺、极端值统计和非互易相互作用在资源竞争中的关键作用。

Method: 引入一个资源竞争的随机模型，将竞争过程解耦为瞬时发现阶段和垄断阶段。模型分析初始对称性破缺的极端值统计特性，并研究非互易相互作用偏置对系统稳定性的影响。

Result: 线性空间劣势需要指数级更大的种群才能克服；瞬时优势无法稳定主导地位；非互易相互作用偏置是驱动系统进入稳健吸收状态的必要条件。

Conclusion: 空间优势转化为宏观主导需要两个阶段：基于极端值统计的初始对称性破缺，以及通过非互易相互作用稳定主导地位。仅靠瞬时优势不足以保证长期垄断。

Abstract: How do competing populations convert a spatial advantage into macroscopic dominance? We introduce a stochastic model for resource competition that decouples the transient discovery phase from monopolization. Initial symmetry breaking is governed by extreme value statistics of first-passage times: a linear spatial disadvantage requires an exponentially larger population to overcome. However, transient superiority cannot stabilize dominance. A non-reciprocal interaction bias is strictly necessary to arrest local fluctuations and drive the system into a robust absorbing state.

</details>
